            else:
                incident_id = incident["incident_id"]
                current_state = incident["state"]
                current_severity = incident["severity"]

            event_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            occurred_at = event.occurred_at.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="microseconds") if event.occurred_at else None
            db.execute(
                """INSERT INTO proactive_events
                   (event_id, incident_id, source_id, source_event_id, payload_hash, event_type, severity, title,
                    summary, occurred_at, received_at, correlation_key, confidence, evidence_refs_json, attributes_json)
                   VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                (
                    event_id, incident_id, source_id, event.source_event_id, payload_hash, event.event_type.value,
                    event.severity.value, event.title, event.summary, occurred_at, now,
                    event.correlation_key, event.confidence, _json(event.evidence_refs), _json(event.attributes),
                ),
            )

            rank = max(_SEVERITY_RANK[current_severity], _SEVERITY_RANK[event.severity.value])
            merged_severity = next(name for name, score in _SEVERITY_RANK.items() if score == rank)
            if incident is None:
                db.execute(
                    "UPDATE proactive_incidents SET first_event_id = ?, last_event_id = ? WHERE incident_id = ?",
                    (event_id, event_id, incident_id),
                )
            else:
                db.execute(
                    """UPDATE proactive_incidents
                       SET severity = ?, updated_at = ?, last_event_id = ?
                       WHERE incident_id = ?""",
                    (merged_severity, now, event_id, incident_id),
                )

            self._audit(
                db, incident_id, "source", source_id, "event_ingested",
                {"event_id": event_id, "source_event_id": event.source_event_id, "event_type": event.event_type.value}, now,
            )
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return {"event_id": event_id, "incident_id": incident_id, "incident_state": current_state}, False
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    @staticmethod
    def _audit(
        db: sqlite3.Connection,
        incident_id: str,
        actor_type: str,
        actor_id: str,
        action: str,
        metadata: dict[str, Any],
        occurred_at: str,
    ) -> None:
        db.execute(
            """INSERT INTO proactive_audit
               (audit_id, incident_id, actor_type, actor_id, action, metadata_json, occurred_at)
               VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
            (str(uuid.uuid4()), incident_id, actor_type, actor_id, action, _json(metadata), occurred_at),
        )

    def transition(self, incident_id: str, target: IncidentState, actor_id: str, reason: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_incidents WHERE incident_id = ?", (incident_id,)).fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise IncidentNotFound(incident_id)
            current = IncidentState(row["state"])
            now = utc_now()
            if target == IncidentState.ACKNOWLEDGED:
                if current == IncidentState.ACKNOWLEDGED:
                    db.execute("COMMIT")
                    return {"incident_id": incident_id, "state": current.value}
                if current != IncidentState.OPEN:
                    raise IncidentConflict("Only an open incident can be acknowledged")
                db.execute(
                    "UPDATE proactive_incidents SET state = 'acknowledged', acknowledged_at = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE incident_id = ?",
                    (now, now, incident_id),
                )
                action = "incident_acknowledged"
                metadata: dict[str, Any] = {}
            elif target == IncidentState.RESOLVED:
                if current == IncidentState.RESOLVED:
                    if row["resolution_reason"] == reason:
                        db.execute("COMMIT")
                        return {"incident_id": incident_id, "state": current.value}
                    raise IncidentConflict("The incident is already resolved with a different recorded reason")
                db.execute(
                    """UPDATE proactive_incidents
                       SET state = 'resolved', resolved_at = ?, updated_at = ?, resolution_reason = ?
                       WHERE incident_id = ?""",
                    (now, now, reason, incident_id),
                )
                action = "incident_resolved"
                metadata = {"reason": reason}
            else:
                raise IncidentConflict("Unsupported state transition")

            self._audit(db, incident_id, "user", actor_id, action, metadata, now)
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return {"incident_id": incident_id, "state": target.value}
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def list_incidents(self, state: IncidentState | None, limit: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            if state is None:
                rows = db.execute(
                    "SELECT incident_id FROM proactive_incidents ORDER BY updated_at DESC, incident_id DESC LIMIT ?",
                    (limit,),
                ).fetchall()
            else:
                rows = db.execute(
                    "SELECT incident_id FROM proactive_incidents WHERE state = ? ORDER BY updated_at DESC, incident_id DESC LIMIT ?",
                    (state.value, limit),
                ).fetchall()
            ids = [row["incident_id"] for row in rows]
        return [self.get_incident(incident_id) for incident_id in ids]

    def get_incident(self, incident_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            incident_row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_incidents WHERE incident_id = ?", (incident_id,)).fetchone()
            if incident_row is None:
                raise IncidentNotFound(incident_id)
            event_rows = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM proactive_events WHERE incident_id = ? ORDER BY received_at ASC, event_id ASC",
                (incident_id,),
            ).fetchall()
            audit_rows = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM proactive_audit WHERE incident_id = ? ORDER BY occurred_at ASC, audit_id ASC",
                (incident_id,),
            ).fetchall()

        incident = dict(incident_row)
        incident["events"] = [
            {
                "event_id": row["event_id"], "source_id": row["source_id"],
                "source_event_id": row["source_event_id"], "event_type": row["event_type"],
                "severity": row["severity"], "title": row["title"], "summary": row["summary"],
                "occurred_at": row["occurred_at"], "received_at": row["received_at"],
                "correlation_key": row["correlation_key"], "confidence": row["confidence"],
                "evidence_refs": _parse_json(row["evidence_refs_json"]),
                "attributes": _parse_json(row["attributes_json"]),
            }
            for row in event_rows
        ]
        incident["audit"] = [
            {
                "audit_id": row["audit_id"], "actor_type": row["actor_type"],
                "actor_id": row["actor_id"], "action": row["action"],
                "metadata": _parse_json(row["metadata_json"]), "occurred_at": row["occurred_at"],
            }
            for row in audit_rows
        ]
        return incident


# Aariya Proactive Event Foundation — integration overlay

This is an additive backend slice for the existing Aariya FastAPI application. It contains no demo events, fake provider, canned notification, default credentials, fabricated confidence, or synthetic runtime observations.

## Included real behavior

- Authenticated event ingestion. `source_id` is derived from a configured source token, never trusted from the request body.
- Durable persistence to an explicitly configured SQLite database file.
- Per-source event-type allow-lists, so a task monitor cannot claim a security event unless that exact capability was explicitly granted.
- Event idempotency using `(authenticated source ID, source_event_id)`.
- Incident creation and same-source correlation only when the real source supplies a `correlation_key`.
- Event history and evidence references retained with the incident.
- Authenticated list/detail endpoints plus audited acknowledge/resolve transitions.
- Strict input validation, bounded payload size, unique source tokens, UTC normalization for actual source timestamps, and failure-closed configuration.

## Not yet included

This slice does not claim to deliver push messages or VoIP calls. It does not contain the existing Flutter source, authenticated mobile-session code, the current Aariya user identity system, the real event producers/watchers, the existing database abstraction, or provider credentials. Those must be connected to actual project code before dispatch, Guardian, or mobile call work can be safely integrated. It also does not execute corrective actions or estimate importance from an LLM.

## Add to the actual project

Copy `server/proactive/` into the existing repository's `server/proactive/` directory. Preserve the repository's current files.

In the existing `server/main.py`, after the FastAPI `app` is created and before the module finishes loading, include this router:

```python
from server.proactive.router import router as proactive_router
app.include_router(proactive_router)
```

Do not create a second FastAPI app. Apply the import/include only after checking the existing app factory/lifespan and import conventions. The source tree was not available when this overlay was prepared, so `server/main.py` is deliberately not overwritten by a guessed version.

## Required configuration

Configure these variables using the project's real secret manager or local protected environment file. Do not commit their values.

- `AARIYA_PROACTIVE_DB_PATH`: absolute path for the persistent event database file.
- `AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_TOKEN`: a random token of at least 32 characters for the single configured user identity used by this initial slice.
- `AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_ID`: the real user/account identifier to be written in the audit trail.
- `AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON`: JSON object mapping each actual source ID to an object with exactly two properties: `token` (a unique random secret of at least 32 characters) and `event_types` (a non-empty explicit allow-list using the supported event-type names). Only configure sources that really exist and are controlled by Aariya. A source's event-type permission is denied by default unless configured here.

Generate secrets with the system cryptographic random generator, e.g. `python -c "import secrets; print(secrets.token_urlsafe(48))"`. Generate a separate token for the user and for each source. Do not reuse authentication tokens from other services.

The application returns `503` for this feature while required configuration is missing; it does not fall back to open access or sample credentials.

## Endpoints

All paths below are relative to `/api/proactive/v1`.

- `POST /events` — source-token authenticated. `source_event_id`, `event_type`, `severity`, `title`, and `summary` must come from a real, authorized source, and the source must be explicitly allow-listed for the submitted event type. Supported event types are `system_failure`, `task_failure`, `critical_event`, `deadline`, `security_event`, `autonomous_discovery`, `recovery`, and `task_completed`. `occurred_at`, `confidence`, `correlation_key`, `evidence_refs`, and `attributes` are optional source-supplied facts; the service does not invent them.
- `GET /incidents?state=open&limit=50` — user-token authenticated; `state` is optional.
- `GET /incidents/{incident_id}` — includes persisted events and audit history.
- `POST /incidents/{incident_id}/acknowledge` — audited transition from open to acknowledged.
- `POST /incidents/{incident_id}/resolve` — requires a non-empty, user-supplied resolution reason; audited.

Send `Authorization: Bearer <configured token>`. Do not include the bearer token in a URL, event body, evidence reference, or log. Do not place passwords, API keys, session cookies, private keys, or full conversation transcripts in `summary` or `attributes`; send minimum diagnostic facts and opaque references to evidence stored under the existing access-control system.

## Storage and deployment boundary

SQLite is appropriate here only as a first durable local/single-host slice. Its file must live in a protected application-data location with filesystem permissions and encrypted disk/storage appropriate to the device. This implementation intentionally does not claim multi-instance consistency, cloud replication, mobile push, end-to-end encryption, or Guardian independence. Before multiple backend instances are deployed, move the schema and transaction logic to Aariya's real shared database/migration system instead of sharing a SQLite file across hosts.

## Next implementation step

Once the actual backend and Flutter source are available, wire existing system/task monitors into `POST /events`, replace the initial single-user token guard with Aariya's current session/authorization dependency, connect an outbox to configured real push/VoIP providers, and add the Flutter incident thread and evidence routes. Do not enable escalation until those provider delivery acknowledgements and policy checks are implemented.

# Aariya Proactive Event Foundation

Additive FastAPI backend package for real event ingestion and incident tracking. See [`INTEGRATION.md`](INTEGRATION.md) for installation, required real configuration, endpoints, and limitations.

No event data, user identities, credentials, notification provider, or provider responses are fabricated or shipped with this package.



pytest>=8.0



# Implemented scope and integration status

## Code in this overlay

- `server/proactive/config.py` — environment-only secrets, database path, source identities, and explicit per-source event-type capabilities.
- `server/proactive/schemas.py` — strict source event and incident API contracts.
- `server/proactive/store.py` — SQLite schema initialization, durable event/incident/audit storage, idempotency/content-integrity checks, deterministic same-source correlation, severity escalation, and state transitions.
- `server/proactive/router.py` — authenticated FastAPI endpoints.
- `tests/` — automated tests against real FastAPI routing and temporary SQLite databases; no mocked delivery providers are used.

## Not merged into the Aariya runtime yet

The existing `server/main.py`, backend auth/session service, current storage/migration layer, true event producer modules, and Flutter source were not present in the available project files. Therefore the router has not been registered in the existing running app, and no current source watcher has been modified. The overlay includes explicit integration instructions rather than overwriting the actual app with a guessed entrypoint.

## Required next slices before calling the whole feature complete

1. Register this router in the real FastAPI app and replace the initial user bearer-token guard with the project's existing authenticated account/device identity.
2. Connect the real task/system/security monitors to the event endpoint and map only fields those sources can actually prove.
3. Add a durable delivery outbox and connect it to explicitly configured push providers with real delivery receipts and retry state.
4. Integrate a real WebRTC/VoIP signalling and mobile call UI into the existing Flutter code, including iOS/Android native lifecycle behavior.
5. Add Guardian monitoring with a separate failure domain and dispatch path.
6. Add the existing action/authorization pipeline, approvals, execution evidence, and verification before enabling voice-driven actions.
7. Replace single-host SQLite with the existing shared database and its migration framework before multi-instance/server deployment.
8. Add end-to-end encryption and key management after the actual gateway, mobile session, and storage boundaries are identified and threat-modelled.

These items are explicitly not simulated by this overlay.


"""Persistence tests against real isolated SQLite files; no services are mocked."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from server.proactive.schemas import EventIn, EventType, IncidentState, Severity
from server.proactive.store import DuplicateEventConflict, EventStore, IncidentConflict


def _event(
    source_event_id: str,
    *,
    severity: Severity = Severity.WARNING,
    correlation_key: str | None = None,
) -> EventIn:
    return EventIn(
        source_event_id=source_event_id,
        event_type=EventType.TASK_FAILURE,
        severity=severity,
        title="Task execution failed",
        summary="The authorized task source reported a failed execution.",
        occurred_at=datetime(2026, 10, 24, 9, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        correlation_key=correlation_key,
        evidence_refs=["evidence-record:observed-task-log"],
        attributes={"exit_code": 1},
    )


def test_ingest_is_durable_and_idempotent(tmp_path):
    path = (tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve()
    first_store = EventStore(path)
    first, duplicate = first_store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001"))
    assert duplicate is False

    restarted_store = EventStore(path)
    repeated, duplicate = restarted_store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001"))
    assert duplicate is True
    assert repeated["event_id"] == first["event_id"]
    assert repeated["incident_id"] == first["incident_id"]

    incident = restarted_store.get_incident(first["incident_id"])
    assert incident["state"] == IncidentState.OPEN.value
    assert len(incident["events"]) == 1
    assert incident["events"][0]["evidence_refs"] == ["evidence-record:observed-task-log"]
    assert incident["audit"][0]["action"] == "event_ingested"


def test_correlation_uses_only_source_supplied_key_and_escalates_severity(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    low, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001", severity=Severity.WARNING, correlation_key="task-run-12"))
    high, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-002", severity=Severity.CRITICAL, correlation_key="task-run-12"))

    assert low["incident_id"] == high["incident_id"]
    incident = store.get_incident(low["incident_id"])
    assert incident["severity"] == Severity.CRITICAL.value
    assert len(incident["events"]) == 2


def test_different_sources_are_not_implicitly_correlated(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    first, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001", correlation_key="same-looking-key"))
    second, _ = store.ingest("system-monitor", _event("event-002", correlation_key="same-looking-key"))
    assert first["incident_id"] != second["incident_id"]


def test_acknowledge_and_resolve_are_audited_and_resolution_is_idempotent(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    result, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001"))
    incident_id = result["incident_id"]

    store.transition(incident_id, IncidentState.ACKNOWLEDGED, "account-test")
    store.transition(incident_id, IncidentState.RESOLVED, "account-test", "The task owner verified recovery.")
    store.transition(incident_id, IncidentState.RESOLVED, "account-test", "The task owner verified recovery.")

    incident = store.get_incident(incident_id)
    assert incident["state"] == IncidentState.RESOLVED.value
    assert [a["action"] for a in incident["audit"]] == [
        "event_ingested", "incident_acknowledged", "incident_resolved"
    ]


def test_resolved_incident_is_not_silently_reopened(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    first, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001", correlation_key="task-run-12"))
    store.transition(first["incident_id"], IncidentState.RESOLVED, "account-test", "Verified fixed.")
    next_event, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-002", correlation_key="task-run-12"))
    assert next_event["incident_id"] != first["incident_id"]


def test_invalid_state_transition_is_rejected(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    result, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001"))
    store.transition(result["incident_id"], IncidentState.RESOLVED, "account-test", "Verified fixed.")
    try:
        store.transition(result["incident_id"], IncidentState.ACKNOWLEDGED, "account-test")
    except IncidentConflict:
        pass
    else:
        raise AssertionError("Resolved incidents must not be acknowledged again")


def test_reusing_event_id_with_changed_content_is_rejected(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001", severity=Severity.WARNING))
    try:
        store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001", severity=Severity.CRITICAL))
    except DuplicateEventConflict:
        pass
    else:
        raise AssertionError("Reusing a source event ID with changed content must be rejected")


"""HTTP boundary tests with a real FastAPI app and isolated SQLite storage."""

import json
import secrets

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from server.proactive.router import _store_for_path, router


def test_authenticated_ingestion_and_user_incident_lifecycle(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    user_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(48)
    source_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(48)
    database_path = (tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve()
    monkeypatch.setenv("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_DB_PATH", str(database_path))
    monkeypatch.setenv("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_TOKEN", user_token)
    monkeypatch.setenv("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_ID", "verified-test-user")
    monkeypatch.setenv("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON", json.dumps({"task-monitor": {"token": source_token, "event_types": ["task_failure"]}}))
    _store_for_path.cache_clear()

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(router)
    client = TestClient(app)
    body = {
        "source_event_id": "task-run-event-01",
        "event_type": "task_failure",
        "severity": "high",
        "title": "Observed task failure",
        "summary": "The task monitor reported a failure from its real execution path.",
        "occurred_at": "2026-10-24T09:00:00+00:00",
        "evidence_refs": ["evidence-record:task-run-event-01"],
    }

    denied = client.post("/api/proactive/v1/events", json=body)
    assert denied.status_code == 401

    forbidden_body = {**body, "event_type": "security_event"}
    forbidden = client.post(
        "/api/proactive/v1/events", json=forbidden_body,
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {source_token}"},
    )
    assert forbidden.status_code == 403

    response = client.post(
        "/api/proactive/v1/events", json=body,
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {source_token}"},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 202, response.text
    created = response.json()
    assert created["duplicate"] is False

    repeated = client.post(
        "/api/proactive/v1/events", json=body,
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {source_token}"},
    )
    assert repeated.status_code == 202
    assert repeated.json()["duplicate"] is True
    assert repeated.json()["event_id"] == created["event_id"]
    tampered_duplicate = {**body, "severity": "critical"}
    tampered = client.post(
        "/api/proactive/v1/events", json=tampered_duplicate,
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {source_token}"},
    )
    assert tampered.status_code == 409

    user_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {user_token}"}
    detail = client.get(f"/api/proactive/v1/incidents/{created['incident_id']}", headers=user_headers)
    assert detail.status_code == 200, detail.text
    assert len(detail.json()["events"]) == 1

    ack = client.post(f"/api/proactive/v1/incidents/{created['incident_id']}/acknowledge", headers=user_headers)
    assert ack.status_code == 200, ack.text
    resolved = client.post(
        f"/api/proactive/v1/incidents/{created['incident_id']}/resolve",
        headers=user_headers,
        json={"reason": "The task owner confirmed recovery."},
    )
    assert resolved.status_code == 200, resolved.text
    assert resolved.json()["state"] == "resolved"
    assert [row["action"] for row in resolved.json()["audit"]] == [
        "event_ingested", "incident_acknowledged", "incident_resolved"
    ]
    _store_for_path.cache_clear()


def test_unconfigured_feature_fails_closed(monkeypatch):
    for key in (
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_DB_PATH",
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_TOKEN",
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_ID",
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON",
    ):
        monkeypatch.delenv(key, raising=False)
    _store_for_path.cache_clear()
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(router)
    response = TestClient(app).get(
        "/api/proactive/v1/incidents",
        headers={"Authorization": "Bearer not-a-real-configured-token"},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 503
    _store_for_path.cache_clear()


Connect a real Aariya source to proactive events
This file describes the next code change after the event API foundation. It does not claim any source has been connected.
1. Configure a real source process
In the environment for a source that already exists (for example, the actual task runner or actual system monitor), configure:
- AARIYA_PROACTIVE_API_BASE_URL: the real API origin, such as the origin where the authenticated Aariya backend is actually running. Do not include /api/proactive/v1/events in this value.
- AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKEN: a unique secret configured for that source in the backend's AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON allow-list.
- Optionally, AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: a timeout from 0.2 to 30 seconds.
Do not store secrets in source code, commit them, or reuse a user session token as the source token. Production traffic requires HTTPS.
2. Call the source client at the real failure boundary
The available function is server.proactive.source_client.emit_event. Call it only from the actual monitor/task lifecycle where the failure has been observed. Map only facts the source has recorded:
- source_event_id: a stable ID generated and persisted by that source for the real event. It must remain the same on retry.
- event_type: the event type that source is configured to emit.
- severity, title, summary: actual source output or deterministic values derived from actual source facts. Never use an LLM guess as proof that an incident happened.
- occurred_at: the actual timezone-aware timestamp if the source records one.
- correlation_key: the actual durable run/task/incident key if the source has one. Omit it if no genuine key exists.
- evidence_refs: references to real evidence in the existing access-controlled evidence store. Do not fabricate references or upload secrets/transcripts into attributes.
Do not call the API after catching an unrelated exception just to create an incident. Preserve the original exception and execution result; report the observed failure without replacing the original failure path.
3. Error handling belongs to the actual producer
emit_event raises ProactiveSourceError for missing configuration, schema errors, rejected requests, invalid acknowledgements, and network failures. Integrate those errors into the source's existing durable job/retry mechanism. Retry a delivery with the same source_event_id; do not generate a new event ID on each retry. The client deliberately does not implement an independent infinite retry loop.
4. Required codebase inspection before merge
Before changing a real task runner or monitor, identify its actual event type, persistent event/run ID, exception handling boundary, evidence model, logger policy, and retry mechanism. The project source was not available in this session, so no guessed integration call is included here. The client file is standalone until the real producer is inspected.
5. Current feature boundary
This only delivers an observed event to the authenticated backend. It does not send push messages, start internet calls, execute corrective actions, or prove that a user received an alert. Those require the actual outbox, delivery-provider, Flutter and authorization integrations.

from server.proactive.router import router as proactive_router

# Add this after the existing FastAPI app is created.
app.include_router(proactive_router)


6. What still needs actual project code
The event backend is not the complete proactive communication system. The remaining implementation is:
1. Integrate actual Aariya task/system monitors and existing authentication.
2. Add a durable notification outbox with delivery status, retries, and acknowledgements.
3. Connect a real push-notification provider and internet voice-call/VoIP service.
4. Add the incident conversation and incoming-call handling to the existing Flutter app.
5. Connect the independent Guardian/Watchdog and authorization-controlled action pipeline.
6. Add the encryption and key-management design at the real mobile, gateway, and AI Core boundaries.

"""Environment-backed configuration for the proactive-event API.

No credentials or operational data have defaults. Missing configuration fails
closed rather than silently enabling unauthenticated event ingestion.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Mapping

from .schemas import EventType


class ConfigurationError(RuntimeError):
    """Required proactive-system configuration is absent or invalid."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SourceAuthorization:
    token: str
    allowed_event_types: frozenset[EventType]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Settings:
    database_path: Path
    user_token: str
    user_id: str
    source_tokens: Mapping[str, SourceAuthorization]

    @classmethod
    def from_environment(cls) -> "Settings":
        raw_db_path = os.environ.get("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_DB_PATH", "").strip()
        raw_user_token = os.environ.get("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_TOKEN", "")
        user_id = os.environ.get("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_ID", "").strip()
        raw_sources = os.environ.get("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON", "").strip()

        if not raw_db_path:
            raise ConfigurationError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_DB_PATH is required")
        database_path = Path(raw_db_path).expanduser()
        if not database_path.is_absolute():
            raise ConfigurationError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_DB_PATH must be an absolute path")
        if str(database_path) == ":memory:":
            raise ConfigurationError("An in-memory database is not permitted for durable incident storage")

        if len(raw_user_token) < 32:
            raise ConfigurationError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_TOKEN must be a generated secret of at least 32 characters")
        if not user_id:
            raise ConfigurationError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_ID is required")
        if len(user_id) > 160:
            raise ConfigurationError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_ID must not exceed 160 characters")
        if not raw_sources:
            raise ConfigurationError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON is required")

        try:
            source_tokens = json.loads(raw_sources)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise ConfigurationError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON must be valid JSON") from exc

        if not isinstance(source_tokens, dict) or not source_tokens:
            raise ConfigurationError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON must be a non-empty JSON object")
        validated_sources: dict[str, str] = {}
        seen_tokens: set[str] = set()
        allowed_keys = {"token", "event_types"}
        for source_id, entry in source_tokens.items():
            if not isinstance(source_id, str) or not source_id.strip() or len(source_id) > 160:
                raise ConfigurationError("Every configured source ID must be a non-empty string of at most 160 characters")
            if not isinstance(entry, dict) or set(entry) != allowed_keys:
                raise ConfigurationError("Each source must define only 'token' and 'event_types'")
            token = entry["token"]
            raw_event_types = entry["event_types"]
            if not isinstance(token, str) or len(token) < 32:
                raise ConfigurationError("Every configured source token must be a generated secret of at least 32 characters")
            if token == raw_user_token or token in seen_tokens:
                raise ConfigurationError("Source tokens must be unique and must not equal the user token")
            if not isinstance(raw_event_types, list) or not raw_event_types:
                raise ConfigurationError("Each source must have a non-empty explicit event_types allow-list")
            try:
                event_types = frozenset(EventType(item) for item in raw_event_types)
            except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise ConfigurationError("A source event_types allow-list contains an unsupported event type") from exc
            if len(event_types) != len(raw_event_types):
                raise ConfigurationError("A source event_types allow-list must not contain duplicates")
            validated_sources[source_id] = SourceAuthorization(token=token, allowed_event_types=event_types)
            seen_tokens.add(token)

        return cls(
            database_path=database_path,
            user_token=raw_user_token,
            user_id=user_id,
            source_tokens=validated_sources,
        )

"""Validated API contracts for real source observations and incident actions."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator


class EventType(StrEnum):
    SYSTEM_FAILURE = "system_failure"
    TASK_FAILURE = "task_failure"
    CRITICAL_EVENT = "critical_event"
    DEADLINE = "deadline"
    SECURITY_EVENT = "security_event"
    AUTONOMOUS_DISCOVERY = "autonomous_discovery"
    RECOVERY = "recovery"
    TASK_COMPLETED = "task_completed"


class Severity(StrEnum):
    INFO = "info"
    WARNING = "warning"
    HIGH = "high"
    CRITICAL = "critical"


class IncidentState(StrEnum):
    OPEN = "open"
    ACKNOWLEDGED = "acknowledged"
    RESOLVED = "resolved"


class EventIn(BaseModel):
    """A source-reported observation. Values are supplied by the real source."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", str_strip_whitespace=True)

    source_event_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    event_type: EventType
    severity: Severity
    title: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    summary: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=4_000)
    occurred_at: datetime | None = None
    correlation_key: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=200)
    confidence: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    evidence_refs: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=50)
    attributes: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

    @field_validator("occurred_at")
    @classmethod
    def require_timezone_for_source_time(cls, value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:
        if value is not None and (value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None):
            raise ValueError("occurred_at must include a timezone offset")
        return value

    @field_validator("evidence_refs")
    @classmethod
    def validate_evidence_refs(cls, values: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        for item in values:
            if not item.strip() or len(item) > 512:
                raise ValueError("each evidence reference must be non-empty and at most 512 characters")
        if len(values) != len(set(values)):
            raise ValueError("evidence_refs must not contain duplicates")
        return values

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_serializable_size(self) -> "EventIn":
        try:
            encoded = json.dumps(self.attributes, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False, separators=(",", ":"))
        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ValueError("attributes must contain JSON-compatible values only") from exc
        if len(encoded.encode("utf-8")) > 16_384:
            raise ValueError("attributes exceeds the 16 KiB limit")
        return self


class ResolveIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", str_strip_whitespace=True)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2_000)


class EventOut(BaseModel):
    event_id: str
    source_id: str
    source_event_id: str
    event_type: EventType
    severity: Severity
    title: str
    summary: str
    occurred_at: datetime | None
    received_at: datetime
    correlation_key: str | None
    confidence: float | None
    evidence_refs: list[str]
    attributes: dict[str, Any]


class AuditOut(BaseModel):
    audit_id: str
    actor_type: str
    actor_id: str
    action: str
    metadata: dict[str, Any]
    occurred_at: datetime


class IncidentOut(BaseModel):
    incident_id: str
    state: IncidentState
    severity: Severity
    title: str
    summary: str
    source_id: str
    correlation_key: str | None
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime
    acknowledged_at: datetime | None
    resolved_at: datetime | None
    resolution_reason: str | None
    events: list[EventOut] = Field(default_factory=list)
    audit: list[AuditOut] = Field(default_factory=list)


class EventIngestOut(BaseModel):
    event_id: str
    incident_id: str
    duplicate: bool
    incident_state: IncidentState

"""Persistence tests against real isolated SQLite files; no services are mocked."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from server.proactive.schemas import EventIn, EventType, IncidentState, Severity
from server.proactive.store import DuplicateEventConflict, EventStore, IncidentConflict


def _event(
    source_event_id: str,
    *,
    severity: Severity = Severity.WARNING,
    correlation_key: str | None = None,
) -> EventIn:
    return EventIn(
        source_event_id=source_event_id,
        event_type=EventType.TASK_FAILURE,
        severity=severity,
        title="Task execution failed",
        summary="The authorized task source reported a failed execution.",
        occurred_at=datetime(2026, 10, 24, 9, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        correlation_key=correlation_key,
        evidence_refs=["evidence-record:observed-task-log"],
        attributes={"exit_code": 1},
    )


def test_ingest_is_durable_and_idempotent(tmp_path):
    path = (tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve()
    first_store = EventStore(path)
    first, duplicate = first_store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001"))
    assert duplicate is False

    restarted_store = EventStore(path)
    repeated, duplicate = restarted_store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001"))
    assert duplicate is True
    assert repeated["event_id"] == first["event_id"]
    assert repeated["incident_id"] == first["incident_id"]

    incident = restarted_store.get_incident(first["incident_id"])
    assert incident["state"] == IncidentState.OPEN.value
    assert len(incident["events"]) == 1
    assert incident["events"][0]["evidence_refs"] == ["evidence-record:observed-task-log"]
    assert incident["audit"][0]["action"] == "event_ingested"


def test_correlation_uses_only_source_supplied_key_and_escalates_severity(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    low, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001", severity=Severity.WARNING, correlation_key="task-run-12"))
    high, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-002", severity=Severity.CRITICAL, correlation_key="task-run-12"))

    assert low["incident_id"] == high["incident_id"]
    incident = store.get_incident(low["incident_id"])
    assert incident["severity"] == Severity.CRITICAL.value
    assert len(incident["events"]) == 2


def test_different_sources_are_not_implicitly_correlated(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    first, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001", correlation_key="same-looking-key"))
    second, _ = store.ingest("system-monitor", _event("event-002", correlation_key="same-looking-key"))
    assert first["incident_id"] != second["incident_id"]


def test_acknowledge_and_resolve_are_audited_and_resolution_is_idempotent(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    result, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001"))
    incident_id = result["incident_id"]

    store.transition(incident_id, IncidentState.ACKNOWLEDGED, "account-test")
    store.transition(incident_id, IncidentState.RESOLVED, "account-test", "The task owner verified recovery.")
    store.transition(incident_id, IncidentState.RESOLVED, "account-test", "The task owner verified recovery.")

    incident = store.get_incident(incident_id)
    assert incident["state"] == IncidentState.RESOLVED.value
    assert [a["action"] for a in incident["audit"]] == [
        "event_ingested", "incident_acknowledged", "incident_resolved"
    ]


def test_resolved_incident_is_not_silently_reopened(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    first, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001", correlation_key="task-run-12"))
    store.transition(first["incident_id"], IncidentState.RESOLVED, "account-test", "Verified fixed.")
    next_event, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-002", correlation_key="task-run-12"))
    assert next_event["incident_id"] != first["incident_id"]


def test_invalid_state_transition_is_rejected(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    result, _ = store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001"))
    store.transition(result["incident_id"], IncidentState.RESOLVED, "account-test", "Verified fixed.")
    try:
        store.transition(result["incident_id"], IncidentState.ACKNOWLEDGED, "account-test")
    except IncidentConflict:
        pass
    else:
        raise AssertionError("Resolved incidents must not be acknowledged again")


def test_reusing_event_id_with_changed_content_is_rejected(tmp_path):
    store = EventStore((tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve())
    store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001", severity=Severity.WARNING))
    try:
        store.ingest("task-monitor", _event("event-001", severity=Severity.CRITICAL))
    except DuplicateEventConflict:
        pass
    else:
        raise AssertionError("Reusing a source event ID with changed content must be rejected")

"""HTTP boundary tests with a real FastAPI app and isolated SQLite storage."""

import json
import secrets

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from server.proactive.router import _store_for_path, router


def test_authenticated_ingestion_and_user_incident_lifecycle(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    user_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(48)
    source_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(48)
    database_path = (tmp_path / "proactive.sqlite3").resolve()
    monkeypatch.setenv("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_DB_PATH", str(database_path))
    monkeypatch.setenv("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_TOKEN", user_token)
    monkeypatch.setenv("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_ID", "verified-test-user")
    monkeypatch.setenv("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON", json.dumps({"task-monitor": {"token": source_token, "event_types": ["task_failure"]}}))
    _store_for_path.cache_clear()

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(router)
    client = TestClient(app)
    body = {
        "source_event_id": "task-run-event-01",
        "event_type": "task_failure",
        "severity": "high",
        "title": "Observed task failure",
        "summary": "The task monitor reported a failure from its real execution path.",
        "occurred_at": "2026-10-24T09:00:00+00:00",
        "evidence_refs": ["evidence-record:task-run-event-01"],
    }

    denied = client.post("/api/proactive/v1/events", json=body)
    assert denied.status_code == 401

    forbidden_body = {**body, "event_type": "security_event"}
    forbidden = client.post(
        "/api/proactive/v1/events", json=forbidden_body,
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {source_token}"},
    )
    assert forbidden.status_code == 403

    response = client.post(
        "/api/proactive/v1/events", json=body,
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {source_token}"},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 202, response.text
    created = response.json()
    assert created["duplicate"] is False

    repeated = client.post(
        "/api/proactive/v1/events", json=body,
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {source_token}"},
    )
    assert repeated.status_code == 202
    assert repeated.json()["duplicate"] is True
    assert repeated.json()["event_id"] == created["event_id"]
    tampered_duplicate = {**body, "severity": "critical"}
    tampered = client.post(
        "/api/proactive/v1/events", json=tampered_duplicate,
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {source_token}"},
    )
    assert tampered.status_code == 409

    user_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {user_token}"}
    detail = client.get(f"/api/proactive/v1/incidents/{created['incident_id']}", headers=user_headers)
    assert detail.status_code == 200, detail.text
    assert len(detail.json()["events"]) == 1

    ack = client.post(f"/api/proactive/v1/incidents/{created['incident_id']}/acknowledge", headers=user_headers)
    assert ack.status_code == 200, ack.text
    resolved = client.post(
        f"/api/proactive/v1/incidents/{created['incident_id']}/resolve",
        headers=user_headers,
        json={"reason": "The task owner confirmed recovery."},
    )
    assert resolved.status_code == 200, resolved.text
    assert resolved.json()["state"] == "resolved"
    assert [row["action"] for row in resolved.json()["audit"]] == [
        "event_ingested", "incident_acknowledged", "incident_resolved"
    ]
    _store_for_path.cache_clear()


def test_unconfigured_feature_fails_closed(monkeypatch):
    for key in (
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_DB_PATH",
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_TOKEN",
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_USER_ID",
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON",
    ):
        monkeypatch.delenv(key, raising=False)
    _store_for_path.cache_clear()
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(router)
    response = TestClient(app).get(
        "/api/proactive/v1/incidents",
        headers={"Authorization": "Bearer not-a-real-configured-token"},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 503
    _store_for_path.cache_clear()

"""Fail-closed client for real Aariya monitoring sources.

This module sends an event that an actual source has observed. It does not
create events, infer severity, retry indefinitely, or contain credentials.
Configure AARIYA_PROACTIVE_API_BASE_URL and AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKEN in
the environment used by the real source process.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import socket
from dataclasses import dataclass
from urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen
from typing import Any, Mapping

from pydantic import ValidationError

from .schemas import EventIn


class ProactiveSourceError(RuntimeError):
    """An event could not be submitted to the configured Aariya API."""

    def __init__(self, message: str, *, status_code: int | None = None) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SourceClientSettings:
    base_url: str
    source_token: str
    timeout_seconds: float

    @classmethod
    def from_environment(cls) -> "SourceClientSettings":
        raw_base_url = os.environ.get("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_API_BASE_URL", "").strip()
        token = os.environ.get("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKEN", "")
        raw_timeout = os.environ.get("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS", "5").strip()

        if not raw_base_url:
            raise ProactiveSourceError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_API_BASE_URL is required")
        if len(token) < 32:
            raise ProactiveSourceError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKEN must be a configured secret of at least 32 characters")

        parsed = urlsplit(raw_base_url)
        if parsed.scheme not in {"https", "http"} or not parsed.netloc:
            raise ProactiveSourceError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_API_BASE_URL must be an absolute HTTP(S) URL")
        if parsed.username or parsed.password or parsed.query or parsed.fragment:
            raise ProactiveSourceError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_API_BASE_URL must not contain credentials, query, or fragment")
        if parsed.scheme != "https" and parsed.hostname not in {"localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1"}:
            raise ProactiveSourceError("HTTPS is required except when connecting to a loopback development server")
        if parsed.path not in {"", "/"}:
            raise ProactiveSourceError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_API_BASE_URL must contain only the origin, without a path prefix")

        try:
            timeout = float(raw_timeout)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise ProactiveSourceError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS must be numeric") from exc
        if not 0.2 <= timeout <= 30:
            raise ProactiveSourceError("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS must be between 0.2 and 30")

        return cls(base_url=raw_base_url.rstrip("/"), source_token=token, timeout_seconds=timeout)


def emit_event(event: EventIn | Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Submit one source-observed event and return the server acknowledgement.

    Pass only real fields from an authorized source. `source_event_id` must be
    stable across retries so the server can enforce idempotency. Server-side
    event-type allow-lists remain authoritative.
    """
    try:
        validated = event if isinstance(event, EventIn) else EventIn.model_validate(event)
    except ValidationError as exc:
        raise ProactiveSourceError("Event payload failed schema validation") from exc

    settings = SourceClientSettings.from_environment()
    body = json.dumps(
        validated.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True),
        ensure_ascii=False,
        allow_nan=False,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    ).encode("utf-8")
    request = Request(
        url=f"{settings.base_url}/api/proactive/v1/events",
        data=body,
        method="POST",
        headers={
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {settings.source_token}",
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "Accept": "application/json",
        },
    )

    try:
        with urlopen(request, timeout=settings.timeout_seconds) as response:
            response_body = response.read(64 * 1024)
            if response.status != 202:
                raise ProactiveSourceError(
                    f"Aariya event API returned unexpected HTTP status {response.status}",
                    status_code=response.status,
                )
    except HTTPError as exc:
        # Do not propagate arbitrary response bodies into source logs; they may
        # contain operational details. The status code is enough for handling.
        raise ProactiveSourceError(
            f"Aariya event API rejected the event with HTTP status {exc.code}",
            status_code=exc.code,
        ) from exc
    except (URLError, TimeoutError, socket.timeout, OSError) as exc:
        # Deliberately no automatic retry: the caller's real job/event runner
        # should retry with the same source_event_id under its own policy.
        raise ProactiveSourceError("Aariya event API could not be reached") from exc

    try:
        result = json.loads(response_body.decode("utf-8"))
    except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ProactiveSourceError("Aariya event API returned an invalid acknowledgement") from exc
    if not isinstance(result, dict) or not isinstance(result.get("event_id"), str) or not isinstance(result.get("incident_id"), str):
        raise ProactiveSourceError("Aariya event API acknowledgement is missing required identifiers")
    return result

Connect a real Aariya source to proactive events
This file describes the next code change after the event API foundation. It does not claim any source has been connected.
1. Configure a real source process
In the environment for a source that already exists (for example, the actual task runner or actual system monitor), configure:
- AARIYA_PROACTIVE_API_BASE_URL: the real API origin, such as the origin where the authenticated Aariya backend is actually running. Do not include /api/proactive/v1/events in this value.
- AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKEN: a unique secret configured for that source in the backend's AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON allow-list.
- Optionally, AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: a timeout from 0.2 to 30 seconds.
Do not store secrets in source code, commit them, or reuse a user session token as the source token. Production traffic requires HTTPS.
2. Call the source client at the real failure boundary
The available function is server.proactive.source_client.emit_event. Call it only from the actual monitor/task lifecycle where the failure has been observed. Map only facts the source has recorded:
- source_event_id: a stable ID generated and persisted by that source for the real event. It must remain the same on retry.
- event_type: the event type that source is configured to emit.
- severity, title, summary: actual source output or deterministic values derived from actual source facts. Never use an LLM guess as proof that an incident happened.
- occurred_at: the actual timezone-aware timestamp if the source records one.
- correlation_key: the actual durable run/task/incident key if the source has one. Omit it if no genuine key exists.
- evidence_refs: references to real evidence in the existing access-controlled evidence store. Do not fabricate references or upload secrets/transcripts into attributes.
Do not call the API after catching an unrelated exception just to create an incident. Preserve the original exception and execution result; report the observed failure without replacing the original failure path.
3. Error handling belongs to the actual producer
emit_event raises ProactiveSourceError for missing configuration, schema errors, rejected requests, invalid acknowledgements, and network failures. Integrate those errors into the source's existing durable job/retry mechanism. Retry a delivery with the same source_event_id; do not generate a new event ID on each retry. The client deliberately does not implement an independent infinite retry loop.
4. Required codebase inspection before merge
Before changing a real task runner or monitor, identify its actual event type, persistent event/run ID, exception handling boundary, evidence model, logger policy, and retry mechanism. The project source was not available in this session, so no guessed integration call is included here. The client file is standalone until the real producer is inspected.
5. Current feature boundary
This only delivers an observed event to the authenticated backend. It does not send push messages, start internet calls, execute corrective actions, or prove that a user received an alert. Those require the actual outbox, delivery-provider, Flutter and authorization integrations.
 Connect a real Aariya source to proactive events

This file describes the next code change after the event API foundation. It does not claim any source has been connected.

## 1. Configure a real source process

In the environment for a source that already exists (for example, the actual task runner or actual system monitor), configure:

- `AARIYA_PROACTIVE_API_BASE_URL`: the real API origin, such as the origin where the authenticated Aariya backend is actually running. Do not include `/api/proactive/v1/events` in this value.
- `AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKEN`: a unique secret configured for that source in the backend's `AARIYA_PROACTIVE_SOURCE_TOKENS_JSON` allow-list.
- Optionally, `AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`: a timeout from 0.2 to 30 seconds.

Do not store secrets in source code, commit them, or reuse a user session token as the source token. Production traffic requires HTTPS.

## 2. Call the source client at the real failure boundary

The available function is `server.proactive.source_client.emit_event`. Call it only from the actual monitor/task lifecycle where the failure has been observed. Map only facts the source has recorded:

- `source_event_id`: a stable ID generated and persisted by that source for the real event. It must remain the same on retry.
- `event_type`: the event type that source is configured to emit.
- `severity`, `title`, `summary`: actual source output or deterministic values derived from actual source facts. Never use an LLM guess as proof that an incident happened.
- `occurred_at`: the actual timezone-aware timestamp if the source records one.
- `correlation_key`: the actual durable run/task/incident key if the source has one. Omit it if no genuine key exists.
- `evidence_refs`: references to real evidence in the existing access-controlled evidence store. Do not fabricate references or upload secrets/transcripts into `attributes`.

Do not call the API after catching an unrelated exception just to create an incident. Preserve the original exception and execution result; report the observed failure without replacing the original failure path.

## 3. Error handling belongs to the actual producer

`emit_event` raises `ProactiveSourceError` for missing configuration, schema errors, rejected requests, invalid acknowledgements, and network failures. Integrate those errors into the source's existing durable job/retry mechanism. Retry a delivery with the same `source_event_id`; do not generate a new event ID on each retry. The client deliberately does not implement an independent infinite retry loop.

## 4. Required codebase inspection before merge

Before changing a real task runner or monitor, identify its actual event type, persistent event/run ID, exception handling boundary, evidence model, logger policy, and retry mechanism. The project source was not available in this session, so no guessed integration call is included here. The client file is standalone until the real producer is inspected.

## 5. Current feature boundary

This only delivers an observed event to the authenticated backend. It does **not** send push messages, start internet calls, execute corrective actions, or prove that a user received an alert. Those require the actual outbox, delivery-provider, Flutter and authorization integrations.

"""Durable notification outbox primitives for Aariya.

This module queues notifications only. It deliberately contains no fake provider,
recipient, event, credential, or automatic notification policy. A real dispatcher
must supply channel, recipient, payload, and retry/lease policy from trusted runtime
configuration and real incident state.

Delivery semantics are at-least-once. Providers should honor ``job_id`` as an
idempotency key when they support it; a process crash after provider acceptance but
before recording the receipt can otherwise result in a duplicate delivery.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
import uuid
from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable


class OutboxConflict(RuntimeError):
    """A job transition is invalid or an idempotency key was reused differently."""


class OutboxNotFound(LookupError):
    """The requested outbox job or attempt does not exist."""


class OutboxValidationError(ValueError):
    """The supplied delivery data or timing policy is invalid."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class NotificationJob:
    job_id: str
    dedupe_key: str
    incident_id: str | None
    channel: str
    recipient_id: str
    payload: dict[str, Any]
    state: str
    attempts: int
    max_attempts: int
    available_at: str
    lease_until: str | None
    provider_message_id: str | None
    last_error_code: str | None
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ClaimedNotification:
    job: NotificationJob
    attempt_id: str
    attempt_number: int


def utc_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def _iso(value: datetime) -> str:
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="microseconds")


def _json(value: Any) -> str:
    try:
        encoded = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise OutboxValidationError("payload must contain finite JSON-compatible values") from exc
    if len(encoded.encode("utf-8")) > 16_384:
        raise OutboxValidationError("payload must not exceed 16 KiB")
    return encoded


def _decode(row: sqlite3.Row) -> NotificationJob:
    return NotificationJob(
        job_id=row["job_id"],
        dedupe_key=row["dedupe_key"],
        incident_id=row["incident_id"],
        channel=row["channel"],
        recipient_id=row["recipient_id"],
        payload=json.loads(row["payload_json"]),
        state=row["state"],
        attempts=row["attempts"],
        max_attempts=row["max_attempts"],
        available_at=row["available_at"],
        lease_until=row["lease_until"],
        provider_message_id=row["provider_message_id"],
        last_error_code=row["last_error_code"],
        created_at=row["created_at"],
        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
    )


class NotificationOutbox:
    """SQLite outbox; use the same configured DB path as the proactive event store.

    This is a single-host implementation. For multiple backend instances, use a
    shared production database/queue with equivalent atomic-claim semantics.
    """

    def __init__(self, database_path: Path):
        if not database_path.is_absolute():
            raise OutboxValidationError("database_path must be absolute")
        if str(database_path) == ":memory:":
            raise OutboxValidationError("durable outbox requires a file-backed database")
        self.database_path = database_path
        self.database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._initialize()

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        connection = sqlite3.connect(self.database_path, timeout=5.0, isolation_level=None)
        connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        connection.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
        connection.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000")
        connection.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
        return connection

    def _initialize(self) -> None:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            db.executescript(
                """
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_notification_outbox (
                    job_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    dedupe_key TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                    incident_id TEXT,
                    channel TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(channel) BETWEEN 1 AND 80),
                    recipient_id TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(recipient_id) BETWEEN 1 AND 256),
                    payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                    state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('pending','in_flight','delivered','dead','cancelled')),
                    attempts INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(attempts >= 0),
                    max_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(max_attempts BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),
                    available_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    lease_until TEXT,
                    provider_message_id TEXT,
                    last_error_code TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_proactive_outbox_due
                    ON proactive_notification_outbox(state, available_at, lease_until, created_at);
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_proactive_outbox_incident
                    ON proactive_notification_outbox(incident_id, state);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_notification_attempts (
                    attempt_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    job_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES proactive_notification_outbox(job_id),
                    attempt_number INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(attempt_number > 0),
                    state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('started','delivered','retryable_failure','permanent_failure','cancelled')),
                    started_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    finished_at TEXT,
                    provider_message_id TEXT,
                    error_code TEXT,
                    UNIQUE(job_id, attempt_number)
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_proactive_outbox_attempts_job
                    ON proactive_notification_attempts(job_id, attempt_number);
                """
            )

    def enqueue(
        self,
        *,
        dedupe_key: str,
        incident_id: str | None,
        channel: str,
        recipient_id: str,
        payload: dict[str, Any],
        max_attempts: int,
        available_at: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[NotificationJob, bool]:
        """Persist a notification request; return (job, duplicate)."""
        values = {
            "dedupe_key": dedupe_key,
            "incident_id": incident_id,
            "channel": channel,
            "recipient_id": recipient_id,
        }
        for key, value in values.items():
            if value is not None and (not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip()):
                raise OutboxValidationError(f"{key} must be a non-empty string when provided")
        if len(dedupe_key) > 512:
            raise OutboxValidationError("dedupe_key must not exceed 512 characters")
        if incident_id is not None and len(incident_id) > 200:
            raise OutboxValidationError("incident_id must not exceed 200 characters")
        if len(channel) > 80 or len(recipient_id) > 256:
            raise OutboxValidationError("channel or recipient_id exceeds its maximum length")
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            raise OutboxValidationError("payload must be a JSON object")
        if not 1 <= max_attempts <= 1_000:
            raise OutboxValidationError("max_attempts must be between 1 and 1000")
        payload_json = _json(payload)
        now = utc_now()
        available = available_at or now
        if available.tzinfo is None or available.utcoffset() is None:
            raise OutboxValidationError("available_at must include a timezone")
        now_text, available_text = _iso(now), _iso(available)

        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            existing = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE dedupe_key = ?",
                (dedupe_key,),
            ).fetchone()
            if existing:
                expected = (incident_id, channel, recipient_id, payload_json, max_attempts)
                actual = (existing["incident_id"], existing["channel"], existing["recipient_id"], existing["payload_json"], existing["max_attempts"])
                if actual != expected:
                    raise OutboxConflict("dedupe_key was reused with different notification content")
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return _decode(existing), True

            job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            db.execute(
                """INSERT INTO proactive_notification_outbox
                   (job_id, dedupe_key, incident_id, channel, recipient_id, payload_json,
                    state, attempts, max_attempts, available_at, lease_until, provider_message_id,
                    last_error_code, created_at, updated_at)
                   VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending', 0, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?, ?)""",
                (job_id, dedupe_key, incident_id, channel, recipient_id, payload_json, max_attempts, available_text, now_text, now_text),
            )
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return _decode(row), False
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def claim_due(
        self,
        *,
        channels: Iterable[str],
        limit: int,
        lease_seconds: int,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> list[ClaimedNotification]:
        """Atomically lease due jobs only for channels with registered providers."""
        channel_list = tuple(dict.fromkeys(channels))
        if not channel_list:
            return []
        if any(not isinstance(ch, str) or not ch.strip() for ch in channel_list):
            raise OutboxValidationError("channels must contain non-empty names")
        if not 1 <= limit <= 500:
            raise OutboxValidationError("limit must be between 1 and 500")
        if not 1 <= lease_seconds <= 86_400:
            raise OutboxValidationError("lease_seconds must be between 1 and 86400")
        clock = now or utc_now()
        if clock.tzinfo is None or clock.utcoffset() is None:
            raise OutboxValidationError("now must include a timezone")
        now_text = _iso(clock)
        lease_until = _iso(clock + timedelta(seconds=lease_seconds))
        placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in channel_list)
        db = self._connect()
        claimed: list[ClaimedNotification] = []
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            exhausted = db.execute(
                """SELECT job_id FROM proactive_notification_outbox
                   WHERE state = 'in_flight' AND lease_until <= ? AND attempts >= max_attempts""",
                (now_text,),
            ).fetchall()
            for exhausted_row in exhausted:
                job_id = exhausted_row["job_id"]
                db.execute(
                    """UPDATE proactive_notification_attempts
                       SET state = 'permanent_failure', finished_at = ?, error_code = 'lease_expired_after_max_attempts'
                       WHERE job_id = ? AND state = 'started'""",
                    (now_text, job_id),
                )
                db.execute(
                    """UPDATE proactive_notification_outbox
                       SET state = 'dead', lease_until = NULL, last_error_code = 'lease_expired_after_max_attempts', updated_at = ?
                       WHERE job_id = ?""",
                    (now_text, job_id),
                )

            rows = db.execute(
                f"""SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox
                    WHERE channel IN ({placeholders})
                      AND attempts < max_attempts
                      AND available_at <= ?
                      AND (state = 'pending' OR (state = 'in_flight' AND lease_until <= ?))
                    ORDER BY available_at ASC, created_at ASC, job_id ASC
                    LIMIT ?""",
                (*channel_list, now_text, now_text, limit),
            ).fetchall()
            for row in rows:
                # Close the expired attempt before creating its successor.
                # The previous worker may still return later, so transition methods
                # also verify that the attempt is still the current attempt number.
                if row["state"] == "in_flight":
                    db.execute(
                        """UPDATE proactive_notification_attempts
                           SET state = 'retryable_failure', finished_at = ?, error_code = 'lease_expired'
                           WHERE job_id = ? AND attempt_number = ? AND state = 'started'""",
                        (now_text, row["job_id"], int(row["attempts"])),
                    )
                # Reclaiming expired leases is at-least-once. Provider idempotency should use job_id.
                attempt_number = int(row["attempts"]) + 1
                attempt_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
                db.execute(
                    """UPDATE proactive_notification_outbox
                       SET state = 'in_flight', attempts = ?, lease_until = ?, updated_at = ?
                       WHERE job_id = ?""",
                    (attempt_number, lease_until, now_text, row["job_id"]),
                )
                db.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO proactive_notification_attempts
                       (attempt_id, job_id, attempt_number, state, started_at)
                       VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'started', ?)""",
                    (attempt_id, row["job_id"], attempt_number, now_text),
                )
                updated = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (row["job_id"],)).fetchone()
                claimed.append(ClaimedNotification(_decode(updated), attempt_id, attempt_number))
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return claimed
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def mark_delivered(self, *, job_id: str, attempt_id: str, provider_message_id: str | None) -> NotificationJob:
        now = _iso(utc_now())
        if provider_message_id is not None and (not provider_message_id.strip() or len(provider_message_id) > 512):
            raise OutboxValidationError("provider_message_id must be non-empty and at most 512 characters when provided")
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            job = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            attempt = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_attempts WHERE attempt_id = ? AND job_id = ?", (attempt_id, job_id)).fetchone()
            if job is None or attempt is None:
                raise OutboxNotFound("Notification job or attempt not found")
            if job["state"] == "delivered":
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return _decode(job)
            if (job["state"] != "in_flight" or attempt["state"] != "started"
                    or int(attempt["attempt_number"]) != int(job["attempts"])):
                raise OutboxConflict("Only the current in-flight attempt can be marked delivered")
            db.execute(
                """UPDATE proactive_notification_outbox
                   SET state = 'delivered', lease_until = NULL, provider_message_id = ?,
                       last_error_code = NULL, updated_at = ? WHERE job_id = ?""",
                (provider_message_id, now, job_id),
            )
            db.execute(
                """UPDATE proactive_notification_attempts
                   SET state = 'delivered', finished_at = ?, provider_message_id = ? WHERE attempt_id = ?""",
                (now, provider_message_id, attempt_id),
            )
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return _decode(row)
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def mark_failed(
        self,
        *,
        job_id: str,
        attempt_id: str,
        error_code: str,
        retryable: bool,
        retry_delay_seconds: int,
    ) -> NotificationJob:
        """Record failure without persisting raw exception text or credential-bearing details."""
        if not error_code.strip() or len(error_code) > 120:
            raise OutboxValidationError("error_code must be a non-empty provider-safe code of at most 120 characters")
        if not 0 <= retry_delay_seconds <= 31_536_000:
            raise OutboxValidationError("retry_delay_seconds must be between 0 and one year")
        now_dt = utc_now()
        now_text = _iso(now_dt)
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            job = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            attempt = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_attempts WHERE attempt_id = ? AND job_id = ?", (attempt_id, job_id)).fetchone()
            if job is None or attempt is None:
                raise OutboxNotFound("Notification job or attempt not found")
            if (job["state"] != "in_flight" or attempt["state"] != "started"
                    or int(attempt["attempt_number"]) != int(job["attempts"])):
                raise OutboxConflict("Only the current in-flight attempt can be marked failed")

            will_retry = retryable and int(job["attempts"]) < int(job["max_attempts"])
            next_state = "pending" if will_retry else "dead"
            attempt_state = "retryable_failure" if will_retry else "permanent_failure"
            next_available = _iso(now_dt + timedelta(seconds=retry_delay_seconds)) if will_retry else job["available_at"]
            db.execute(
                """UPDATE proactive_notification_outbox
                   SET state = ?, available_at = ?, lease_until = NULL, last_error_code = ?, updated_at = ?
                   WHERE job_id = ?""",
                (next_state, next_available, error_code, now_text, job_id),
            )
            db.execute(
                """UPDATE proactive_notification_attempts
                   SET state = ?, finished_at = ?, error_code = ? WHERE attempt_id = ?""",
                (attempt_state, now_text, error_code, attempt_id),
            )
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return _decode(row)
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def cancel_claimed(self, *, job_id: str, attempt_id: str, reason_code: str) -> NotificationJob:
        """Cancel a claimed job when a caller confirms its incident is no longer actionable."""
        if not reason_code.strip() or len(reason_code) > 120:
            raise OutboxValidationError("reason_code must be a short non-empty code")
        now = _iso(utc_now())
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            job = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            attempt = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_attempts WHERE attempt_id = ? AND job_id = ?", (attempt_id, job_id)).fetchone()
            if job is None or attempt is None:
                raise OutboxNotFound("Notification job or attempt not found")
            if (job["state"] != "in_flight" or attempt["state"] != "started"
