5. What this finishes—and what remains
Implemented in the overlay

Persistent heartbeat evaluation, health-state transition records, a retryable transition queue, worker leases, recovery after expired leases, and six new evaluator tests.



Still required before alerts are sent

A concrete HealthTransitionHandler backed by the real persisted importance/escalation policy, authenticated user/device registry, and notification outbox. The queue does not yet send a push notification or call.



Guardian is not independent yet

The evaluator worker is a separate process, but it shares the configured database and source configuration. A real Guardian must operate in an independent failure domain and use a separate alert path if it needs to report a database or Core outage.

Next implementation: real source-health transition handler
This step connects the monitoring-health evaluator to Aariya's incident and notification-decision pipeline.

The new handler has been tested with the existing overlay: 50 tests pass, and the Python modules compile successfully.

The important change is that a monitoring source becoming stale no longer stops at a database record. The handler can now take a persisted transition, load an approved policy, resolve the source's owner, check authorized recipients, create the source-health incident and persist its escalation decision and notification jobs.

It still does not send an actual push notification or call. That remains the responsibility of the configured provider and delivery worker.
1. Create server/proactive/health_transition_handler.py
The complete source is available individually here: Open health_transition_handler.py.

This module has four important safeguards:
- Missing or overlapping approved policies cause processing to fail closed.
- Source ownership and authorized recipients come from injected registry adapters—not invented IDs.
- The incident and escalation decision are written through the existing persistent stores.
- A recovery transition resolves only the corresponding monitoring-source incident, not unrelated application incidents.
The policy table has no seeded/default rules. Policy publication must be handled by an authenticated administrative/configuration workflow.
2. Required change in server/proactive/schemas.py
Add a distinct event type for monitoring-source health so the system does not mislabel a heartbeat outage as proof of an underlying system failure.
class EventType(StrEnum):    SYSTEM_FAILURE = "system_failure"    TASK_FAILURE = "task_failure"    CRITICAL_EVENT = "critical_event"    DEADLINE = "deadline"    SECURITY_EVENT = "security_event"    AUTONOMOUS_DISCOVERY = "autonomous_discovery"    RECOVERY = "recovery"    TASK_COMPLETED = "task_completed"    # Internal event describing a monitor's reporting state.    MONITORING_SOURCE_HEALTH = "monitoring_source_health"



The handler uses this event type when persisting transitions such as healthy → stale or stale → healthy.
3. Required change in server/proactive/store.py
The recovery path needs a narrowly scoped method that resolves a source-health incident only when its internal producer and correlation key match the specific recovered source.

Add this method inside the existing EventStore class:
def resolve_source_health_incident(    self,    incident_id: str,    monitored_source_id: str,    *,    reason: str,) -> dict[str, Any]:    """Resolve only the internal incident for a recovered monitor."""    if not monitored_source_id.strip():        raise ValueError("monitored_source_id is required")    if not reason.strip() or len(reason) > 2_000:        raise ValueError("reason must be 1..2000 characters")    internal_source_id = "aariya-source-health-evaluator"    correlation_key = f"source-health:{monitored_source_id}"    db = self._connect()    try:        db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")        row = db.execute(            "SELECT * FROM proactive_incidents WHERE incident_id = ?",            (incident_id,),        ).fetchone()        if row is None:            raise IncidentNotFound(incident_id)        if (            row["source_id"] != internal_source_id            or row["correlation_key"] != correlation_key        ):            raise IncidentConflict(                "Only the matching source-health incident "                "can be auto-resolved"            )



This uses the existing store's _connect(), _audit(), utc_now(), IncidentNotFound and IncidentConflict definitions. It does not create a separate database or duplicate the incident model.
4. Connect the handler to the transition worker
The worker must process the durable transition queue, not just evaluate heartbeats. The updated worker is available separately: Open source_health_worker.py.

The critical processing call is:
from .source_health_evaluator import process_pending_transitionsprocessing = process_pending_transitions(    evaluator,    handler,    batch_size=batch_size,    lease_seconds=lease_seconds,    retry_delay_seconds=retry_delay_seconds,)_log_event("transition_processing_completed", **processing)



The worker's configuration must include:
AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_HANDLER_FACTORY=
AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_TRANSITION_BATCH_SIZE=
AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_TRANSITION_LEASE_SECONDS=
AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_TRANSITION_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS=


AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_HANDLER_FACTORY must identify a trusted factory in the real project, in the form your_actual_module:build_health_transition_handler. That factory must construct the handler using Aariya's real source-ownership registry and authorized device/recipient registry. It must not use a test adapter.

The worker intentionally refuses to start if its required handler factory or operational limits are missing. That is safer than silently recording health transitions while pretending alerts are being processed.
5. Initial heartbeat grace period
The previous evaluator reported a configured source with no heartbeat immediately. That could produce false alerts when a monitor had only just been installed.

The updated source_health_evaluator.py now tracks when a source was first observed by the evaluator. A source with no heartbeat must wait one configured heartbeat interval before becoming no_heartbeat.

The key check is:
if (    health.status == "no_heartbeat"    and health.configured_interval_seconds is not None):    registration = db.execute(        """        SELECT * FROM proactive_source_health_registration        WHERE source_id = ?        """,        (health.source_id,),    ).fetchone()    interval = health.configured_interval_seconds    if registration is None:        db.execute(            """            INSERT INTO proactive_source_health_registration (                source_id, first_seen_at, interval_seconds            )            VALUES (?, ?, ?)            """,            (health.source_id, stamp, interval),        )        continue    if int(registration["interval_seconds"]) != interval:        db.execute(            """            UPDATE proactive_source_health_registration            SET first_seen_at = ?, interval_seconds = ?            WHERE source_id = ?            """,            (stamp, interval, health.source_id),        )        continue



The evaluator persists the registration time, so restarting the worker does not restart the grace period. If the configured interval changes, it begins a new grace period. Sources without a configured interval remain unconfigured; they are never automatically considered healthy.

The handler tests cover:
- A stale-source transition creating an incident and authorized notification job.

These are isolated automated tests. They do not prove that the actual Aariya identity system, admin policy workflow, push provider, VoIP provider or Flutter app is integrated.

The authenticated policy publication workflow, the concrete source-owner and trusted-device adapters, and the factory connecting those real services to the worker. No real push or call provider is enabled by this step.



The next step is to implement the real registry adapters and policy administration path against your actual Aariya backend. That requires inspecting the current backend authentication and database schemas rather than inventing replacement tables for accounts and devices.


"""Translate persisted source-health transitions into authorized incidents.

The handler never invents a policy, owner, recipient, or operational secret.
It requires an approved policy store plus adapters backed by Aariya's real
source ownership and trusted-device registry. Missing dependencies fail closed.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Protocol, Sequence

from .escalation_delivery import (
    CONTACT_CHANNELS,
    NON_CONTACT_CHANNELS,
    DeliveryIntent,
    EscalationDecision,
    EscalationDeliveryStore,
    make_decision_key,
)
from .schemas import EventIn, EventType, Severity
from .source_health_evaluator import HealthTransition
from .store import EventStore


class HealthPolicyUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    pass


class HealthPolicyAmbiguous(RuntimeError):
    pass


class SourceOwnershipUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    pass


class NoAuthorizedRecipient(RuntimeError):
    pass


class HealthPolicyStore:
    """Read-only resolver for approved, effective-dated health policies.

    Policy publication belongs to Aariya's authenticated admin/configuration
    plane. This class intentionally has no seed/default policy and no public
    method that can silently publish an approval.
    """

    def __init__(self, database_path: Path):
        if not database_path.is_absolute() or str(database_path) == ":memory:":
            raise ValueError("A file-backed absolute database path is required")
        self.database_path = database_path
        self.database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            db.executescript(
                """
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_health_policies (
                    policy_record_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    source_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    transition_to TEXT NOT NULL,
                    version TEXT NOT NULL,
                    severity TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(severity IN (
                        'info', 'warning', 'high', 'critical'
                    )),
                    score REAL NOT NULL CHECK(score >= 0 AND score <= 1),
                    confidence REAL NOT NULL
                        CHECK(confidence >= 0 AND confidence <= 1),
                    recommended_channel TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(
                        recommended_channel IN (
                            'record_only', 'review_required', 'suppressed',
                            'message', 'push', 'call', 'sms'
                        )
                    ),
                    requires_verification INTEGER NOT NULL
                        CHECK(requires_verification IN (0, 1)),
                    rationale TEXT NOT NULL,
                    max_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL
                        CHECK(max_attempts BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),
                    approval_reference TEXT NOT NULL,
                    approved_by TEXT NOT NULL,
                    state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('approved', 'retired')),
                    valid_from TEXT NOT NULL,
                    valid_until TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    CHECK(valid_until IS NULL OR valid_until > valid_from)
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_proactive_health_policy_lookup
                    ON proactive_health_policies(
                        source_id, transition_to, state, valid_from, valid_until
                    );
                """
            )

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        db = sqlite3.connect(
            self.database_path,
            timeout=5.0,
            isolation_level=None,
        )
        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        db.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000")
        db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
        return db

    def get_for_transition(
        self,
        *,
        source_id: str,
        transition_to: str,
        observed_at: datetime,
    ) -> "HealthEscalationPolicy":
        if observed_at.tzinfo is None or observed_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("observed_at must be timezone-aware")
        at = observed_at.astimezone(timezone.utc)

        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            rows = db.execute(
                """
                SELECT * FROM proactive_health_policies
                WHERE source_id = ?
                  AND transition_to = ?
                  AND state = 'approved'
                ORDER BY policy_record_id
                """,
                (source_id, transition_to),
            ).fetchall()

        active_rows = []
        for candidate in rows:
            try:
                valid_from = datetime.fromisoformat(candidate["valid_from"])
                valid_until = (
                    datetime.fromisoformat(candidate["valid_until"])
                    if candidate["valid_until"] is not None
                    else None
                )
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise HealthPolicyUnavailable(
                    "A stored health policy has an invalid effective timestamp"
                ) from exc

            if (
                valid_from.tzinfo is None
                or valid_from.utcoffset() is None
                or (
                    valid_until is not None
                    and (
                        valid_until.tzinfo is None
                        or valid_until.utcoffset() is None
                    )
                )
            ):
                raise HealthPolicyUnavailable(
                    "Stored policy effective timestamps must be timezone-aware"
                )

            effective = (
                valid_from.astimezone(timezone.utc) <= at
                and (
                    valid_until is None
                    or valid_until.astimezone(timezone.utc) > at
                )
            )
            if effective:
                active_rows.append(candidate)

        if not active_rows:
            raise HealthPolicyUnavailable(
                "No approved effective policy exists for this source transition"
            )
        if len(active_rows) > 1:
            raise HealthPolicyAmbiguous(
                "Multiple approved health policies apply to this transition"
            )

        row = active_rows[0]
        if not row["approval_reference"].strip() or not row["approved_by"].strip():
            raise HealthPolicyUnavailable(
                "Health policy lacks approval provenance"
            )

        return HealthEscalationPolicy(
            policy_record_id=row["policy_record_id"],
            source_id=row["source_id"],
            transition_to=row["transition_to"],
            version=row["version"],
            severity=Severity(row["severity"]),
            score=float(row["score"]),
            confidence=float(row["confidence"]),
            recommended_channel=row["recommended_channel"],
            requires_verification=bool(row["requires_verification"]),
            rationale=row["rationale"],
            max_attempts=int(row["max_attempts"]),
            approval_reference=row["approval_reference"],
            approved_by=row["approved_by"],
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class HealthEscalationPolicy:
    policy_record_id: str
    source_id: str
    transition_to: str
    version: str
    severity: Severity
    score: float
    confidence: float
    recommended_channel: str
    requires_verification: bool
    rationale: str
    max_attempts: int
    approval_reference: str
    approved_by: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SourceOwner:
    user_id: str
    active: bool


class SourceOwnershipRegistry(Protocol):
    def get_active_owner(self, source_id: str) -> SourceOwner | None:
        """Load ownership from Aariya's real source-registration store."""
        ...


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AuthorizedRecipient:
    recipient_id: str
    user_id: str
    channel: str
    active: bool
    trusted: bool
    consented_for_channel: bool


class AuthorizedRecipientRegistry(Protocol):
    def resolve_recipients(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        source_id: str,
        incident_id: str,
        channel: str,
    ) -> Sequence[AuthorizedRecipient]:
        """Return active targets authorized by real user/device records."""
        ...


_INTERNAL_EVENT_SOURCE = "aariya-source-health-evaluator"
_ALLOWED_SOURCE_STATUSES = frozenset({
    "healthy", "stale", "no_heartbeat", "unconfigured", "clock_anomaly"
})


class HealthTransitionHandler:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        database_path: Path,
        policies: HealthPolicyStore,
        ownership: SourceOwnershipRegistry,
        recipients: AuthorizedRecipientRegistry,
        events: EventStore,
        decisions: EscalationDeliveryStore,
    ) -> None:
        if not database_path.is_absolute():
            raise ValueError("database_path must be absolute")
        for name, store_path in (
            ("policy", policies.database_path),
            ("event", events.database_path),
            ("decision", decisions.database_path),
        ):
            if store_path.resolve() != database_path.resolve():
                raise ValueError(f"{name} store must use the configured database")
        self.database_path = database_path
        self.policies = policies
        self.ownership = ownership
        self.recipients = recipients
        self.events = events
        self.decisions = decisions

    def handle(self, transition: HealthTransition) -> None:
        """Process one durable transition, safely retryable by transition ID."""
        if transition.current_status not in _ALLOWED_SOURCE_STATUSES:
            raise ValueError("Unsupported source-health status")
        if not transition.source_id.strip() or not transition.transition_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("Transition identity is incomplete")

        observed_at = datetime.fromisoformat(transition.observed_at)
        if observed_at.tzinfo is None or observed_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("Transition timestamp must include a timezone")

        policy = self.policies.get_for_transition(
            source_id=transition.source_id,
            transition_to=transition.current_status,
            observed_at=observed_at,
        )

        owner = self.ownership.get_active_owner(transition.source_id)
        if owner is None or not owner.active or not owner.user_id.strip():
            raise SourceOwnershipUnavailable(
                "No active authenticated owner is registered for this source"
            )

        # Recovery is recorded and closes only this monitor-health incident.
        # A recovery transition is not itself a reason to start a new call.
        if transition.current_status == "healthy" and (
            policy.recommended_channel != "record_only"
            or policy.requires_verification
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Healthy transitions must use an approved record_only policy"
            )

        title = "Monitoring source health changed"
        summary = (
            f"Monitoring source '{transition.source_id}' changed status "
            f"from '{transition.previous_status}' to "
            f"'{transition.current_status}'. This confirms the source's "
            "reporting state only; the health of any subsystem it monitors "
            "has not been established by this transition."
        )
        event = EventIn(
            source_event_id=transition.transition_id,
            event_type=EventType.MONITORING_SOURCE_HEALTH,
            severity=policy.severity,
            title=title,
            summary=summary,
            occurred_at=observed_at,
            correlation_key=f"source-health:{transition.source_id}",
            confidence=policy.confidence,
            evidence_refs=[],
            attributes={
                "transition_id": transition.transition_id,
                "monitoring_source_id": transition.source_id,
                "previous_status": transition.previous_status,
                "current_status": transition.current_status,
                "configured_interval_seconds": transition.interval_seconds,
                "heartbeat_age_seconds": transition.age_seconds,
                "heartbeat_id": transition.heartbeat_id,
                "heartbeat_sequence": transition.heartbeat_sequence,
                "underlying_subsystem_health_verified": False,
            },
        )

        event_result, _duplicate = self.events.ingest(
            _INTERNAL_EVENT_SOURCE,
            event,
        )
        incident_id = event_result["incident_id"]

        if transition.current_status == "healthy":
            if policy.recommended_channel != "record_only":
                raise ValueError("Recovery policy cannot contact a user")
            intents: tuple[DeliveryIntent, ...] = ()
        elif policy.requires_verification or policy.recommended_channel in NON_CONTACT_CHANNELS:
            intents = ()
        elif policy.recommended_channel in CONTACT_CHANNELS:
            resolved = self.recipients.resolve_recipients(
                user_id=owner.user_id,
                source_id=transition.source_id,
                incident_id=incident_id,
                channel=policy.recommended_channel,
            )

            allowed: list[AuthorizedRecipient] = []
            seen: set[str] = set()
            for target in resolved:
                if (
                    target.user_id != owner.user_id
                    or target.channel != policy.recommended_channel
                    or not target.recipient_id.strip()
                    or not target.active
                    or not target.trusted
                    or not target.consented_for_channel
                ):
                    continue
                if target.recipient_id in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(target.recipient_id)
                allowed.append(target)

            if not allowed:
                raise NoAuthorizedRecipient(
                    "Policy requires contact but no active trusted, consented "
                    "recipient is available"
                )

            # Push/call payloads contain only routing metadata. The client must
            # fetch private incident details through authenticated endpoints.
            intents = tuple(
                DeliveryIntent(
                    channel=policy.recommended_channel,
                    recipient_id=target.recipient_id,
                    payload={
                        "incident_id": incident_id,
                        "kind": "monitoring_source_health",
                        "title": "A monitored source needs attention",
                    },
                    max_attempts=policy.max_attempts,
                    # Use the immutable transition timestamp for stable retries.
                    available_at=observed_at,
                )
                for target in allowed
            )
        else:
            raise HealthPolicyUnavailable(
                "The approved policy returned an unsupported channel"
            )

        decision_key = make_decision_key(
            incident_id=incident_id,
            trigger_id=transition.transition_id,
            policy_record_id=policy.policy_record_id,
            policy_version=policy.version,
        )

        self.decisions.persist_and_enqueue(
            EscalationDecision(
                decision_key=decision_key,
                incident_id=incident_id,
                user_id=owner.user_id,
                policy_record_id=policy.policy_record_id,
                policy_version=policy.version,
                trigger_id=transition.transition_id,
                score=policy.score,
                confidence=policy.confidence,
                recommended_channel=policy.recommended_channel,
                rationale=(
                    f"{policy.rationale} Source reporting changed from "
                    f"'{transition.previous_status}' to "
                    f"'{transition.current_status}'. Underlying subsystem "
                    "health is not verified by heartbeat status alone."
                ),
                requires_verification=policy.requires_verification,
                intents=intents,
            )
        )

        if transition.current_status == "healthy":
            # The incident event and record-only decision are persisted first.
            # Only the dedicated source-health incident can be auto-resolved.
            self.events.resolve_source_health_incident(
                incident_id,
                transition.source_id,
                reason=(
                    "Monitoring source recovery was confirmed by an accepted "
                    "heartbeat. This resolves the source-reporting incident, "
                    "not every underlying system incident."
                ),
            )

"""Run source-health evaluations continuously in a supervised process.

Run from the repository root with:
    python -m server.proactive.source_health_worker

This worker records health-state transitions only. It does not send messages,
initiate calls, or claim that the monitored application itself is healthy.
The durable transition processor must connect to the approved policy/outbox
handler before alerts can be delivered.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import importlib
import json
import logging
import os
import signal
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from .config import ConfigurationError, Settings
from .source_health import SourceHealthStore
from .source_health_evaluator import (
    SourceHealthEvaluator,
    process_pending_transitions,
)


logger = logging.getLogger("aariya.proactive.source_health_worker")


def _required_int(name: str, *, minimum: int, maximum: int) -> int:
    raw = os.environ.get(name, "").strip()
    if not raw:
        raise ConfigurationError(f"{name} is required")
    try:
        value = int(raw)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ConfigurationError(f"{name} must be an integer") from exc
    if not minimum <= value <= maximum:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"{name} must be between {minimum} and {maximum}"
        )
    return value


def _log_event(event: str, **fields: object) -> None:
    # Only operational IDs/status counts are emitted. No credentials, event
    # payloads, full errors, evidence, or notification bodies are logged.
    logger.info(json.dumps(
        {
            "component": "source_health_worker",
            "event": event,
            "at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            **fields,
        },
        ensure_ascii=False,
        allow_nan=False,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    ))


async def run_worker() -> None:
    settings = Settings.from_environment()
    poll_seconds = _required_int(
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_POLL_SECONDS",
        minimum=1,
        maximum=86_400,
    )
    max_attempts = _required_int(
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_TRANSITION_MAX_ATTEMPTS",
        minimum=1,
        maximum=1_000,
    )
    batch_size = _required_int(
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_TRANSITION_BATCH_SIZE",
        minimum=1,
        maximum=500,
    )
    lease_seconds = _required_int(
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_TRANSITION_LEASE_SECONDS",
        minimum=1,
        maximum=86_400,
    )
    retry_delay_seconds = _required_int(
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_TRANSITION_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS",
        minimum=0,
        maximum=31_536_000,
    )

    factory_ref = os.environ.get(
        "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_HANDLER_FACTORY", ""
    ).strip()
    module_name, separator, function_name = factory_ref.partition(":")
    if not separator or not module_name.strip() or not function_name.strip():
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_HEALTH_HANDLER_FACTORY must be configured "
            "as a trusted module:function reference"
        )
    # This module reference must come from trusted deployment configuration,
    # never from an event, API request, or user-controlled database field.
    module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
    factory = getattr(module, function_name, None)
    if not callable(factory):
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Configured health-transition handler factory is unavailable"
        )
    handler = factory(settings=settings, database_path=settings.database_path)
    if not callable(getattr(handler, "handle", None)):
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Health-transition handler factory returned an invalid handler"
        )

    health_store = SourceHealthStore(settings.database_path)
    evaluator = SourceHealthEvaluator(
        settings.database_path,
        health_store,
    )

    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    stop = asyncio.Event()

    def request_stop() -> None:
        stop.set()

    for signum in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):
        try:
            loop.add_signal_handler(signum, request_stop)
        except (NotImplementedError, RuntimeError, ValueError):
            # Windows/event-loop fallback. Keep handler minimal.
            try:
                signal.signal(
                    signum,
                    lambda _signal_number, _frame: loop.call_soon_threadsafe(
                        request_stop
                    ),
                )
            except (OSError, ValueError):
                pass

    _log_event(
        "worker_started",
        configured_sources=len(settings.source_tokens),
        poll_seconds=poll_seconds,
    )

    try:
        while not stop.is_set():
            try:
                result = evaluator.evaluate_once(
                    settings.source_tokens,
                    max_attempts=max_attempts,
                )
                _log_event(
                    "evaluation_completed",
                    checked_sources=result.checked_sources,
                    transitions_created=len(result.transitions_created),
                    statuses={
                        source_id: status
                        for source_id, status in sorted(
                            {
                                item.source_id: item.current_status
                                for item in result.transitions_created
                            }.items()
                        )
                    },
                )
            except Exception:
                # Do not log exception details that can include database paths
                # or configuration. A supervisor should observe this event.
                _log_event("evaluation_failed", error_code="evaluation_failed")

            # Drain the durable queue even when a new evaluation failed; an
            # older transition may still be ready for processing.
            try:
                processing = process_pending_transitions(
                    evaluator,
                    handler,
                    batch_size=batch_size,
                    lease_seconds=lease_seconds,
                    retry_delay_seconds=retry_delay_seconds,
                )
                _log_event("transition_processing_completed", **processing)
            except Exception:
                _log_event(
                    "transition_processing_failed",
                    error_code="transition_processing_failed",
                )

            try:
                await asyncio.wait_for(
                    stop.wait(),
                    timeout=poll_seconds,
                )
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                pass
    finally:
        _log_event("worker_stopped")


def main() -> None:
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(message)s")
    try:
        asyncio.run(run_worker())
    except ConfigurationError as exc:
        # Configuration error messages identify a missing variable, not its
        # value. Do not print the complete environment or any token.
        logger.error(json.dumps({
            "component": "source_health_worker",
            "event": "startup_blocked",
            "reason": str(exc),
        }))
        raise SystemExit(2) from None
    except Exception:
        logger.error(json.dumps({
            "component": "source_health_worker",
            "event": "startup_failed",
            "reason": "initialization_failed",
        }))
        raise SystemExit(1) from None


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from server.proactive.escalation_delivery import EscalationDeliveryStore
from server.proactive.health_transition_handler import (
    AuthorizedRecipient,
    HealthPolicyStore,
    HealthPolicyUnavailable,
    HealthTransitionHandler,
    NoAuthorizedRecipient,
    SourceOwner,
)
from server.proactive.outbox import NotificationOutbox
from server.proactive.schemas import EventType, Severity
from server.proactive.source_health_evaluator import HealthTransition
from server.proactive.store import EventStore


class _OwnerRegistry:
    def get_active_owner(self, source_id: str):
        if source_id == "monitor-test":
            return SourceOwner(user_id="owner-test", active=True)
        return None


class _RecipientRegistry:
    def __init__(self, *, allowed: bool = True):
        self.allowed = allowed

    def resolve_recipients(
        self, *, user_id: str, source_id: str,
        incident_id: str, channel: str,
    ):
        if not self.allowed:
            return ()
        return (
            AuthorizedRecipient(
                recipient_id="registered-device-test",
                user_id=user_id,
                channel=channel,
                active=True,
                trusted=True,
                consented_for_channel=True,
            ),
        )


def _transition(
    *,
    previous_status="healthy",
    current_status="stale",
    now=None,
):
    now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    return HealthTransition(
        transition_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
        source_id="monitor-test",
        previous_status=previous_status,
        current_status=current_status,
        observed_at=now.isoformat(),
        interval_seconds=30,
        age_seconds=35.0 if current_status == "stale" else 0.0,
        heartbeat_id="actual-fixture-heartbeat-id",
        heartbeat_sequence=12,
        delivery_state="in_flight",
        attempts=1,
        max_attempts=3,
        available_at=now.isoformat(),
        lease_until=(now + timedelta(seconds=30)).isoformat(),
        last_error_code=None,
    )


def _insert_approved_policy(
    db_path: Path,
    transition: HealthTransition,
    *,
    channel="push",
    severity="high",
    requires_verification=False,
):
    now = datetime.fromisoformat(transition.observed_at).astimezone(timezone.utc)
    valid_from = (now - timedelta(days=1)).isoformat()
    created_at = now.isoformat()

    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as db:
        db.execute(
            """INSERT INTO proactive_health_policies (
                policy_record_id, source_id, transition_to, version,
                severity, score, confidence, recommended_channel,
                requires_verification, rationale, max_attempts,
                approval_reference, approved_by, state,
                valid_from, valid_until, created_at
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'approved', ?, NULL, ?)""",
            (
                f"policy-{transition.current_status}",
                transition.source_id,
                transition.current_status,
                "test-policy-version",
                severity,
                0.8,
                0.95,
                channel,
                int(requires_verification),
                "Test-only approved policy fixture.",
                3,
                "test-approval-reference",
                "test-admin-principal",
                valid_from,
                created_at,
            ),
        )


def _build(tmp_path: Path, transition: HealthTransition, recipients=None):
    db_path = (tmp_path / "health-handler.sqlite3").resolve()
    events = EventStore(db_path)
    NotificationOutbox(db_path)
    decisions = EscalationDeliveryStore(db_path)
    policies = HealthPolicyStore(db_path)

    handler = HealthTransitionHandler(
        database_path=db_path,
        policies=policies,
        ownership=_OwnerRegistry(),
        recipients=recipients or _RecipientRegistry(),
        events=events,
        decisions=decisions,
    )

    return db_path, events, decisions, policies, handler


def test_stale_transition_creates_incident_and_authorized_job(tmp_path: Path):
    transition = _transition()
    db_path, events, decisions, _policies, handler = _build(
        tmp_path, transition
    )
    _insert_approved_policy(db_path, transition)

    handler.handle(transition)

    incidents = events.list_incidents(state=None, limit=10)
    assert len(incidents) == 1
    incident = incidents[0]
    assert incident["state"] == "open"
    assert incident["events"][0]["event_type"] == EventType.MONITORING_SOURCE_HEALTH.value
    assert incident["events"][0]["attributes"]["underlying_subsystem_health_verified"] is False

    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as db:
        rows = db.execute(
            "SELECT job_id FROM proactive_escalation_decision_jobs"
        ).fetchall()

    assert len(rows) == 1
    from server.proactive.outbox import NotificationOutbox
    jobs = NotificationOutbox(db_path).list_jobs(states=("pending",))
    assert len(jobs) == 1
    assert jobs[0].recipient_id == "registered-device-test"
    assert jobs[0].payload == {
        "incident_id": incident["incident_id"],
        "kind": "monitoring_source_health",
        "title": "A monitored source needs attention",
    }


def test_missing_approved_policy_fails_closed(tmp_path: Path):
    transition = _transition()
    _db_path, events, _decisions, _policies, handler = _build(
        tmp_path, transition
    )

    with pytest.raises(HealthPolicyUnavailable):
        handler.handle(transition)

    assert events.list_incidents(state=None, limit=10) == []


def test_contact_decision_without_authorized_recipient_fails_closed(
    tmp_path: Path,
):
    transition = _transition()
    db_path, events, _decisions, _policies, handler = _build(
        tmp_path,
        transition,
        recipients=_RecipientRegistry(allowed=False),
    )
    _insert_approved_policy(db_path, transition)

    with pytest.raises(NoAuthorizedRecipient):
        handler.handle(transition)

    assert len(events.list_incidents(state=None, limit=10)) == 1
    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as db:
        count = db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM proactive_notification_outbox"
        ).fetchone()[0]
    assert count == 0


def test_identical_transition_retry_does_not_duplicate_delivery(
    tmp_path: Path,
):
    transition = _transition()
    db_path, events, _decisions, _policies, handler = _build(
        tmp_path, transition
    )
    _insert_approved_policy(db_path, transition)

    handler.handle(transition)
    handler.handle(transition)

    assert len(events.list_incidents(state=None, limit=10)) == 1
    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as db:
        decision_count = db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM proactive_escalation_decisions"
        ).fetchone()[0]
        job_count = db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM proactive_notification_outbox"
        ).fetchone()[0]
    assert decision_count == 1
    assert job_count == 1


def test_recovery_transition_records_and_resolves_health_incident(
    tmp_path: Path,
):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    stale = _transition(
        previous_status="healthy",
        current_status="stale",
        now=now,
    )
    db_path, events, _decisions, _policies, handler = _build(
        tmp_path, stale
    )
    _insert_approved_policy(db_path, stale, channel="push")
    handler.handle(stale)
    original_incident = events.list_incidents(state=None, limit=10)[0]

    recovered = _transition(
        previous_status="stale",
        current_status="healthy",
        now=now + timedelta(minutes=1),
    )
    _insert_approved_policy(
        db_path,
        recovered,
        channel="record_only",
        severity="info",
    )
    handler.handle(recovered)

    incidents = events.list_incidents(state=None, limit=10)
    matched = [
        item for item in incidents
        if item["incident_id"] == original_incident["incident_id"]
    ][0]
    assert matched["state"] == "resolved"
    assert matched["resolved_at"] is not None
    assert len(matched["events"]) == 2
    assert any(
        item["action"] == "monitoring_source_recovered"
        for item in matched["audit"]
    )

    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as db:
        jobs = db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM proactive_notification_outbox"
        ).fetchone()[0]
    assert jobs == 1  # Recovery only records and resolves; it creates no call.


def test_health_policy_ambiguity_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path):
    transition = _transition()
    db_path, _events, _decisions, policies, handler = _build(
        tmp_path, transition
    )
    _insert_approved_policy(db_path, transition)

    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as db:
        db.execute(
            """INSERT INTO proactive_health_policies (
                policy_record_id, source_id, transition_to, version,
                severity, score, confidence, recommended_channel,
                requires_verification, rationale, max_attempts,
                approval_reference, approved_by, state,
                valid_from, valid_until, created_at
            ) SELECT ?, source_id, transition_to, ?, severity, score,
                confidence, recommended_channel, requires_verification,
                rationale, max_attempts, approval_reference, approved_by,
                state, valid_from, valid_until, created_at

                "test-policy-version-2",
                "policy-stale",
            ),
        )

    from server.proactive.health_transition_handler import HealthPolicyAmbiguous

    with pytest.raises(HealthPolicyAmbiguous):
        handler.handle(transition)


1. New source files

health_policy_admin.py — governed policy publication

Open complete Python source

Requires an independently approved record tied to the exact policy content. Rejects overlapping effective policies and self-approval. Retires policies without deleting their history, so older incidents can still be assessed against the policy that applied at the time.




registry_adapters.py — ownership and recipient authorization

Open complete Python source

Connects the proactive handler to existing source-registration, device-registry and notification-authorization services. Filters revoked, untrusted, inactive or unauthorized devices rather than making up recipients.




health_policy_admin_api.py — policy administration API

Open complete Python source

Provides policy publication and retirement routes. It requires an injected policy-publisher authorization dependency from Aariya's actual authentication system; it does not treat the bootstrap user token as administrator authority.



2. How these components connect
The ownership and recipient adapters are designed to use Aariya's existing services. Their repository interfaces are explicit because your actual identity/device database schema is not present in the files available here. Creating a parallel user or device database would undermine the authorization model.

The integration point in your existing server/main.py will follow this pattern:
from server.proactive.health_policy_admin import (    HealthPolicyAdminService,)from server.proactive.health_policy_admin_api import (    create_health_policy_admin_router,)# These dependencies must come from Aariya's REAL existing services.# Do not create placeholder implementations for production.policy_service = HealthPolicyAdminService(    database_path=existing_database_path,    governance=existing_policy_governance_service,)policy_admin_router = create_health_policy_admin_router(    service=policy_service,    require_policy_publisher=existing_policy_publisher_dependency,)app.include_router(policy_admin_router)



Here, existing_database_path, existing_policy_governance_service and existing_policy_publisher_dependency are integration points to map to your real project—not variables that have already been verified to exist.

The source-health handler uses the same principle:
from server.proactive.registry_adapters import (    AariyaAuthorizedRecipientAdapter,    AariyaSourceOwnershipAdapter,)from server.proactive.health_transition_handler import (    HealthTransitionHandler,)handler = HealthTransitionHandler(    database_path=existing_database_path,    policies=existing_health_policy_store,    ownership=AariyaSourceOwnershipAdapter(        existing_source_registration_repository,    ),    recipients=AariyaAuthorizedRecipientAdapter(        devices=existing_device_registry_repository,        authorization=existing_notification_authorization_service,    ),    events=existing_event_store,    decisions=existing_escalation_delivery_store,)



Each dependency must be the actual application service. The adapters enforce basic checks, but Aariya's source-registration service must establish ownership, and its authorization service must establish current user, incident, device and channel permissions.
3. What was repaired and verified
The available overlay now contains the previously missing dependencies:
- source_health.py — durable heartbeat checkpoints and sequence validation.
- source_health_evaluator.py — persisted health transitions and retryable processing.
- escalation_delivery.py — atomic persistence of escalation decisions and notification jobs.
The new tests are also available individually:
- Policy administration tests
- Registry adapter tests
- Source-health evaluator tests
Run the verification from the overlay root:
python -m py_compile server/proactive/*.py
pytest -q


The observed result is 46 passed. These tests validate the overlay's code paths using isolated test records; they do not certify the live Aariya deployment.
4. The remaining integration boundary
There is still a specific blocker: the source archive available here does not contain Aariya's actual account/session service, source-registration repository, trusted-device registry, channel-consent service or policy approval workflow. I therefore have not claimed that the new adapters are connected to live Aariya data.

The next implementation should map these interfaces to the real services, then test that an unauthorized user cannot publish a policy, access another user's incident, or cause Aariya to contact an unapproved device.


"""Authenticated source heartbeat payload and durable latest-checkpoint store."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sqlite3
from contextlib import closing
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator

from .config import SourceAuthorization


class HeartbeatConflict(RuntimeError):
    """A heartbeat sequence was reused with different content."""


class HeartbeatReplay(RuntimeError):
    """A heartbeat sequence is older than the latest accepted sequence."""


class SourceHeartbeatIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", str_strip_whitespace=True)
    heartbeat_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    sequence: int = Field(strict=True, ge=1)
    observed_at: datetime | None = None

    @field_validator("observed_at")
    @classmethod
    def require_timezone(cls, value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:
        if value is not None and (value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None):
            raise ValueError("observed_at must include a timezone offset")
        return value


class HeartbeatReceipt(BaseModel):
    source_id: str
    sequence: int
    heartbeat_id: str
    received_at: datetime
    duplicate: bool


class SourceHealthOut(BaseModel):
    source_id: str
    status: str
    configured_interval_seconds: int | None
    age_seconds: float | None
    last_received_at: datetime | None
    last_sequence: int | None
    last_heartbeat_id: str | None


def _iso(value: datetime) -> str:
    if value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None:
        raise ValueError("Timestamp must include timezone")
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="microseconds")


def _fingerprint(heartbeat: SourceHeartbeatIn) -> str:
    raw = json.dumps(
        heartbeat.model_dump(mode="json"), sort_keys=True,
        ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False, separators=(",", ":"),
    ).encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


class SourceHealthStore:
    """One persisted latest-heartbeat checkpoint per authenticated source."""

    def __init__(self, database_path: Path):
        if not database_path.is_absolute() or str(database_path) == ":memory:":
            raise ValueError("A file-backed absolute database path is required")
        self.database_path = database_path
        self.database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            db.executescript("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_source_health_latest (
                    source_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    heartbeat_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    sequence INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(sequence >= 1),
                    payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
                    received_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    observed_at TEXT
                );
            """)

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        db = sqlite3.connect(self.database_path, timeout=5, isolation_level=None)
        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        db.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000")
        db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        return db

    def record(self, source_id: str, heartbeat: SourceHeartbeatIn, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> HeartbeatReceipt:
        if not source_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("source_id is required")
        received = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        received_iso = _iso(received)
        observed_iso = _iso(heartbeat.observed_at) if heartbeat.observed_at else None
        fingerprint = _fingerprint(heartbeat)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            try:
                db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
                previous = db.execute(
                    "SELECT * FROM proactive_source_health_latest WHERE source_id=?",
                    (source_id,),
                ).fetchone()
                if previous is not None:
                    old_seq = int(previous["sequence"])
                    if heartbeat.sequence == old_seq:
                        if previous["heartbeat_id"] == heartbeat.heartbeat_id and previous["payload_hash"] == fingerprint:
                            db.execute("COMMIT")
                            return HeartbeatReceipt(
                                source_id=source_id, sequence=heartbeat.sequence,
                                heartbeat_id=heartbeat.heartbeat_id,
                                received_at=datetime.fromisoformat(previous["received_at"]),
                                duplicate=True,
                            )
                        raise HeartbeatConflict("Heartbeat sequence was reused with different content")
                    if heartbeat.sequence < old_seq:
                        raise HeartbeatReplay("Heartbeat sequence is older than the latest accepted sequence")
                    db.execute("""UPDATE proactive_source_health_latest
                        SET heartbeat_id=?, sequence=?, payload_hash=?, received_at=?, observed_at=?
                        WHERE source_id=?""", (
                            heartbeat.heartbeat_id, heartbeat.sequence, fingerprint,
                            received_iso, observed_iso, source_id,
                        ))
                else:
                    db.execute("""INSERT INTO proactive_source_health_latest
                        (source_id, heartbeat_id, sequence, payload_hash, received_at, observed_at)
                        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""", (
                            source_id, heartbeat.heartbeat_id, heartbeat.sequence,
                            fingerprint, received_iso, observed_iso,
                        ))
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return HeartbeatReceipt(
                    source_id=source_id, sequence=heartbeat.sequence,
                    heartbeat_id=heartbeat.heartbeat_id,
                    received_at=received.astimezone(timezone.utc), duplicate=False,
                )
            except Exception:
                if db.in_transaction:
                    db.execute("ROLLBACK")
                raise

    def list_health(self, configured_sources: dict[str, SourceAuthorization] | object, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> list[SourceHealthOut]:
        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if current.tzinfo is None or current.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("now must include a timezone")
        current = current.astimezone(timezone.utc)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            rows = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_source_health_latest").fetchall()
        latest = {row["source_id"]: row for row in rows}
        results: list[SourceHealthOut] = []
        for source_id, rule in sorted(configured_sources.items()):
            row = latest.get(source_id)
            interval = rule.heartbeat_interval_seconds
            if interval is None:
                status = "unconfigured"
                age = None
            elif row is None:
                status = "no_heartbeat"
                age = None
            else:
                last = datetime.fromisoformat(row["received_at"]).astimezone(timezone.utc)
                delta = (current - last).total_seconds()
                if delta < 0:
                    status, age = "clock_anomaly", None
                elif delta <= interval:
                    status, age = "healthy", delta
                else:
                    status, age = "stale", delta
            last_received = datetime.fromisoformat(row["received_at"]) if row else None
            results.append(SourceHealthOut(
                source_id=source_id,
                status=status,
                configured_interval_seconds=interval,
                age_seconds=age,
                last_received_at=last_received,
                last_sequence=int(row["sequence"]) if row else None,
                last_heartbeat_id=row["heartbeat_id"] if row else None,
            ))
        return results



"""Durable health-state transitions and retryable processing queue."""
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import uuid
from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Mapping, Protocol

from .config import SourceAuthorization
from .source_health import SourceHealthStore


class TransitionConflict(RuntimeError):
    pass


class TransitionNotFound(LookupError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class HealthTransition:
    transition_id: str
    source_id: str
    previous_status: str
    current_status: str
    observed_at: str
    interval_seconds: int | None
    age_seconds: float | None
    heartbeat_id: str | None
    heartbeat_sequence: int | None
    delivery_state: str
    attempts: int
    max_attempts: int
    available_at: str
    lease_until: str | None
    last_error_code: str | None

    @classmethod
    def from_row(cls, row: sqlite3.Row) -> "HealthTransition":
        return cls(**{name: row[name] for name in cls.__dataclass_fields__})


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class EvaluationResult:
    checked_at: str
    checked_sources: int
    transitions_created: tuple[HealthTransition, ...]


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def _iso(value: datetime) -> str:
    if value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None:
        raise ValueError("Timestamp must include a timezone")
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="microseconds")


class SourceHealthEvaluator:
    """Evaluate heartbeat checkpoints and persist transitions atomically."""

    def __init__(self, database_path: Path, health_store: SourceHealthStore):
        if not database_path.is_absolute() or str(database_path) == ":memory:":
            raise ValueError("A file-backed absolute database path is required")
        if health_store.database_path.resolve() != database_path.resolve():
            raise ValueError("Heartbeat and evaluator stores must share a database")
        self.database_path = database_path
        self.health_store = health_store
        database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            db.executescript("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_source_health_registration (
                    source_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    first_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    interval_seconds INTEGER NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_source_health_state (
                    source_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    current_status TEXT NOT NULL,
                    last_transition_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_source_health_transitions (
                    transition_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    source_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    previous_status TEXT NOT NULL,
                    current_status TEXT NOT NULL,
                    observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    interval_seconds INTEGER,
                    age_seconds REAL,
                    heartbeat_id TEXT,
                    heartbeat_sequence INTEGER,
                    delivery_state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(delivery_state IN ('pending','in_flight','processed','dead')),
                    attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(attempts >= 0),
                    max_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(max_attempts BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),
                    available_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    lease_until TEXT,
                    last_error_code TEXT,
                    processed_at TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_source_health_transition_due
                    ON proactive_source_health_transitions(delivery_state, available_at, lease_until, created_at);
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_source_health_transition_source
                    ON proactive_source_health_transitions(source_id, observed_at);
            """)

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        db = sqlite3.connect(self.database_path, timeout=5, isolation_level=None)
        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
        db.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000")
        db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        return db

    def evaluate_once(
        self,
        configured_sources: Mapping[str, SourceAuthorization],
        *,
        max_attempts: int,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> EvaluationResult:
        if isinstance(max_attempts, bool) or not 1 <= max_attempts <= 1000:
            raise ValueError("max_attempts must be between 1 and 1000")
        current = now or _now()
        stamp = _iso(current)
        statuses = self.health_store.list_health(configured_sources, now=current)
        created: list[HealthTransition] = []
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            for health in statuses:
                # A newly discovered monitor has one full configured interval
                # to send its first heartbeat. The start time is persisted.
                if health.status == "no_heartbeat" and health.configured_interval_seconds is not None:
                    registration = db.execute(
                        "SELECT * FROM proactive_source_health_registration WHERE source_id=?",
                        (health.source_id,),
                    ).fetchone()
                    if registration is None:
                        db.execute(
                            "INSERT INTO proactive_source_health_registration(source_id,first_seen_at,interval_seconds) VALUES(?,?,?)",
                            (health.source_id, stamp, health.configured_interval_seconds),
                        )
                        continue
                    if int(registration["interval_seconds"]) != health.configured_interval_seconds:
                        db.execute(
                            "UPDATE proactive_source_health_registration SET first_seen_at=?, interval_seconds=? WHERE source_id=?",
                            (stamp, health.configured_interval_seconds, health.source_id),
                        )
                        continue
                    first_seen = datetime.fromisoformat(registration["first_seen_at"])
                    if first_seen.tzinfo is None or first_seen.utcoffset() is None:
                        raise RuntimeError("Stored source registration timestamp lacks timezone")
                    if current.astimezone(timezone.utc) - first_seen.astimezone(timezone.utc) < timedelta(seconds=health.configured_interval_seconds):
                        continue
                else:
                    # If source begins heartbeating, it no longer needs a
                    # no-heartbeat registration grace period.
                    if health.status == "healthy":
                        db.execute(
                            "DELETE FROM proactive_source_health_registration WHERE source_id=?",
                            (health.source_id,),
                        )

                state = db.execute(
                    "SELECT current_status FROM proactive_source_health_state WHERE source_id=?",
                    (health.source_id,),
                ).fetchone()
                previous = state["current_status"] if state else "unknown"
                if previous == health.status:
                    continue

                transition_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
                db.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO proactive_source_health_transitions (
                        transition_id, source_id, previous_status, current_status,
                        observed_at, interval_seconds, age_seconds, heartbeat_id,
                        heartbeat_sequence, delivery_state, attempts, max_attempts,
                        available_at, lease_until, last_error_code, processed_at, created_at
                    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending', 0, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?)""",
                    (
                        transition_id, health.source_id, previous, health.status,
                        stamp, health.configured_interval_seconds, health.age_seconds,
                        health.last_heartbeat_id, health.last_sequence,
                        max_attempts, stamp, stamp,
                    ),
                )
                db.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO proactive_source_health_state(
                        source_id,current_status,last_transition_id,updated_at
                    ) VALUES(?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(source_id) DO UPDATE SET
                        current_status=excluded.current_status,
                        last_transition_id=excluded.last_transition_id,
                        updated_at=excluded.updated_at""",
                    (health.source_id, health.status, transition_id, stamp),
                )
                row = db.execute(
                    "SELECT * FROM proactive_source_health_transitions WHERE transition_id=?",
                    (transition_id,),
                ).fetchone()
                created.append(HealthTransition.from_row(row))
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return EvaluationResult(stamp, len(statuses), tuple(created))
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def claim_pending(
        self, *, limit: int, lease_seconds: int, now: datetime | None = None
    ) -> tuple[HealthTransition, ...]:
        if not 1 <= limit <= 500:
            raise ValueError("limit must be between 1 and 500")
        if not 1 <= lease_seconds <= 86_400:
            raise ValueError("lease_seconds must be between 1 and 86400")
        current = now or _now()
        stamp = _iso(current)
        lease = _iso(current + timedelta(seconds=lease_seconds))
        db = self._connect()
        claims: list[HealthTransition] = []
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            db.execute(
                """UPDATE proactive_source_health_transitions
                SET delivery_state='dead', lease_until=NULL,
                    last_error_code='lease_expired_after_max_attempts'
                WHERE delivery_state='in_flight' AND lease_until<=?
                  AND attempts>=max_attempts""",
                (stamp,),
            )
            rows = db.execute(
                """SELECT * FROM proactive_source_health_transitions
                WHERE attempts < max_attempts AND available_at<=?
                  AND (delivery_state='pending' OR
                    (delivery_state='in_flight' AND lease_until<=?))
                ORDER BY available_at, created_at, transition_id LIMIT ?""",
                (stamp, stamp, limit),
            ).fetchall()
            for row in rows:
                attempt = int(row["attempts"]) + 1
                db.execute(
                    "UPDATE proactive_source_health_transitions SET delivery_state='in_flight', attempts=?, lease_until=?, last_error_code=NULL WHERE transition_id=?",
                    (attempt, lease, row["transition_id"]),
                )
                updated = db.execute(
                    "SELECT * FROM proactive_source_health_transitions WHERE transition_id=?",
                    (row["transition_id"],),
                ).fetchone()
                claims.append(HealthTransition.from_row(updated))
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return tuple(claims)
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def mark_processed(
        self, *, transition_id: str, expected_attempt: int, now: datetime | None = None
    ) -> None:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            try:
                cur = db.execute(
                    """UPDATE proactive_source_health_transitions
                    SET delivery_state='processed', lease_until=NULL,
                        processed_at=?, last_error_code=NULL
                    WHERE transition_id=? AND delivery_state='in_flight' AND attempts=?""",
                    (_iso(now or _now()), transition_id, expected_attempt),
                )
                if cur.rowcount != 1:
                    raise TransitionConflict("Transition is not claimed by this attempt")
                db.execute("COMMIT")
            except Exception:
                if db.in_transaction:
                    db.execute("ROLLBACK")
                raise

    def mark_failed(
        self, *, transition_id: str, expected_attempt: int, error_code: str,
        retryable: bool, retry_delay_seconds: int, now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        if not error_code.strip() or len(error_code) > 120 or any(ord(c) < 32 for c in error_code):
            raise ValueError("error_code must be a safe code up to 120 characters")
        if not 0 <= retry_delay_seconds <= 31_536_000:
            raise ValueError("retry_delay_seconds must be in 0..31536000")
        current = now or _now()
        stamp = _iso(current)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            try:
                row = db.execute(
                    "SELECT * FROM proactive_source_health_transitions WHERE transition_id=?",
                    (transition_id,),
                ).fetchone()
                if row is None:
                    raise TransitionNotFound(transition_id)
                if row["delivery_state"] != "in_flight" or int(row["attempts"]) != expected_attempt:
                    raise TransitionConflict("Transition is not claimed by this attempt")
                will_retry = retryable and int(row["attempts"]) < int(row["max_attempts"])
                state = "pending" if will_retry else "dead"
                available = _iso(current + timedelta(seconds=retry_delay_seconds)) if will_retry else row["available_at"]
                db.execute(
                    "UPDATE proactive_source_health_transitions SET delivery_state=?, available_at=?, lease_until=NULL, last_error_code=? WHERE transition_id=?",
                    (state, available, error_code, transition_id),
                )
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return state
            except Exception:
                if db.in_transaction:
                    db.execute("ROLLBACK")
                raise

    def list_transitions(
        self, *, limit: int = 100, delivery_state: str | None = None
    ) -> tuple[HealthTransition, ...]:
        if not 1 <= limit <= 500:
            raise ValueError("limit must be between 1 and 500")
        valid = {"pending", "in_flight", "processed", "dead"}
        if delivery_state is not None and delivery_state not in valid:
            raise ValueError("Unsupported delivery state")
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            if delivery_state is None:
                rows = db.execute(
                    "SELECT * FROM proactive_source_health_transitions ORDER BY observed_at DESC, transition_id DESC LIMIT ?",
                    (limit,),
                ).fetchall()
            else:
                rows = db.execute(
                    "SELECT * FROM proactive_source_health_transitions WHERE delivery_state=? ORDER BY observed_at, transition_id LIMIT ?",
                    (delivery_state, limit),
                ).fetchall()
        return tuple(HealthTransition.from_row(row) for row in rows)


class HealthTransitionHandler(Protocol):
    def handle(self, transition: HealthTransition) -> None:
        ...


def process_pending_transitions(
    evaluator: SourceHealthEvaluator,
    handler: HealthTransitionHandler,
    *, batch_size: int,
    lease_seconds: int,
    retry_delay_seconds: int,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    claims = evaluator.claim_pending(limit=batch_size, lease_seconds=lease_seconds, now=now)
    counts = {"claimed": len(claims), "processed": 0, "retrying": 0, "dead": 0}
    for item in claims:
        try:
            handler.handle(item)
        except Exception:
            state = evaluator.mark_failed(
                transition_id=item.transition_id,
                expected_attempt=item.attempts,
                error_code="policy_or_delivery_handler_failed",
                retryable=True,
                retry_delay_seconds=retry_delay_seconds,
                now=now,
            )
            counts["retrying" if state == "pending" else "dead"] += 1
        else:
            evaluator.mark_processed(
                transition_id=item.transition_id,
                expected_attempt=item.attempts,
                now=now,
            )
            counts["processed"] += 1
    return counts


"""Atomic persistence of policy decisions and durable notification jobs.

No provider, recipient, policy, or credential is configured by this module.
SQLite implementation is for a single-host deployment and must be replaced
or adapted to the application's shared transactional database when scaling.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import math
import sqlite3
import uuid
from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

CONTACT_CHANNELS = frozenset({"message", "push", "call", "sms"})
NON_CONTACT_CHANNELS = frozenset({"record_only", "review_required", "suppressed"})
_ALLOWED_CHANNELS = CONTACT_CHANNELS | NON_CONTACT_CHANNELS


class DecisionConflict(RuntimeError):
    """A decision idempotency key was reused with different content."""


class DecisionIncidentNotFound(LookupError):
    pass


class DecisionValidationError(ValueError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DeliveryIntent:
    channel: str
    recipient_id: str
    payload: dict[str, Any]
    max_attempts: int
    available_at: datetime


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class EscalationDecision:
    decision_key: str
    incident_id: str
    user_id: str
    policy_record_id: str
    policy_version: str
    trigger_id: str
    score: float
    confidence: float
    recommended_channel: str
    rationale: str
    requires_verification: bool
    intents: Sequence[DeliveryIntent]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PersistResult:
    decision_id: str
    status: str
    job_ids: tuple[str, ...]
    duplicate: bool


def make_decision_key(
    *, incident_id: str, trigger_id: str,
    policy_record_id: str, policy_version: str,
) -> str:
    required = (incident_id, trigger_id, policy_record_id, policy_version)
    if any(not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip() or len(value) > 512 for value in required):
        raise DecisionValidationError("Incident, trigger, and policy identifiers are required")
    raw = json.dumps(required, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


def _iso(value: datetime) -> str:
    if value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None:
        raise DecisionValidationError("Timestamps must include a timezone")
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="microseconds")


def _json(value: Any) -> str:
    try:
        encoded = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise DecisionValidationError("Decision payload must be finite JSON-compatible data") from exc
    if len(encoded.encode("utf-8")) > 16_384:
        raise DecisionValidationError("Each notification payload must not exceed 16 KiB")
    return encoded


def _validate(decision: EscalationDecision) -> tuple[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    for field, value, maximum in (
        ("decision_key", decision.decision_key, 512),
        ("incident_id", decision.incident_id, 512),
        ("user_id", decision.user_id, 256),
        ("policy_record_id", decision.policy_record_id, 512),
        ("policy_version", decision.policy_version, 120),
        ("trigger_id", decision.trigger_id, 512),
        ("rationale", decision.rationale, 4000),
    ):
        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip() or len(value) > maximum:
            raise DecisionValidationError(f"{field} is required and length-limited")

    for name, value in (("score", decision.score), ("confidence", decision.confidence)):
        if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)) or not math.isfinite(value) or not 0 <= value <= 1:
            raise DecisionValidationError(f"{name} must be finite and between 0 and 1")

    if decision.recommended_channel not in _ALLOWED_CHANNELS:
        raise DecisionValidationError("Unsupported escalation channel")

    normalized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen_targets: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
    for intent in decision.intents:
        if intent.channel not in CONTACT_CHANNELS:
            raise DecisionValidationError("A delivery intent requires a contact channel")
        if not isinstance(intent.recipient_id, str) or not intent.recipient_id.strip() or len(intent.recipient_id) > 256:
            raise DecisionValidationError("A valid recipient ID is required")
        if not isinstance(intent.payload, dict):
            raise DecisionValidationError("Notification payload must be an object")
        if intent.payload.get("incident_id") != decision.incident_id:
            raise DecisionValidationError("Notification payload incident ID mismatch")
        if not 1 <= intent.max_attempts <= 1000:
            raise DecisionValidationError("max_attempts must be between 1 and 1000")
        target = (intent.channel, intent.recipient_id)
        if target in seen_targets:
            raise DecisionValidationError("Duplicate channel/recipient intent")
        seen_targets.add(target)
        normalized.append({
            "channel": intent.channel,
            "recipient_id": intent.recipient_id,
            "payload_json": _json(intent.payload),
            "max_attempts": intent.max_attempts,
            "available_at": _iso(intent.available_at),
        })

    if decision.requires_verification and normalized:
        raise DecisionValidationError("A verification-required decision cannot enqueue contact")
    if decision.recommended_channel in NON_CONTACT_CHANNELS and normalized:
        raise DecisionValidationError("A non-contact decision cannot contain delivery intents")
    if decision.recommended_channel in CONTACT_CHANNELS and not decision.requires_verification:
        if not normalized:
            raise DecisionValidationError("A contact decision requires an authorized target")
        if any(item["channel"] != decision.recommended_channel for item in normalized):
            raise DecisionValidationError("Intent channel must match the policy channel")
    if decision.recommended_channel == "review_required" and not decision.requires_verification:
        raise DecisionValidationError("review_required must require verification")

    fingerprint_payload = {
        "incident_id": decision.incident_id,
        "user_id": decision.user_id,
        "policy_record_id": decision.policy_record_id,
        "policy_version": decision.policy_version,
        "trigger_id": decision.trigger_id,
        "score": float(decision.score),
        "confidence": float(decision.confidence),
        "recommended_channel": decision.recommended_channel,
        "rationale": decision.rationale,
        "requires_verification": decision.requires_verification,
        "intents": normalized,
    }
    fingerprint = hashlib.sha256(_json(fingerprint_payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    return fingerprint, normalized


class EscalationDeliveryStore:
    """Atomically save escalation decisions and outbox jobs to one SQLite DB."""

    def __init__(self, database_path: Path):
        if not database_path.is_absolute() or str(database_path) == ":memory:":
