            raise ValueError("A file-backed absolute database path is required")
        self.database_path = database_path
        self.database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            existing_tables = {r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
            required = {"proactive_incidents", "proactive_notification_outbox"}
            if not required.issubset(existing_tables):
                raise RuntimeError("Initialize EventStore and NotificationOutbox using this same database first")
            db.executescript("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_escalation_decisions (
                    decision_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    decision_key TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                    decision_fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL,
                    incident_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES proactive_incidents(incident_id),
                    user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    policy_record_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    policy_version TEXT NOT NULL,
                    trigger_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    score REAL NOT NULL CHECK(score >= 0 AND score <= 1),
                    confidence REAL NOT NULL CHECK(confidence >= 0 AND confidence <= 1),
                    recommended_channel TEXT NOT NULL,
                    rationale TEXT NOT NULL,
                    requires_verification INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(requires_verification IN (0,1)),
                    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('queued','recorded','review_required','suppressed')),
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_escalation_decision_jobs (
                    decision_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES proactive_escalation_decisions(decision_id),
                    job_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES proactive_notification_outbox(job_id),
                    PRIMARY KEY(decision_id, job_id),
                    UNIQUE(job_id)
                );
            """)

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        db = sqlite3.connect(self.database_path, timeout=5, isolation_level=None)
        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
        db.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000")
        db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        return db

    def persist_and_enqueue(self, decision: EscalationDecision) -> PersistResult:
        fingerprint, intents = _validate(decision)
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            existing = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM proactive_escalation_decisions WHERE decision_key=?",
                (decision.decision_key,),
            ).fetchone()
            if existing is not None:
                if existing["decision_fingerprint"] != fingerprint:
                    raise DecisionConflict("Decision key was reused with different content")
                jobs = db.execute(
                    "SELECT job_id FROM proactive_escalation_decision_jobs WHERE decision_id=? ORDER BY job_id",
                    (existing["decision_id"],),
                ).fetchall()
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return PersistResult(existing["decision_id"], existing["status"], tuple(r["job_id"] for r in jobs), True)

            incident = db.execute(
                "SELECT state FROM proactive_incidents WHERE incident_id=?",
                (decision.incident_id,),
            ).fetchone()
            if incident is None:
                raise DecisionIncidentNotFound(decision.incident_id)

            if incident["state"] == "resolved":
                status, intents = "suppressed", []
            elif decision.requires_verification or decision.recommended_channel == "review_required":
                status, intents = "review_required", []
            elif decision.recommended_channel == "record_only":
                status, intents = "recorded", []
            elif decision.recommended_channel == "suppressed":
                status, intents = "suppressed", []
            else:
                status = "queued"

            decision_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            now = _iso(datetime.now(timezone.utc))
            db.execute(
                """INSERT INTO proactive_escalation_decisions (
                    decision_id, decision_key, decision_fingerprint, incident_id,
                    user_id, policy_record_id, policy_version, trigger_id, score,
                    confidence, recommended_channel, rationale, requires_verification,
                    status, created_at
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                (decision_id, decision.decision_key, fingerprint, decision.incident_id,
                 decision.user_id, decision.policy_record_id, decision.policy_version,
                 decision.trigger_id, float(decision.score), float(decision.confidence),
                 decision.recommended_channel, decision.rationale,
                 int(decision.requires_verification), status, now),
            )

            job_ids: list[str] = []
            for intent in intents:
                dedupe_raw = json.dumps(
                    [decision.decision_key, intent["channel"], intent["recipient_id"]],
                    ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"),
                ).encode("utf-8")
                dedupe_key = hashlib.sha256(dedupe_raw).hexdigest()
                existing_job = db.execute(
                    "SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE dedupe_key=?",
                    (dedupe_key,),
                ).fetchone()
                expected = (
                    decision.incident_id, intent["channel"], intent["recipient_id"],
                    intent["payload_json"], intent["max_attempts"], intent["available_at"],
                )
                if existing_job is not None:
                    actual = (
                        existing_job["incident_id"], existing_job["channel"],
                        existing_job["recipient_id"], existing_job["payload_json"],
                        existing_job["max_attempts"], existing_job["available_at"],
                    )
                    if actual != expected:
                        raise DecisionConflict("Existing outbox job conflicts with this decision")
                    job_id = existing_job["job_id"]
                else:
                    job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
                    db.execute(
                        """INSERT INTO proactive_notification_outbox (
                            job_id, dedupe_key, incident_id, channel, recipient_id,
                            payload_json, state, attempts, max_attempts, available_at,
                            lease_until, provider_message_id, last_error_code,
                            created_at, updated_at
                        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending', 0, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?, ?)""",
                        (job_id, dedupe_key, decision.incident_id, intent["channel"],
                         intent["recipient_id"], intent["payload_json"], intent["max_attempts"],
                         intent["available_at"], now, now),
                    )
                db.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO proactive_escalation_decision_jobs(decision_id,job_id) VALUES(?,?)",
                    (decision_id, job_id),
                )
                job_ids.append(job_id)
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return PersistResult(decision_id, status, tuple(job_ids), False)
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()


from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from server.proactive.health_policy_admin import (
    HealthPolicyAdminService,
    HealthPolicyDraft,
    PolicyAdministrationDenied,
    PolicyApprovalUnavailable,
    PolicyChangeApproval,
    PolicyGovernance,
    PolicyOverlapConflict,
    policy_digest,
    policy_retirement_digest,
)
from server.proactive.health_transition_handler import HealthPolicyStore
from server.proactive.schemas import Severity
from server.proactive.store import EventStore
from server.proactive.outbox import NotificationOutbox


class _Governance:
    def __init__(self):
        self.approvals = {}

    def assert_can_publish(self, *, actor_id: str, source_id: str) -> None:
        if actor_id != "publisher" or source_id != "monitor-a":
            raise PolicyAdministrationDenied("not authorized to publish")

    def assert_can_approve(self, *, actor_id: str, source_id: str) -> None:
        if actor_id != "independent-approver" or source_id != "monitor-a":
            raise PolicyAdministrationDenied("not authorized to approve")

    def get_approval(self, *, approval_reference: str):
        return self.approvals.get(approval_reference)

    def approve(self, reference: str, digest: str, now: datetime):
        self.approvals[reference] = PolicyChangeApproval(
            approval_reference=reference,
            approved_by="independent-approver",
            policy_digest=digest,
            approved_at=now - timedelta(seconds=1),
            expires_at=now + timedelta(hours=1),
            revoked_at=None,
            state="approved",
        )


def _draft(now: datetime) -> HealthPolicyDraft:
    return HealthPolicyDraft(
        source_id="monitor-a",
        transition_to="stale",
        version="v1",
        severity=Severity.HIGH,
        score=0.8,
        confidence=0.95,
        recommended_channel="push",
        requires_verification=False,
        rationale="Approved policy test fixture.",
        max_attempts=3,
        valid_from=now - timedelta(minutes=1),
        valid_until=now + timedelta(days=1),
    )


def _build(tmp_path: Path):
    path = (tmp_path / "policy-admin.sqlite3").resolve()
    EventStore(path)
    NotificationOutbox(path)
    governance = _Governance()
    service = HealthPolicyAdminService(path, governance)
    return path, governance, service


def test_publish_requires_external_exact_content_approval(tmp_path: Path):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    path, governance, service = _build(tmp_path)
    draft = _draft(now)
    governance.approve("approval-1", policy_digest(draft), now)

    policy_id = service.publish(
        draft,
        actor_id_from_authenticated_session="publisher",
        approval_reference="approval-1",
        now=now,
    )

    resolved = HealthPolicyStore(path).get_for_transition(
        source_id="monitor-a",
        transition_to="stale",
        observed_at=now,
    )
    assert resolved.policy_record_id == policy_id
    assert resolved.version == "v1"


def test_policy_change_after_approval_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    _path, governance, service = _build(tmp_path)
    draft = _draft(now)
    governance.approve("approval-1", policy_digest(draft), now)
    changed = replace(draft, score=0.7)

    with pytest.raises(PolicyApprovalUnavailable):
        service.publish(
            changed,
            actor_id_from_authenticated_session="publisher",
            approval_reference="approval-1",
            now=now,
        )


def test_overlapping_approved_policy_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    _path, governance, service = _build(tmp_path)
    first = _draft(now)
    governance.approve("approval-1", policy_digest(first), now)
    service.publish(
        first,
        actor_id_from_authenticated_session="publisher",
        approval_reference="approval-1",
        now=now,
    )

    second = replace(first, version="v2", score=0.7)
    governance.approve("approval-2", policy_digest(second), now)

    with pytest.raises(PolicyOverlapConflict):
        service.publish(
            second,
            actor_id_from_authenticated_session="publisher",
            approval_reference="approval-2",
            now=now,
        )


def test_publisher_cannot_approve_own_policy(tmp_path: Path):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    _path, governance, service = _build(tmp_path)
    draft = _draft(now)
    governance.approvals["self-approval"] = PolicyChangeApproval(
        approval_reference="self-approval",
        approved_by="publisher",
        policy_digest=policy_digest(draft),
        approved_at=now - timedelta(seconds=1),
        expires_at=now + timedelta(hours=1),
        revoked_at=None,
        state="approved",
    )

    with pytest.raises(PolicyAdministrationDenied):
        service.publish(
            draft,
            actor_id_from_authenticated_session="publisher",
            approval_reference="self-approval",
            now=now,
        )


def test_retired_policy_remains_resolvable_for_historical_event(tmp_path: Path):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    path, governance, service = _build(tmp_path)
    draft = _draft(now)
    governance.approve("approval-1", policy_digest(draft), now)
    policy_id = service.publish(
        draft,
        actor_id_from_authenticated_session="publisher",
        approval_reference="approval-1",
        now=now,
    )

    reason = "Policy replaced under an approved policy change."
    digest = policy_retirement_digest(
        policy_record_id=policy_id,
        source_id=draft.source_id,
        version=draft.version,
        reason=reason,
    )
    governance.approve("retire-approval", digest, now)
    service.retire(
        policy_record_id=policy_id,
        actor_id_from_authenticated_session="publisher",
        approval_reference="retire-approval",
        reason=reason,
        now=now + timedelta(minutes=1),
    )

    reader = HealthPolicyStore(path)
    # An event timestamp before retirement still resolves its historical policy.
    policy = reader.get_for_transition(
        source_id="monitor-a",
        transition_to="stale",
        observed_at=now,
    )
    assert policy.policy_record_id == policy_id

    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        reader.get_for_transition(
            source_id="monitor-a",
            transition_to="stale",
            observed_at=now + timedelta(minutes=2),
        )


from datetime import datetime, timezone

from server.proactive.registry_adapters import (
    AariyaAuthorizedRecipientAdapter,
    AariyaSourceOwnershipAdapter,
    DeviceNotificationRecord,
    SourceRegistrationRecord,
)


class _SourceRepository:
    def find_source_registration(self, *, source_id: str):
        if source_id == "source-a":
            return SourceRegistrationRecord(
                source_id="source-a", owner_user_id="user-a", active=True
            )
        return None


class _DeviceRepository:
    def list_devices_for_user(self, *, authenticated_user_id: str):
        if authenticated_user_id != "user-a":
            return ()
        return (
            DeviceNotificationRecord(
                device_id="device-a", user_id="user-a", active=True,
                trusted=True, revoked_at=None,
                permitted_channels=frozenset({"push"}),
            ),
            DeviceNotificationRecord(
                device_id="revoked-device", user_id="user-a", active=True,
                trusted=True, revoked_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
                permitted_channels=frozenset({"push"}),
            ),
        )


class _AuthorizationRepository:
    def may_notify_device(self, *, user_id, device_id, source_id, incident_id, channel):
        return user_id == "user-a" and device_id == "device-a" and source_id == "source-a" and channel == "push"


def test_source_owner_comes_from_repository():
    owner = AariyaSourceOwnershipAdapter(_SourceRepository()).get_active_owner("source-a")
    assert owner is not None
    assert owner.user_id == "user-a"


def test_recipient_adapter_filters_revoked_and_unauthorized_devices():
    adapter = AariyaAuthorizedRecipientAdapter(
        devices=_DeviceRepository(), authorization=_AuthorizationRepository()
    )
    recipients = adapter.resolve_recipients(
        user_id="user-a", source_id="source-a",
        incident_id="incident-a", channel="push",
    )
    assert [recipient.recipient_id for recipient in recipients] == ["device-a"]


"""Governed, versioned publication of source-health escalation policies.

This module does not authenticate web requests itself. The caller must supply
an actor ID from Aariya's authenticated session and a governance adapter backed
by the existing admin/approval system. It never seeds default policies.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import math
import sqlite3
import uuid
from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Protocol

from .schemas import Severity
from .health_transition_handler import HealthPolicyStore
from .escalation_delivery import CONTACT_CHANNELS, NON_CONTACT_CHANNELS


class PolicyAdministrationDenied(PermissionError):
    pass


class PolicyApprovalUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    pass


class PolicyOverlapConflict(RuntimeError):
    pass


class PolicyNotFound(LookupError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class HealthPolicyDraft:
    source_id: str
    transition_to: str
    version: str
    severity: Severity
    score: float
    confidence: float
    recommended_channel: str
    requires_verification: bool
    rationale: str
    max_attempts: int
    valid_from: datetime
    valid_until: datetime | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PolicyChangeApproval:
    """Approval facts returned by the trusted existing approval service."""
    approval_reference: str
    approved_by: str
    policy_digest: str
    approved_at: datetime
    expires_at: datetime
    revoked_at: datetime | None
    state: str


class PolicyGovernance(Protocol):
    def assert_can_publish(self, *, actor_id: str, source_id: str) -> None:
        """Raise unless actor has policy-publisher authority in the real IdP."""
        ...

    def assert_can_approve(self, *, actor_id: str, source_id: str) -> None:
        """Raise unless approver has independent policy-approver authority."""
        ...

    def get_approval(self, *, approval_reference: str) -> PolicyChangeApproval | None:
        """Read a real approval record from the trusted approval system."""
        ...


def _iso(value: datetime) -> str:
    if value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None:
        raise ValueError("Policy timestamps must include a timezone")
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="microseconds")


def policy_digest(draft: HealthPolicyDraft) -> str:
    """Canonical digest approval must cover the full policy effect."""
    body = {
        "source_id": draft.source_id,
        "transition_to": draft.transition_to,
        "version": draft.version,
        "severity": draft.severity.value,
        "score": float(draft.score),
        "confidence": float(draft.confidence),
        "recommended_channel": draft.recommended_channel,
        "requires_verification": bool(draft.requires_verification),
        "rationale": draft.rationale,
        "max_attempts": int(draft.max_attempts),
        "valid_from": _iso(draft.valid_from),
        "valid_until": _iso(draft.valid_until) if draft.valid_until else None,
    }
    raw = json.dumps(
        body,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        allow_nan=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    ).encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


def _validate_draft(draft: HealthPolicyDraft) -> None:
    for name, value, max_length in (
        ("source_id", draft.source_id, 160),
        ("transition_to", draft.transition_to, 80),
        ("version", draft.version, 120),
        ("rationale", draft.rationale, 4000),
    ):
        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip() or len(value) > max_length:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} is required and must be length-limited")

    if draft.recommended_channel not in CONTACT_CHANNELS | NON_CONTACT_CHANNELS:
        raise ValueError("Unsupported policy channel")
    if (
        isinstance(draft.score, bool) or isinstance(draft.confidence, bool)
        or not isinstance(draft.score, (int, float))
        or not isinstance(draft.confidence, (int, float))
        or not math.isfinite(draft.score) or not math.isfinite(draft.confidence)
        or not 0 <= draft.score <= 1 or not 0 <= draft.confidence <= 1
    ):
        raise ValueError("score and confidence must be finite numbers between 0 and 1")
    if not 1 <= draft.max_attempts <= 1000:
        raise ValueError("max_attempts must be between 1 and 1000")
    if draft.valid_from.tzinfo is None or draft.valid_from.utcoffset() is None:
        raise ValueError("valid_from must include a timezone")
    if draft.valid_until is not None:
        if draft.valid_until.tzinfo is None or draft.valid_until.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("valid_until must include a timezone")
        if draft.valid_until.astimezone(timezone.utc) <= draft.valid_from.astimezone(timezone.utc):
            raise ValueError("valid_until must be later than valid_from")

    # Recovery should not trigger a contact. The source-health handler also
    # enforces this, but reject the unsafe policy at publication time too.
    if draft.transition_to == "healthy":
        if draft.recommended_channel != "record_only" or draft.requires_verification:
            raise ValueError("healthy transition policy must be record_only and not require verification")

    if draft.requires_verification and draft.recommended_channel in CONTACT_CHANNELS:
        # Contact can be permitted by a separate review policy, but this starter
        # refuses contact until verification rather than silently bypassing it.
        raise ValueError("verification-required policy cannot contact directly")

    if draft.recommended_channel == "review_required" and not draft.requires_verification:
        raise ValueError("review_required policy must require verification")


def policy_retirement_digest(
    *, policy_record_id: str, source_id: str, version: str, reason: str
) -> str:
    if any(not value.strip() for value in (policy_record_id, source_id, version, reason)):
        raise ValueError("Retirement digest fields are required")
    raw = json.dumps(
        {"action": "retire", "policy_record_id": policy_record_id,
         "source_id": source_id, "version": version, "reason": reason},
        sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"),
    ).encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


class HealthPolicyAdminService:
    def __init__(self, database_path: Path, governance: PolicyGovernance):
        if not database_path.is_absolute() or str(database_path) == ":memory:":
            raise ValueError("A file-backed absolute database path is required")
        self.database_path = database_path
        self.governance = governance
        # Ensures the resolver table exists, but does not seed any row.
        self.policy_reader = HealthPolicyStore(database_path)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            db.executescript("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_health_policy_audit (
                    audit_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    policy_record_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    action TEXT NOT NULL,
                    actor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    approval_reference TEXT,
                    policy_digest TEXT NOT NULL,
                    metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                    occurred_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_health_policy_audit_policy
                    ON proactive_health_policy_audit(policy_record_id, occurred_at);
                CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS trg_health_policy_audit_no_update
                    BEFORE UPDATE ON proactive_health_policy_audit
                    BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'policy audit is append-only'); END;
                CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS trg_health_policy_audit_no_delete
                    BEFORE DELETE ON proactive_health_policy_audit
                    BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'policy audit is append-only'); END;
            """)

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        db = sqlite3.connect(self.database_path, timeout=5, isolation_level=None)
        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        db.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000")
        db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
        db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        return db

    @staticmethod
    def _check_approval(
        draft: HealthPolicyDraft,
        approval: PolicyChangeApproval | None,
        *,
        actor_id: str,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> PolicyChangeApproval:
        if approval is None:
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("A separate approved policy-change record is required")
        if approval.approval_reference == "":
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Approval reference is missing")
        if approval.state != "approved" or approval.revoked_at is not None:
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Policy approval is not currently valid")
        if not approval.approval_reference.strip() or not approval.approved_by.strip():
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Approval provenance is incomplete")
        if approval.approved_by == actor_id:
            raise PolicyAdministrationDenied("Publisher cannot approve their own policy change")
        if approval.policy_digest != policy_digest(draft):
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Approval does not match the exact policy content")
        for label, timestamp in (("approved_at", approval.approved_at), ("expires_at", approval.expires_at)):
            if timestamp.tzinfo is None or timestamp.utcoffset() is None:
                raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable(f"Approval {label} must be timezone-aware")
        if approval.approved_at.astimezone(timezone.utc) > now:
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Approval timestamp is in the future")
        if approval.expires_at.astimezone(timezone.utc) <= now:
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Approval has expired")
        if approval.revoked_at is not None:
            if approval.revoked_at.tzinfo is None or approval.revoked_at.utcoffset() is None:
                raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Revocation timestamp is invalid")
            if approval.revoked_at.astimezone(timezone.utc) <= now:
                raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Approval has been revoked")
        return approval

    def publish(
        self,
        draft: HealthPolicyDraft,
        *,
        actor_id_from_authenticated_session: str,
        approval_reference: str,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Publish only after authorization and an independent exact-content approval."""
        _validate_draft(draft)
        actor_id = actor_id_from_authenticated_session.strip()
        if not actor_id:
            raise PolicyAdministrationDenied("Authenticated publisher identity is required")
        if not approval_reference.strip():
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("approval_reference is required")

        self.governance.assert_can_publish(actor_id=actor_id, source_id=draft.source_id)
        approval = self.governance.get_approval(approval_reference=approval_reference)
        if approval is not None and approval.approval_reference != approval_reference:
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Approval service returned a different approval reference")
        current_input = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if current_input.tzinfo is None or current_input.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("now must include a timezone")
        current = current_input.astimezone(timezone.utc)
        checked = self._check_approval(draft, approval, actor_id=actor_id, now=current)
        self.governance.assert_can_approve(actor_id=checked.approved_by, source_id=draft.source_id)

        digest = policy_digest(draft)
        policy_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        created_at = _iso(current)
        valid_from = _iso(draft.valid_from)
        valid_until = _iso(draft.valid_until) if draft.valid_until else None
        metadata = json.dumps({
            "source_id": draft.source_id,
            "transition_to": draft.transition_to,
            "version": draft.version,
            "valid_from": valid_from,
            "valid_until": valid_until,
        }, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))

        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            try:
                db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
                all_versions = db.execute(
                    """SELECT version FROM proactive_health_policies
                    WHERE source_id=? AND transition_to=?""",
                    (draft.source_id, draft.transition_to),
                ).fetchall()
                if any(row["version"] == draft.version for row in all_versions):
                    raise PolicyOverlapConflict("Policy version already exists for this source transition")

                candidates = db.execute(
                    """SELECT * FROM proactive_health_policies
                    WHERE source_id=? AND transition_to=? AND state='approved'""",
                    (draft.source_id, draft.transition_to),
                ).fetchall()
                start = draft.valid_from.astimezone(timezone.utc)
                end = draft.valid_until.astimezone(timezone.utc) if draft.valid_until else None
                for row in candidates:
                    old_start = datetime.fromisoformat(row["valid_from"]).astimezone(timezone.utc)
                    old_end = datetime.fromisoformat(row["valid_until"]).astimezone(timezone.utc) if row["valid_until"] else None
                    overlaps = (end is None or old_start < end) and (old_end is None or start < old_end)
                    if overlaps:
                        raise PolicyOverlapConflict(
                            "An approved policy already covers an overlapping effective interval"
                        )

                db.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO proactive_health_policies (
                        policy_record_id, source_id, transition_to, version,
                        severity, score, confidence, recommended_channel,
                        requires_verification, rationale, max_attempts,
                        approval_reference, approved_by, state, valid_from,
                        valid_until, created_at
                    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'approved', ?, ?, ?)""",
                    (
                        policy_id, draft.source_id, draft.transition_to, draft.version,
                        draft.severity.value, float(draft.score), float(draft.confidence),
                        draft.recommended_channel, int(draft.requires_verification),
                        draft.rationale, draft.max_attempts, checked.approval_reference,
                        checked.approved_by, valid_from, valid_until, created_at,
                    ),
                )
                db.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO proactive_health_policy_audit (
                        audit_id, policy_record_id, action, actor_id,
                        approval_reference, policy_digest, metadata_json, occurred_at
                    ) VALUES (?, ?, 'published', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                    (str(uuid.uuid4()), policy_id, actor_id, checked.approval_reference, digest, metadata, created_at),
                )
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return policy_id
            except Exception:
                if db.in_transaction:
                    db.execute("ROLLBACK")
                raise

    def retire(
        self,
        *,
        policy_record_id: str,
        actor_id_from_authenticated_session: str,
        approval_reference: str,
        reason: str,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Retire, never delete, a policy. Retirement needs independent approval."""
        actor_id = actor_id_from_authenticated_session.strip()
        if not actor_id or not policy_record_id.strip() or not approval_reference.strip() or not reason.strip():
            raise PolicyAdministrationDenied("Authenticated actor, policy ID, reason and approval reference are required")
        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if current.tzinfo is None or current.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("now must include a timezone")
        current = current.astimezone(timezone.utc)

        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            row = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM proactive_health_policies WHERE policy_record_id=?",
                (policy_record_id,),
            ).fetchone()
        if row is None:
            raise PolicyNotFound(policy_record_id)

        self.governance.assert_can_publish(actor_id=actor_id, source_id=row["source_id"])
        approval = self.governance.get_approval(approval_reference=approval_reference)
        if approval is None or approval.state != "approved" or approval.revoked_at is not None:
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("A valid retirement approval is required")
        if approval.approval_reference != approval_reference:
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Approval service returned a different approval reference")
        if approval.approved_by == actor_id:
            raise PolicyAdministrationDenied("Publisher cannot approve their own retirement")
        self.governance.assert_can_approve(actor_id=approval.approved_by, source_id=row["source_id"])
        retirement_digest = policy_retirement_digest(
            policy_record_id=policy_record_id,
            source_id=row["source_id"],
            version=row["version"],
            reason=reason,
        )
        if approval.policy_digest != retirement_digest:
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Retirement approval does not match the exact retirement request")
        if (
            approval.approved_at.tzinfo is None
            or approval.approved_at.utcoffset() is None
            or approval.expires_at.tzinfo is None
            or approval.expires_at.utcoffset() is None
            or approval.approved_at.astimezone(timezone.utc) > current
            or approval.expires_at.astimezone(timezone.utc) <= current
        ):
            raise PolicyApprovalUnavailable("Retirement approval has expired or is invalid")

        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            try:
                db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
                current_row = db.execute(
                    "SELECT * FROM proactive_health_policies WHERE policy_record_id=?",
                    (policy_record_id,),
                ).fetchone()
                if current_row is None:
                    raise PolicyNotFound(policy_record_id)
                if current_row["state"] == "retired":
                    db.execute("COMMIT")
                    return
                db.execute(
                    "UPDATE proactive_health_policies SET state='retired', retired_at=? WHERE policy_record_id=?",
                    (_iso(current), policy_record_id),
                )
                db.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO proactive_health_policy_audit (
                        audit_id, policy_record_id, action, actor_id,
                        approval_reference, policy_digest, metadata_json, occurred_at
                    ) VALUES (?, ?, 'retired', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                    (str(uuid.uuid4()), policy_record_id, actor_id, approval_reference,
                     retirement_digest, json.dumps({"source_id": row["source_id"], "reason": reason}, separators=(",", ":")), _iso(current)),
                )
                db.execute("COMMIT")
            except Exception:
                if db.in_transaction:
                    db.execute("ROLLBACK")
                raise

"""Adapters between the proactive handler contracts and Aariya-owned services.

No SQL table names are guessed here: the actual Aariya source registry,
identity/session service, device registry and consent/ACL service must be
injected. This prevents a parallel, disconnected identity database.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Protocol, Sequence

from .health_transition_handler import (
    AuthorizedRecipient,
    NoAuthorizedRecipient,
    SourceOwner,
    SourceOwnershipUnavailable,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SourceRegistrationRecord:
    source_id: str
    owner_user_id: str
    active: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DeviceNotificationRecord:
    device_id: str
    user_id: str
    active: bool
    trusted: bool
    revoked_at: datetime | None
    permitted_channels: frozenset[str]


class SourceRegistrationRepository(Protocol):
    def find_source_registration(
        self, *, source_id: str
    ) -> SourceRegistrationRecord | None:
        """Load the actual registered source and owner from Aariya's store."""
        ...


class DeviceRegistryRepository(Protocol):
    def list_devices_for_user(
        self, *, authenticated_user_id: str
    ) -> Sequence[DeviceNotificationRecord]:
        """Return actual device records from the existing identity service."""
        ...


class NotificationAuthorizationRepository(Protocol):
    def may_notify_device(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
        source_id: str,
        incident_id: str,
        channel: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """Check current per-user/source/incident/channel authorization."""
        ...


class AariyaSourceOwnershipAdapter:
    def __init__(self, repository: SourceRegistrationRepository):
        self._repository = repository

    def get_active_owner(self, source_id: str) -> SourceOwner | None:
        record = self._repository.find_source_registration(source_id=source_id)
        if record is None or record.source_id != source_id or not record.active:
            return None
        if not record.owner_user_id.strip():
            raise SourceOwnershipUnavailable(
                "Registered source has no valid owner assignment"
            )
        return SourceOwner(user_id=record.owner_user_id, active=True)


class AariyaAuthorizedRecipientAdapter:
    """Filter actual registered devices through current consent and ACL service."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        devices: DeviceRegistryRepository,
        authorization: NotificationAuthorizationRepository,
    ):
        self._devices = devices
        self._authorization = authorization

    def resolve_recipients(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        source_id: str,
        incident_id: str,
        channel: str,
    ) -> Sequence[AuthorizedRecipient]:
        if not all(value.strip() for value in (user_id, source_id, incident_id, channel)):
            raise ValueError("Recipient resolution requires complete scoped identifiers")

        allowed: list[AuthorizedRecipient] = []
        seen_ids: set[str] = set()
        for device in self._devices.list_devices_for_user(
            authenticated_user_id=user_id
        ):
            if device.user_id != user_id:
                # A faulty repository must not cross account boundaries.
                continue
            if not device.device_id or device.device_id in seen_ids:
                continue
            if not device.active or not device.trusted or device.revoked_at is not None:
                continue
            if channel not in device.permitted_channels:
                continue
            permitted = self._authorization.may_notify_device(
                user_id=user_id,
                device_id=device.device_id,
                source_id=source_id,
                incident_id=incident_id,
                channel=channel,
            )
            if not permitted:
                continue
            seen_ids.add(device.device_id)
            allowed.append(
                AuthorizedRecipient(
                    recipient_id=device.device_id,
                    user_id=user_id,
                    channel=channel,
                    active=True,
                    trusted=True,
                    consented_for_channel=True,
                )
            )
        return tuple(allowed)

"""Optional HTTP adapter for the health-policy admin service.

The caller must inject Aariya's real policy-publisher authorization dependency.
There is deliberately no fallback to the proactive bootstrap user token.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Callable

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from .health_policy_admin import (
    HealthPolicyAdminService,
    HealthPolicyDraft,
    PolicyAdministrationDenied,
    PolicyApprovalUnavailable,
    PolicyNotFound,
    PolicyOverlapConflict,
)
from .schemas import Severity


class HealthPolicyPublishRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", str_strip_whitespace=True)
    source_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    transition_to: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    version: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    severity: Severity
    score: float = Field(ge=0, le=1, allow_inf_nan=False)
    confidence: float = Field(ge=0, le=1, allow_inf_nan=False)
    recommended_channel: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    requires_verification: bool
    rationale: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=4000)
    max_attempts: int = Field(ge=1, le=1000)
    valid_from: datetime
    valid_until: datetime | None = None
    approval_reference: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=512)


class HealthPolicyRetireRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", str_strip_whitespace=True)
    approval_reference: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=512)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


class HealthPolicyPublishResponse(BaseModel):
    policy_record_id: str
    state: str


def create_health_policy_admin_router(
    *,
    service: HealthPolicyAdminService,
    require_policy_publisher: Callable[..., str],
) -> APIRouter:
    """Create routes wired to an existing authenticated admin dependency.

    `require_policy_publisher` must be a real FastAPI dependency backed by
    Aariya's account/session and role/capability system. It must return the
    authenticated actor ID only after checking policy-publisher authority.
    """
    if not callable(require_policy_publisher):
        raise ValueError("An authenticated policy-publisher dependency is required")

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/admin/proactive/health-policies",
        tags=["Aariya proactive policy administration"],
    )

    @router.post("", response_model=HealthPolicyPublishResponse, status_code=201)
    def publish_policy(
        request: HealthPolicyPublishRequest,
        actor_id: str = Depends(require_policy_publisher),
    ) -> HealthPolicyPublishResponse:
        if not isinstance(actor_id, str) or not actor_id.strip():
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Authenticated administrator required")
        draft = HealthPolicyDraft(
            source_id=request.source_id,
            transition_to=request.transition_to,
            version=request.version,
            severity=request.severity,
            score=request.score,
            confidence=request.confidence,
            recommended_channel=request.recommended_channel,
            requires_verification=request.requires_verification,
            rationale=request.rationale,
            max_attempts=request.max_attempts,
            valid_from=request.valid_from,
            valid_until=request.valid_until,
        )
        try:
            policy_id = service.publish(
                draft,
                actor_id_from_authenticated_session=actor_id,
                approval_reference=request.approval_reference,
            )
        except PolicyAdministrationDenied as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except PolicyOverlapConflict as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except PolicyApprovalUnavailable as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=503,
                detail="Policy persistence is unavailable",
            ) from exc
        return HealthPolicyPublishResponse(
            policy_record_id=policy_id,
            state="approved",
        )

    @router.post("/{policy_record_id}/retire", status_code=204)
    def retire_policy(
        policy_record_id: str,
        request: HealthPolicyRetireRequest,
        actor_id: str = Depends(require_policy_publisher),
    ) -> None:
        if not isinstance(actor_id, str) or not actor_id.strip():
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Authenticated administrator required")
        try:
            service.retire(
                policy_record_id=policy_record_id,
                actor_id_from_authenticated_session=actor_id,
                approval_reference=request.approval_reference,
                reason=request.reason,
            )
        except PolicyAdministrationDenied as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except PolicyNotFound as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Policy not found") from exc
        except PolicyApprovalUnavailable as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=503,
                detail="Policy persistence is unavailable",
            ) from exc

    return router

1. New source files
These are individual files, not a compressed package.

fcm_http_v1.py — real push-provider implementation

Open the complete source code

Implements the FCM HTTP v1 request, Google Application Default Credentials, current device-token authorization, response receipts, retryable errors, and conditional invalid-token revocation. The official API supports sending to individual device registration tokens and uses an authenticated HTTP request. 

firebase.google.com
+1







notification_worker.py — supervised delivery worker

Open the complete source code

Claims due notification jobs, checks current incident actionability, dispatches through the configured push provider, and records safe failure codes. It refuses to start when its required factories or configuration are missing.




delivery_receipt_api.py — authenticated app-received endpoint

Open the complete source code

Lets the existing Flutter app report that it received a notification, provided the request is authenticated and the device has access to the incident. It does not claim the notification was read.




Updated outbox.py and dispatcher.py

Open the outbox implementation

Open the dispatcher implementation

The persisted state now distinguishes provider_accepted from app_received. Older outbox records using the ambiguous delivered state are migrated.

failureInvalid device token





The FCM provider sends only minimal routing metadata with a generic notification. Sensitive evidence and incident details must be fetched from Aariya through an authenticated endpoint.

Important: FCM acceptance is not proof that a phone displayed or that you read the notification. The app-received endpoint proves only that the authenticated application reported receipt. It does not prove human acknowledgement or incident resolution.
3. Configuration and dependencies
The optional runtime dependency file is available here:

Open requirements-proactive.txt

After these source modules are merged into the actual Aariya backend, install the dependency in that application's Python environment:
pip install -r requirements-proactive.txt


The Firebase provider uses Google Application Default Credentials. Firebase's official documentation explains the supported credential approaches, including credentials provided through the GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS environment variable. 

firebase.google.com




Add the following variables to the protected runtime environment. Their values must come from your real Firebase project, reviewed runtime policy, and actual Aariya adapters.
AARIYA_PROACTIVE_DB_PATH=

AARIYA_FCM_PROJECT_ID=
AARIYA_FCM_NOTIFICATION_TITLE=
AARIYA_FCM_NOTIFICATION_BODY=
AARIYA_FCM_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=

AARIYA_PUSH_TOKEN_REPOSITORY_FACTORY=
AARIYA_NOTIFICATION_ACTIONABILITY_FACTORY=

AARIYA_NOTIFICATION_WORKER_POLL_SECONDS=
AARIYA_NOTIFICATION_WORKER_MAX_CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES=

AARIYA_PROACTIVE_OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE=
AARIYA_PROACTIVE_OUTBOX_LEASE_SECONDS=
AARIYA_PROACTIVE_OUTBOX_RETRY_BASE_SECONDS=
AARIYA_PROACTIVE_OUTBOX_RETRY_MAX_SECONDS=


Keep the service-account key outside source control, use the appropriate least-privilege Google IAM role, and do not put push tokens or credentials in the outbox. Google's FCM send method requires an authorized request with the appropriate Firebase Messaging scope. 

firebase.google.com



Start the worker
Once the actual repository adapters, credentials, Firebase project, and notification policy are configured:
python -m server.proactive.notification_worker


The worker currently supports push notifications only. It is not an internet voice-call worker, and it should not be started against placeholder factories.
4. Tests added
These tests are available individually:
- FCM provider tests
- Authenticated delivery-receipt tests
- Updated notification outbox tests
The tests cover privacy-minimized payloads, authorization failures, retryable HTTP errors, unregistered-token handling, provider-acceptance versus app-received states, migration of old status records, and authenticated app receipts.

No live Firebase request was sent because there is no configured Firebase project or registered real device token in this environment.
5. What is needed to complete the actual Aariya system
This has brought the source overlay closer to a working push pipeline, but the overall Aariya system is not complete yet. The remaining work is not solved by adding more generic modules.

The next stage needs the actual application code to connect:
1. The event router, policy service and stores to Aariya's existing FastAPI app and shared database.
2. The push-token repository and incident-actionability factory to the actual authenticated account, device, consent and permission records.
3. The delivery-receipt route to the existing Flutter session and notification handling.
4. VoIP calls to a real provider and Aariya's existing conversation, voice-direction and TTS pipeline.
5. The action-authorization system and independent Guardian to the real services.
The current source files still do not include Aariya's live server/main.py, authentication/device implementation or Flutter source. The next useful input is the current Aariya source archive—or those backend and Flutter directories—so the additions can be merged against the real project and tested end to end. Until that merge is complete, this remains a tested additive foundation rather than a claim that Aariya is already sending you live alerts or calling you.

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import replace

import pytest

from server.proactive.dispatcher import ProviderDeliveryError
from server.proactive.fcm_http_v1 import (
    FcmHttpV1Provider,
    HttpResult,
    PushTarget,
)
from server.proactive.outbox import NotificationJob


class TargetRepository:
    def __init__(self, target):
        self.target = target
        self.invalidated = []

    def resolve_current_authorized_target(self, *, device_id, incident_id):
        if self.target is None or self.target.device_id != device_id:
            return None
        return self.target

    def invalidate_token_if_version_matches(self, *, device_id, token_version):
        self.invalidated.append((device_id, token_version))
        return True


def _target(**overrides):
    values = dict(
        device_id="device-record-1",
        user_id="account-1",
        incident_owner_user_id="account-1",
        registration_token="device-registration-token-test-only",
        token_version="token-version-1",
        active=True,
        trusted=True,
        push_enabled=True,
        push_consent=True,
        incident_access_allowed=True,
    )
    values.update(overrides)
    return PushTarget(**values)


def _job(**overrides):
    values = dict(
        job_id="job-1",
        dedupe_key="dedupe-1",
        incident_id="incident-1",
        channel="push",
        recipient_id="device-record-1",
        payload={
            "incident_id": "incident-1",
            "kind": "monitoring_source_health",
            "title": "ignored-private-title",
        },
        state="in_flight",
        attempts=1,
        max_attempts=3,
        available_at="2026-10-24T10:00:00+00:00",
        lease_until="2026-10-24T10:01:00+00:00",
        provider_message_id=None,
        last_error_code=None,
        created_at="2026-10-24T10:00:00+00:00",
        updated_at="2026-10-24T10:00:00+00:00",
    )
    values.update(overrides)
    return NotificationJob(**values)


def _provider(repository, response, captured):
    def post(url, headers, body, timeout):
        captured.append((url, dict(headers), json.loads(body), timeout))
        return response

    return FcmHttpV1Provider(
        project_id="aariya-prod-project",
        notification_title="Aariya needs your attention",
        notification_body="Open Aariya to review an update.",
        token_repository=repository,
        timeout_seconds=5,
        access_token_supplier=lambda: "test-access-token-isolated",
        http_post=post,
    )


def test_fcm_sends_minimized_payload_and_uses_provider_receipt():
    repo = TargetRepository(_target())
    captured = []
    provider = _provider(
        repo,
        HttpResult(
            status_code=200,
            headers={},
            body=b'{"name":"projects/aariya-prod-project/messages/receipt-1"}',
        ),
        captured,
    )

    receipt = provider.deliver(_job(), idempotency_key="stable-job-key")

    assert receipt.provider_message_id.endswith("receipt-1")
    url, headers, body, timeout = captured[0]
    assert url == (
        "https://fcm.googleapis.com/v1/projects/"
        "aariya-prod-project/messages:send"
    )
    assert headers["Authorization"] == "Bearer test-access-token-isolated"
    assert body["message"]["data"] == {
        "incident_id": "incident-1",
        "kind": "monitoring_source_health",
        "delivery_id": "stable-job-key",
    }
    assert "ignored-private-title" not in json.dumps(body)
    assert body["message"]["notification"]["title"] == "Aariya needs your attention"
    assert body["message"]["android"]["notification"]["tag"]
    assert body["message"]["apns"]["headers"]["apns-collapse-id"]
    assert timeout == 5


def test_fcm_fails_closed_when_target_is_not_authorized():
    repo = TargetRepository(_target(push_consent=False))
    captured = []
    provider = _provider(
        repo,
        HttpResult(200, {}, b'{"name":"must-not-send"}'),
        captured,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ProviderDeliveryError) as error:
        provider.deliver(_job(), idempotency_key="stable-job-key")

    assert error.value.error_code == "push_target_not_authorized"
    assert captured == []


def test_fcm_rejects_cross_user_incident_target():
    repo = TargetRepository(_target(incident_owner_user_id="different-account"))
    captured = []
    provider = _provider(
        repo,
        HttpResult(200, {}, b'{"name":"must-not-send"}'),
        captured,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ProviderDeliveryError):
        provider.deliver(_job(), idempotency_key="stable-job-key")

    assert captured == []


def test_fcm_rejects_mismatched_incident_reference():
    repo = TargetRepository(_target())
    captured = []
    provider = _provider(
        repo,
        HttpResult(200, {}, b'{"name":"must-not-send"}'),
        captured,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ProviderDeliveryError) as error:
        provider.deliver(
            _job(payload={"incident_id": "other-incident", "kind": "alert"}),
            idempotency_key="stable-job-key",
        )

    assert error.value.error_code == "fcm_incident_reference_mismatch"
    assert captured == []


def test_fcm_retries_rate_limit_and_uses_retry_after():
    repo = TargetRepository(_target())
    captured = []
    provider = _provider(
        repo,
        HttpResult(429, {"Retry-After": "17"}, b'{"error":{"status":"RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED"}}'),
        captured,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ProviderDeliveryError) as error:
        provider.deliver(_job(), idempotency_key="stable-job-key")

    assert error.value.retryable is True
    assert error.value.retry_after_seconds == 17


def test_fcm_invalidates_only_the_current_unregistered_token():
    repo = TargetRepository(_target())
    captured = []
    provider = _provider(
        repo,
        HttpResult(
            404,
            {},
            b'{"error":{"status":"NOT_FOUND","details":[{"errorCode":"UNREGISTERED"}]}}',
        ),
        captured,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ProviderDeliveryError) as error:
        provider.deliver(_job(), idempotency_key="stable-job-key")

    assert error.value.error_code == "fcm_unregistered_token"
    assert error.value.retryable is False
    assert repo.invalidated == [("device-record-1", "token-version-1")]


def test_fcm_does_not_treat_generic_not_found_as_unregistered_token():
    repo = TargetRepository(_target())
    captured = []
    provider = _provider(
        repo,
        HttpResult(404, {}, b'{"error":{"status":"NOT_FOUND"}}'),
        captured,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ProviderDeliveryError) as error:
        provider.deliver(_job(), idempotency_key="stable-job-key")

    assert error.value.error_code == "fcm_http_404"
    assert repo.invalidated == []

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from server.proactive.delivery_receipt_api import (
    AuthenticatedDeviceContext,
    create_delivery_receipt_router,
)
from server.proactive.outbox import NotificationOutbox


class ReceiptAuthorization:
    def __init__(self, allowed: bool = True):
        self.allowed = allowed
        self.last_check = None

    def may_confirm_app_receipt(self, *, user_id, device_id, incident_id):
        self.last_check = (user_id, device_id, incident_id)
        return self.allowed


def _build(tmp_path: Path, *, allowed: bool = True):
    db_path = (tmp_path / "receipt.sqlite3").resolve()
    outbox = NotificationOutbox(db_path)
    job, _ = outbox.enqueue(
        dedupe_key="receipt-test-dedupe",
        incident_id="incident-test",
        channel="push",
        recipient_id="device-test",
        payload={"incident_id": "incident-test", "kind": "test-fixture"},
        max_attempts=2,
    )
    claim = outbox.claim_due(channels=["push"], limit=1, lease_seconds=30)[0]
    outbox.mark_provider_accepted(
        job_id=job.job_id,
        attempt_id=claim.attempt_id,
        provider_message_id="provider-test-receipt",
    )

    app = FastAPI()
    authorization = ReceiptAuthorization(allowed=allowed)
    router = create_delivery_receipt_router(
        outbox=outbox,
        authenticated_device_dependency=lambda: AuthenticatedDeviceContext(
            user_id="account-test",
            device_id="device-test",
        ),
        authorization=authorization,
    )
    app.include_router(router)
    return TestClient(app), outbox, job, authorization


def test_authenticated_authorized_device_can_report_app_received(tmp_path):
    client, outbox, job, authorization = _build(tmp_path)
    response = client.post(
        f"/api/proactive/v1/deliveries/{job.job_id}/app-received"
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    assert response.json()["state"] == "app_received"
    persisted = outbox.get(job.job_id)
    assert persisted.state == "app_received"
    assert persisted.app_received_at is not None
    assert authorization.last_check == (
        "account-test", "device-test", "incident-test"
    )


def test_unauthorized_user_incident_access_is_not_disclosed(tmp_path):
    client, outbox, job, _authorization = _build(tmp_path, allowed=False)
    response = client.post(
        f"/api/proactive/v1/deliveries/{job.job_id}/app-received"
    )
    assert response.status_code == 404
    assert outbox.get(job.job_id).state == "provider_accepted"


def test_repeated_app_receipt_is_idempotent(tmp_path):
    client, outbox, job, _authorization = _build(tmp_path)
    first = client.post(
        f"/api/proactive/v1/deliveries/{job.job_id}/app-received"
    )
    second = client.post(
        f"/api/proactive/v1/deliveries/{job.job_id}/app-received"
    )
    assert first.status_code == second.status_code == 200
    assert first.json()["app_received_at"] == second.json()["app_received_at"]
