"""Firebase Cloud Messaging HTTP v1 push provider.

Runtime requirements:
- AARIYA_FCM_PROJECT_ID identifies the real Firebase project.
- AARIYA_FCM_NOTIFICATION_TITLE and BODY are generic, privacy-safe strings.
- Google Application Default Credentials must be available to the process.
- The injected token repository must resolve a current, authorized device token
  and conditionally revoke an unregistered token using its token version.

The notification contains only an incident reference and routing metadata. It
must not contain logs, evidence, secrets, or private summaries. FCM acceptance
is not proof that the device displayed the notification or that the user read it.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import socket
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from email.utils import parsedate_to_datetime
from typing import Callable, Mapping, Protocol
from urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen

from .dispatcher import DeliveryReceipt, NotificationJob, ProviderDeliveryError

_FCM_SCOPE = "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/firebase.messaging"
_PROJECT_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9-]{4,128}$")


class FcmConfigurationError(RuntimeError):
    """FCM project or notification configuration is missing/invalid."""


class PushTargetUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    """No current, authorized push target exists for this device and incident."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PushTarget:
    device_id: str
    user_id: str
    incident_owner_user_id: str
    registration_token: str
    token_version: str
    active: bool
    trusted: bool
    push_enabled: bool
    push_consent: bool
    incident_access_allowed: bool


class PushTokenRepository(Protocol):
    def resolve_current_authorized_target(
        self,
        *,
        device_id: str,
        incident_id: str,
    ) -> PushTarget | None:
        """Resolve the latest token while rechecking owner, consent and ACLs.

        Implementation must query Aariya's real identity/device/authorization
        records. Never store FCM tokens in the notification outbox.
        """
        ...

    def invalidate_token_if_version_matches(
        self,
        *,
        device_id: str,
        token_version: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """Conditionally revoke the failed token without revoking a newer token."""
        ...


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class HttpResult:
    status_code: int
    headers: Mapping[str, str]
    body: bytes


HttpJsonPost = Callable[[str, Mapping[str, str], bytes, float], HttpResult]
TokenSupplier = Callable[[], str]


def _post_json(
    url: str,
    headers: Mapping[str, str],
    body: bytes,
    timeout_seconds: float,
) -> HttpResult:
    request = Request(url, data=body, headers=dict(headers), method="POST")
    try:
        with urlopen(request, timeout=timeout_seconds) as response:
            return HttpResult(
                status_code=response.status,
                headers=dict(response.headers.items()),
                body=response.read(65_536),
            )
    except HTTPError as exc:
        # HTTPError is also a response. Only a bounded body is read and raw
        # text is never included in exceptions/logs.
        try:
            response_body = exc.read(65_536)
        finally:
            exc.close()
        return HttpResult(
            status_code=exc.code,
            headers=dict(exc.headers.items()) if exc.headers else {},
            body=response_body,
        )
    except (URLError, TimeoutError, socket.timeout, OSError) as exc:
        raise ProviderDeliveryError(
            "fcm_network_unavailable", retryable=True
        ) from exc


class _GoogleAdcTokenSupplier:
    """Lazily load Google ADC and refresh short-lived access tokens."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._credentials = None
        self._request = None

    def __call__(self) -> str:
        # Imports are delayed so the rest of Aariya can load when FCM is not
        # installed/configured. The FCM worker itself must fail readiness if
        # this provider is configured but Google auth cannot initialize.
        try:
            import google.auth
            from google.auth.transport.requests import Request as GoogleRequest
        except ImportError as exc:
            raise FcmConfigurationError(
                "FCM requires the google-auth requests extra"
            ) from exc

        with self._lock:
            if self._credentials is None:
                credentials, _detected_project = google.auth.default(
                    scopes=[_FCM_SCOPE]
                )
                self._credentials = credentials
                self._request = GoogleRequest()

            credentials = self._credentials
            if not credentials.valid or credentials.expired or not credentials.token:
                credentials.refresh(self._request)

            token = credentials.token
            if not isinstance(token, str) or not token:
                raise FcmConfigurationError(
                    "Google ADC did not provide an access token"
                )
            return token


def _parse_json(body: bytes) -> dict:
    try:
        value = json.loads(body.decode("utf-8"))
    except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return {}
    return value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}


def _fcm_error_code(body: bytes) -> str | None:
    document = _parse_json(body)
    error = document.get("error")
    if not isinstance(error, dict):
        return None

    details = error.get("details")
    if isinstance(details, list):
        for detail in details:
            if isinstance(detail, dict):
                code = detail.get("errorCode")
                if isinstance(code, str) and code.strip():
                    return code

    status = error.get("status")
    return status if isinstance(status, str) and status else None


def _retry_after_seconds(headers: Mapping[str, str]) -> int | None:
    value = next(
        (v for k, v in headers.items() if k.lower() == "retry-after"),
        None,
    )
    if value is None:
        return None

    try:
        seconds = int(value.strip())
        return max(0, min(seconds, 31_536_000))
    except (ValueError, AttributeError):
        pass

    try:
        when = parsedate_to_datetime(value)
        if when.tzinfo is None:
            when = when.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        return max(
            0,
            min(
                int((when.astimezone(timezone.utc) - datetime.now(timezone.utc)).total_seconds()),
                31_536_000,
            ),
        )
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
        return None


class FcmHttpV1Provider:
    """Real FCM HTTP v1 sender implementing NotificationProvider."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        project_id: str,
        notification_title: str,
        notification_body: str,
        token_repository: PushTokenRepository,
        timeout_seconds: float,
        access_token_supplier: TokenSupplier | None = None,
        http_post: HttpJsonPost | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if not isinstance(project_id, str) or not _PROJECT_ID_RE.fullmatch(project_id):
            raise FcmConfigurationError("A valid AARIYA_FCM_PROJECT_ID is required")
        if not isinstance(notification_title, str) or not notification_title.strip() or len(notification_title) > 100:
            raise FcmConfigurationError("A generic notification title (1-100 chars) is required")
        if not isinstance(notification_body, str) or not notification_body.strip() or len(notification_body) > 240:
            raise FcmConfigurationError("A generic notification body (1-240 chars) is required")
        if not 0.2 <= timeout_seconds <= 60:
            raise FcmConfigurationError("FCM timeout must be between 0.2 and 60 seconds")
        self.project_id = project_id
        self.notification_title = notification_title
        self.notification_body = notification_body
        self.token_repository = token_repository
        self.timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds
        self._access_token_supplier = access_token_supplier or _GoogleAdcTokenSupplier()
        self._http_post = http_post or _post_json

    @classmethod
    def from_environment(
        cls,
        *,
        token_repository: PushTokenRepository,
    ) -> "FcmHttpV1Provider":
        project_id = os.environ.get("AARIYA_FCM_PROJECT_ID", "").strip()
        title = os.environ.get("AARIYA_FCM_NOTIFICATION_TITLE", "").strip()
        body = os.environ.get("AARIYA_FCM_NOTIFICATION_BODY", "").strip()
        raw_timeout = os.environ.get("AARIYA_FCM_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS", "").strip()

        if not raw_timeout:
            raise FcmConfigurationError(
                "AARIYA_FCM_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS is required"
            )
        try:
            timeout = float(raw_timeout)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise FcmConfigurationError(
                "AARIYA_FCM_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS must be numeric"
            ) from exc

        return cls(
            project_id=project_id,
            notification_title=title,
            notification_body=body,
            token_repository=token_repository,
            timeout_seconds=timeout,
        )

    def check_credentials(self) -> None:
        """Verify that ADC can mint an OAuth token; this does not verify FCM IAM."""
        try:
            token = self._access_token_supplier()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise FcmConfigurationError(
                "Google Application Default Credentials are unavailable"
            ) from exc
        if not isinstance(token, str) or not token:
            raise FcmConfigurationError(
                "Google Application Default Credentials returned no access token"
            )

    def _authorized_target(self, job: NotificationJob) -> PushTarget:
        if job.channel != "push":
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_channel_not_supported", retryable=False
            )
        if not job.incident_id:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_incident_reference_required", retryable=False
            )
        if job.payload.get("incident_id") != job.incident_id:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_incident_reference_mismatch", retryable=False
            )

        try:
            target = self.token_repository.resolve_current_authorized_target(
                device_id=job.recipient_id,
                incident_id=job.incident_id,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "push_target_lookup_failed", retryable=True
            ) from exc

        if target is None:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "push_target_not_authorized", retryable=False
            )

        if (
            target.device_id != job.recipient_id
            or not target.user_id.strip()
            or target.user_id != target.incident_owner_user_id
            or not target.registration_token.strip()
            or not target.token_version.strip()
            or not target.active
            or not target.trusted
            or not target.push_enabled
            or not target.push_consent
            or not target.incident_access_allowed
        ):
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "push_target_not_authorized", retryable=False
            )

        return target

    def deliver(
        self,
        job: NotificationJob,
        *,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> DeliveryReceipt:
        """Ask FCM to accept a privacy-minimized push message.

        FCM HTTP v1 does not provide a general idempotency-key contract; a
        stable Android tag/APNs collapse ID and client-side delivery_id are
        included to reduce duplicate visible notifications. This is still
        at-least-once, not exactly-once delivery.
        """
        if not isinstance(idempotency_key, str) or not idempotency_key.strip():
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_idempotency_key_required", retryable=False
            )

        target = self._authorized_target(job)
        incident_id = job.incident_id
        kind = job.payload.get("kind")
        if not isinstance(kind, str) or not kind.strip() or len(kind) > 100:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_notification_kind_invalid", retryable=False
            )

        collapse_id = hashlib.sha256(
            idempotency_key.encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()

        message = {
            "message": {
                "token": target.registration_token,
                "notification": {
                    "title": self.notification_title,
                    "body": self.notification_body,
                },
                "data": {
                    "incident_id": incident_id,
                    "kind": kind,
                    "delivery_id": idempotency_key,
                },
                "android": {
                    "notification": {"tag": collapse_id},
                },
                "apns": {
                    "headers": {
                        "apns-collapse-id": collapse_id,
                        "apns-priority": "10",
                    },
                },
            }
        }

        try:
            access_token = self._access_token_supplier()
        except FcmConfigurationError as exc:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_auth_configuration_unavailable", retryable=True
            ) from exc
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_auth_token_unavailable", retryable=True
            ) from exc

        if not isinstance(access_token, str) or not access_token:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_auth_token_unavailable", retryable=True
            )

        url = (
            "https://fcm.googleapis.com/v1/projects/"
            f"{self.project_id}/messages:send"
        )
        body = json.dumps(
            message,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            allow_nan=False,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        ).encode("utf-8")

        try:
            response = self._http_post(
                url,
                {
                    "Authorization": f"Bearer {access_token}",
                    "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                    "Accept": "application/json",
                },
                body,
                self.timeout_seconds,
            )
        except ProviderDeliveryError:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_transport_error", retryable=True
            ) from exc

        response_json = _parse_json(response.body)
        if 200 <= response.status_code < 300:
            receipt_id = response_json.get("name")
            if not isinstance(receipt_id, str) or not receipt_id.strip():
                raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                    "fcm_success_without_receipt", retryable=True
                )
            return DeliveryReceipt(provider_message_id=receipt_id)

        provider_error = _fcm_error_code(response.body)
        if response.status_code == 404 and provider_error == "UNREGISTERED":
            try:
                self.token_repository.invalidate_token_if_version_matches(
                    device_id=target.device_id,
                    token_version=target.token_version,
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                    "fcm_unregistered_token_cleanup_failed", retryable=True
                ) from exc
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_unregistered_token", retryable=False
            )

        retryable = (
            response.status_code in {408, 425, 429}
            or 500 <= response.status_code <= 599
        )
        raise ProviderDeliveryError(
            f"fcm_http_{response.status_code}",
            retryable=retryable,
            retry_after_seconds=_retry_after_seconds(response.headers),
        )


"""Supervised dispatcher process for real configured notification providers.

Required runtime dependencies are supplied by trusted deployment configuration:
- actual push-token/authorization repository factory;
- actual incident-actionability callback factory;
- FCM project, privacy-safe notification text, and Google ADC;
- durable database and explicit dispatch retry settings.

This worker supports FCM push only. Internet calls require a separate real VoIP
provider and must not be routed through this FCM adapter.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import importlib
import json
import logging
import os
import signal
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

from .dispatcher import DispatchPolicy, NotificationDispatcher
from .fcm_http_v1 import FcmConfigurationError, FcmHttpV1Provider
from .outbox import NotificationOutbox, NotificationJob

logger = logging.getLogger("aariya.proactive.notification_worker")


class NotificationWorkerConfigurationError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def _required_string(name: str) -> str:
    value = os.environ.get(name, "").strip()
    if not value:
        raise NotificationWorkerConfigurationError(f"{name} is required")
    return value


def _required_int(name: str, *, minimum: int, maximum: int) -> int:
    raw = _required_string(name)
    try:
        value = int(raw)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise NotificationWorkerConfigurationError(
            f"{name} must be an integer"
        ) from exc
    if not minimum <= value <= maximum:
        raise NotificationWorkerConfigurationError(
            f"{name} must be between {minimum} and {maximum}"
        )
    return value


def _factory(name: str) -> Callable:
    reference = _required_string(name)
    module_name, separator, attribute_name = reference.partition(":")
    if (
        not separator
        or not module_name.strip()
        or not attribute_name.strip()
        or "." not in module_name
    ):
        raise NotificationWorkerConfigurationError(
            f"{name} must be a trusted module:function reference"
        )

    try:
        module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
        factory = getattr(module, attribute_name)
    except (ImportError, AttributeError) as exc:
        raise NotificationWorkerConfigurationError(
            f"Configured factory for {name} is unavailable"
        ) from exc

    if not callable(factory):
        raise NotificationWorkerConfigurationError(
            f"Configured factory for {name} is not callable"
        )
    return factory


def _log_event(event: str, **fields: object) -> None:
    logger.info(json.dumps(
        {
            "component": "notification_worker",
            "event": event,
            **fields,
        },
        ensure_ascii=False,
        allow_nan=False,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    ))


def _load_database_path() -> Path:
    raw = _required_string("AARIYA_PROACTIVE_DB_PATH")
    path = Path(raw).expanduser()
    if not path.is_absolute() or str(path) == ":memory:":
        raise NotificationWorkerConfigurationError(
            "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_DB_PATH must be an absolute file-backed path"
        )
    return path


def build_runtime():
    """Build a real FCM dispatcher from existing Aariya repository adapters."""
    database_path = _load_database_path()
    token_repository_factory = _factory(
        "AARIYA_PUSH_TOKEN_REPOSITORY_FACTORY"
    )
    actionability_factory = _factory(
        "AARIYA_NOTIFICATION_ACTIONABILITY_FACTORY"
    )

    # Factories must read Aariya's actual device/token registry, ACL/consent
    # service and incident state. The worker does not create a parallel registry.
    token_repository = token_repository_factory(
        database_path=database_path
    )
    can_deliver = actionability_factory(
        database_path=database_path
    )

    if not callable(getattr(token_repository, "resolve_current_authorized_target", None)):
        raise NotificationWorkerConfigurationError(
            "Push-token repository does not implement the required resolver"
        )
    if not callable(getattr(token_repository, "invalidate_token_if_version_matches", None)):
        raise NotificationWorkerConfigurationError(
            "Push-token repository must support version-conditional token revocation"
        )
    if not callable(can_deliver):
        raise NotificationWorkerConfigurationError(
            "Actionability factory must return a callable"
        )

    outbox = NotificationOutbox(database_path)
    provider = FcmHttpV1Provider.from_environment(
        token_repository=token_repository
    )
    provider.check_credentials()

    dispatcher = NotificationDispatcher(
        outbox=outbox,
        providers={"push": provider},
        policy=DispatchPolicy.from_environment(),
    )

    return dispatcher, can_deliver


async def run_worker() -> None:
    poll_seconds = _required_int(
        "AARIYA_NOTIFICATION_WORKER_POLL_SECONDS",
        minimum=1,
        maximum=3600,
    )
    max_consecutive_failures = _required_int(
        "AARIYA_NOTIFICATION_WORKER_MAX_CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES",
        minimum=1,
        maximum=1000,
    )

    dispatcher, can_deliver = build_runtime()
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    stop = asyncio.Event()

    def request_stop() -> None:
        stop.set()

    for signum in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):
        try:
            loop.add_signal_handler(signum, request_stop)
        except (NotImplementedError, RuntimeError, ValueError):
            try:
                signal.signal(
                    signum,
                    lambda _sig, _frame: loop.call_soon_threadsafe(request_stop),
                )
            except (OSError, ValueError):
                pass

    failures = 0
    _log_event("worker_started", channels=["push"], poll_seconds=poll_seconds)

    try:
        while not stop.is_set():
            try:
                result = await asyncio.to_thread(
                    dispatcher.dispatch_due,
                    can_deliver=can_deliver,
                )
                failures = 0
                _log_event(
                    "dispatch_batch_completed",
                    claimed=result.get("claimed", 0),
                    counts=result.get("counts", {}),
                )
            except Exception:
                failures += 1
                _log_event(
                    "dispatch_batch_failed",
                    error_code="dispatch_batch_failed",
                    consecutive_failures=failures,
                )
                if failures >= max_consecutive_failures:
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        "Notification worker reached its configured failure limit"
                    ) from None

            try:
                await asyncio.wait_for(stop.wait(), timeout=poll_seconds)
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                pass
    finally:
        _log_event("worker_stopped")


def main() -> None:
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(message)s")
    try:
        asyncio.run(run_worker())
    except (NotificationWorkerConfigurationError, FcmConfigurationError) as exc:
        _log_event("startup_blocked", reason=str(exc))
        raise SystemExit(2) from None
    except Exception:
        _log_event("worker_failed", error_code="worker_failed")
        raise SystemExit(1) from None


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


"""Authenticated device receipt endpoint for proactive push jobs.

The caller must inject Aariya's real device-session dependency and a current
incident/device authorization service. This module never accepts user_id or
device_id from the request body as identity proof.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Callable, Protocol

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel

from .outbox import (
    NotificationOutbox,
    OutboxConflict,
    OutboxNotFound,
    OutboxValidationError,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AuthenticatedDeviceContext:
    user_id: str
    device_id: str


class DeliveryReceiptAuthorization(Protocol):
    def may_confirm_app_receipt(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
        incident_id: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """Check current device/session, user/incident ownership and access."""
        ...


class AppReceivedResponse(BaseModel):
    job_id: str
    state: str
    app_received_at: str | None


def create_delivery_receipt_router(
    *,
    outbox: NotificationOutbox,
    authenticated_device_dependency: Callable[..., AuthenticatedDeviceContext],
    authorization: DeliveryReceiptAuthorization,
) -> APIRouter:
    """Build routes using existing authenticated Aariya identity services."""
    if not callable(authenticated_device_dependency):
        raise ValueError("A real authenticated device dependency is required")

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/proactive/v1/deliveries",
        tags=["Aariya delivery receipts"],
    )

    @router.post("/{job_id}/app-received", response_model=AppReceivedResponse)
    def app_received(
        job_id: str,
        device: AuthenticatedDeviceContext = Depends(
            authenticated_device_dependency
        ),
    ) -> AppReceivedResponse:
        if (
            not isinstance(device, AuthenticatedDeviceContext)
            or not device.user_id.strip()
            or not device.device_id.strip()
        ):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Authenticated device required")

        try:
            job = outbox.get(job_id)
        except OutboxNotFound as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Delivery not found") from exc

        # Avoid disclosing whether another device has a notification job.
        if not job.incident_id or job.recipient_id != device.device_id:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Delivery not found")

        try:
            permitted = authorization.may_confirm_app_receipt(
                user_id=device.user_id,
                device_id=device.device_id,
                incident_id=job.incident_id,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=503,
                detail="Delivery authorization is unavailable",
            ) from exc

        if not permitted:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Delivery not found")

        try:
            received = outbox.mark_app_received(
                job_id=job_id,
                authenticated_device_id=device.device_id,
            )
        except OutboxNotFound as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Delivery not found") from exc
        except OutboxConflict as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except OutboxValidationError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=str(exc)) from exc

        return AppReceivedResponse(
            job_id=received.job_id,
            state=received.state,
            app_received_at=received.app_received_at,
        )

    return router

"""Durable notification outbox primitives for Aariya.

This module queues notifications only. It deliberately contains no fake provider,
recipient, event, credential, or automatic notification policy. A real dispatcher
must supply channel, recipient, payload, and retry/lease policy from trusted runtime
configuration and real incident state.

Delivery semantics are at-least-once. Some providers, including FCM HTTP v1,
do not offer a general idempotency-key contract. A crash after provider acceptance
but before saving its receipt can therefore result in a duplicate attempt. Provider
acceptance is distinct from app receipt and from user acknowledgement.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
import uuid
from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable


class OutboxConflict(RuntimeError):
    """A job transition is invalid or an idempotency key was reused differently."""


class OutboxNotFound(LookupError):
    """The requested outbox job or attempt does not exist."""


class OutboxValidationError(ValueError):
    """The supplied delivery data or timing policy is invalid."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class NotificationJob:
    job_id: str
    dedupe_key: str
    incident_id: str | None
    channel: str
    recipient_id: str
    payload: dict[str, Any]
    state: str
    attempts: int
    max_attempts: int
    available_at: str
    lease_until: str | None
    provider_message_id: str | None
    last_error_code: str | None
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str
    provider_accepted_at: str | None = None
    app_received_at: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ClaimedNotification:
    job: NotificationJob
    attempt_id: str
    attempt_number: int


def utc_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def _iso(value: datetime) -> str:
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="microseconds")


def _json(value: Any) -> str:
    try:
        encoded = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise OutboxValidationError("payload must contain finite JSON-compatible values") from exc
    if len(encoded.encode("utf-8")) > 16_384:
        raise OutboxValidationError("payload must not exceed 16 KiB")
    return encoded


def _decode(row: sqlite3.Row) -> NotificationJob:
    return NotificationJob(
        job_id=row["job_id"],
        dedupe_key=row["dedupe_key"],
        incident_id=row["incident_id"],
        channel=row["channel"],
        recipient_id=row["recipient_id"],
        payload=json.loads(row["payload_json"]),
        state=row["state"],
        attempts=row["attempts"],
        max_attempts=row["max_attempts"],
        available_at=row["available_at"],
        lease_until=row["lease_until"],
        provider_message_id=row["provider_message_id"],
        provider_accepted_at=row["provider_accepted_at"],
        app_received_at=row["app_received_at"],
        last_error_code=row["last_error_code"],
        created_at=row["created_at"],
        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
    )


class NotificationOutbox:
    """SQLite outbox; use the same configured DB path as the proactive event store.

    This is a single-host implementation. For multiple backend instances, use a
    shared production database/queue with equivalent atomic-claim semantics.
    """

    def __init__(self, database_path: Path):
        if not database_path.is_absolute():
            raise OutboxValidationError("database_path must be absolute")
        if str(database_path) == ":memory:":
            raise OutboxValidationError("durable outbox requires a file-backed database")
        self.database_path = database_path
        self.database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._initialize()

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        connection = sqlite3.connect(self.database_path, timeout=5.0, isolation_level=None)
        connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        connection.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
        connection.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000")
        connection.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
        return connection

    def _initialize(self) -> None:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            table = db.execute(
                "SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' "
                "AND name='proactive_notification_outbox'"
            ).fetchone()
            if table is not None:
                schema_sql = table["sql"] or ""
                legacy_delivered = (
                    "'delivered'" in schema_sql
                    and "'provider_accepted'" not in schema_sql
                )
                intermediate_device_state = (
                    "'device_confirmed'" in schema_sql
                    and "'app_received'" not in schema_sql
                )
                if legacy_delivered or intermediate_device_state:
                    self._migrate_delivery_states(db)

            db.executescript(
                """
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_notification_outbox (
                    job_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    dedupe_key TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                    incident_id TEXT,
                    channel TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(channel) BETWEEN 1 AND 80),
                    recipient_id TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(recipient_id) BETWEEN 1 AND 256),
                    payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                    state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN (
                        'pending','in_flight','provider_accepted',
                        'app_received','dead','cancelled'
                    )),
                    attempts INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(attempts >= 0),
                    max_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(max_attempts BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),
                    available_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    lease_until TEXT,
                    provider_message_id TEXT,
                    provider_accepted_at TEXT,
                    app_received_at TEXT,
                    last_error_code TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_proactive_outbox_due
                    ON proactive_notification_outbox(state, available_at, lease_until, created_at);
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_proactive_outbox_incident
                    ON proactive_notification_outbox(incident_id, state);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_notification_attempts (
                    attempt_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    job_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES proactive_notification_outbox(job_id),
                    attempt_number INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(attempt_number > 0),
                    state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN (
                        'started','provider_accepted','retryable_failure',
                        'permanent_failure','cancelled'
                    )),
                    started_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    finished_at TEXT,
                    provider_message_id TEXT,
                    error_code TEXT,
                    UNIQUE(job_id, attempt_number)
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_proactive_outbox_attempts_job
                    ON proactive_notification_attempts(job_id, attempt_number);
                """
            )

            # Additive migration for a database created by an intermediate
            # overlay version that already had the new state names but lacked
            # receipt timestamps. No production data is discarded.
            columns = {
                row["name"]
                for row in db.execute(
                    "PRAGMA table_info(proactive_notification_outbox)"
                ).fetchall()
            }
            if "provider_accepted_at" not in columns:
                db.execute(
                    "ALTER TABLE proactive_notification_outbox "
                    "ADD COLUMN provider_accepted_at TEXT"
                )
            if "app_received_at" not in columns:
                db.execute(
                    "ALTER TABLE proactive_notification_outbox "
                    "ADD COLUMN app_received_at TEXT"
                )

    @staticmethod
    def _migrate_delivery_states(db: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        """Migrate legacy state names to provider_accepted/app_received accurately."""
        tables = {
            row[0]
            for row in db.execute(
                "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"
            ).fetchall()
        }
        if "proactive_notification_attempts" not in tables:
            raise OutboxValidationError(
                "Legacy outbox exists without its attempts table; refusing migration"
            )

        old_outbox_columns = {
            row["name"]
            for row in db.execute(
                "PRAGMA table_info(proactive_notification_outbox)"
            ).fetchall()
        }
        accepted_at_expr = (
            "COALESCE(provider_accepted_at, CASE "
            "WHEN state IN ('delivered','provider_accepted','device_confirmed') "
            "THEN updated_at ELSE NULL END)"
            if "provider_accepted_at" in old_outbox_columns
            else "CASE WHEN state IN ('delivered','provider_accepted','device_confirmed') THEN updated_at ELSE NULL END"
        )
        app_received_at_expr = (
            "app_received_at"
            if "app_received_at" in old_outbox_columns
            else (
                "device_confirmed_at"
                if "device_confirmed_at" in old_outbox_columns
                else "CASE WHEN state='device_confirmed' THEN updated_at ELSE NULL END"
            )
        )

        db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF")
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            for index in (
                "idx_proactive_outbox_due",
                "idx_proactive_outbox_incident",
                "idx_proactive_outbox_attempts_job",
            ):
                db.execute(f"DROP INDEX IF EXISTS {index}")

            db.execute(
                "ALTER TABLE proactive_notification_attempts "
                "RENAME TO proactive_notification_attempts_legacy"
            )
            db.execute(
                "ALTER TABLE proactive_notification_outbox "
                "RENAME TO proactive_notification_outbox_legacy"
            )

            db.execute(
                """CREATE TABLE proactive_notification_outbox (
                    job_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    dedupe_key TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                    incident_id TEXT,
                    channel TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(channel) BETWEEN 1 AND 80),
                    recipient_id TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(recipient_id) BETWEEN 1 AND 256),
                    payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                    state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN (
                        'pending','in_flight','provider_accepted',
                        'app_received','dead','cancelled'
                    )),
                    attempts INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(attempts >= 0),
                    max_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(max_attempts BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),
                    available_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    lease_until TEXT,
                    provider_message_id TEXT,
                    provider_accepted_at TEXT,
                    app_received_at TEXT,
                    last_error_code TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
                )"""
            )
            db.execute(
                """CREATE TABLE proactive_notification_attempts (
                    attempt_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    job_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES proactive_notification_outbox(job_id),
                    attempt_number INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(attempt_number > 0),
                    state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN (
                        'started','provider_accepted','retryable_failure',
                        'permanent_failure','cancelled'
                    )),
                    started_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    finished_at TEXT,
                    provider_message_id TEXT,
                    error_code TEXT,
                    UNIQUE(job_id, attempt_number)
                )"""
            )

            db.execute(
                f"""INSERT INTO proactive_notification_outbox (
                    job_id, dedupe_key, incident_id, channel, recipient_id,
                    payload_json, state, attempts, max_attempts, available_at,
                    lease_until, provider_message_id, provider_accepted_at,
                    app_received_at, last_error_code, created_at, updated_at
                )
                SELECT job_id, dedupe_key, incident_id, channel, recipient_id,
                    payload_json,
                    CASE
                        WHEN state='delivered' THEN 'provider_accepted'
                        WHEN state='device_confirmed' THEN 'app_received'
                        ELSE state
                    END,
                    attempts, max_attempts, available_at, lease_until,
                    provider_message_id,
                    {accepted_at_expr},
                    {app_received_at_expr},
                    last_error_code, created_at, updated_at
                FROM proactive_notification_outbox_legacy"""
            )
            db.execute(
                """INSERT INTO proactive_notification_attempts (
                    attempt_id, job_id, attempt_number, state, started_at,
                    finished_at, provider_message_id, error_code
                )
                SELECT attempt_id, job_id, attempt_number,
                    CASE WHEN state='delivered' THEN 'provider_accepted' ELSE state END,
                    started_at, finished_at, provider_message_id, error_code
                FROM proactive_notification_attempts_legacy"""
            )
            db.execute("DROP TABLE proactive_notification_attempts_legacy")
            db.execute("DROP TABLE proactive_notification_outbox_legacy")
            db.execute(
                "CREATE INDEX idx_proactive_outbox_due ON proactive_notification_outbox(state, available_at, lease_until, created_at)"
            )
            db.execute(
                "CREATE INDEX idx_proactive_outbox_incident ON proactive_notification_outbox(incident_id, state)"
            )
            db.execute(
                "CREATE INDEX idx_proactive_outbox_attempts_job ON proactive_notification_attempts(job_id, attempt_number)"
            )
            db.execute("COMMIT")
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")

    def enqueue(
        self,
        *,
        dedupe_key: str,
        incident_id: str | None,
        channel: str,
        recipient_id: str,
        payload: dict[str, Any],
        max_attempts: int,
        available_at: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[NotificationJob, bool]:
        """Persist a notification request; return (job, duplicate)."""
        values = {
            "dedupe_key": dedupe_key,
            "incident_id": incident_id,
            "channel": channel,
            "recipient_id": recipient_id,
        }
        for key, value in values.items():
            if value is not None and (not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip()):
                raise OutboxValidationError(f"{key} must be a non-empty string when provided")
        if len(dedupe_key) > 512:
            raise OutboxValidationError("dedupe_key must not exceed 512 characters")
        if incident_id is not None and len(incident_id) > 200:
            raise OutboxValidationError("incident_id must not exceed 200 characters")
        if len(channel) > 80 or len(recipient_id) > 256:
            raise OutboxValidationError("channel or recipient_id exceeds its maximum length")
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            raise OutboxValidationError("payload must be a JSON object")
        if not 1 <= max_attempts <= 1_000:
            raise OutboxValidationError("max_attempts must be between 1 and 1000")
        payload_json = _json(payload)
        now = utc_now()
        available = available_at or now
        if available.tzinfo is None or available.utcoffset() is None:
            raise OutboxValidationError("available_at must include a timezone")
        now_text, available_text = _iso(now), _iso(available)

        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            existing = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE dedupe_key = ?",
                (dedupe_key,),
            ).fetchone()
            if existing:
                expected = (incident_id, channel, recipient_id, payload_json, max_attempts)
                actual = (existing["incident_id"], existing["channel"], existing["recipient_id"], existing["payload_json"], existing["max_attempts"])
                if actual != expected:
                    raise OutboxConflict("dedupe_key was reused with different notification content")
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return _decode(existing), True

            job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            db.execute(
                """INSERT INTO proactive_notification_outbox
                   (job_id, dedupe_key, incident_id, channel, recipient_id, payload_json,
                    state, attempts, max_attempts, available_at, lease_until, provider_message_id,
                    last_error_code, created_at, updated_at)
                   VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending', 0, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?, ?)""",
                (job_id, dedupe_key, incident_id, channel, recipient_id, payload_json, max_attempts, available_text, now_text, now_text),
            )
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return _decode(row), False
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def claim_due(
        self,
        *,
        channels: Iterable[str],
        limit: int,
        lease_seconds: int,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> list[ClaimedNotification]:
        """Atomically lease due jobs only for channels with registered providers."""
        channel_list = tuple(dict.fromkeys(channels))
        if not channel_list:
            return []
        if any(not isinstance(ch, str) or not ch.strip() for ch in channel_list):
            raise OutboxValidationError("channels must contain non-empty names")
        if not 1 <= limit <= 500:
            raise OutboxValidationError("limit must be between 1 and 500")
        if not 1 <= lease_seconds <= 86_400:
            raise OutboxValidationError("lease_seconds must be between 1 and 86400")
        clock = now or utc_now()
        if clock.tzinfo is None or clock.utcoffset() is None:
            raise OutboxValidationError("now must include a timezone")
        now_text = _iso(clock)
        lease_until = _iso(clock + timedelta(seconds=lease_seconds))
        placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in channel_list)
        db = self._connect()
        claimed: list[ClaimedNotification] = []
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            exhausted = db.execute(
                """SELECT job_id FROM proactive_notification_outbox
                   WHERE state = 'in_flight' AND lease_until <= ? AND attempts >= max_attempts""",
                (now_text,),
            ).fetchall()
            for exhausted_row in exhausted:
                job_id = exhausted_row["job_id"]
                db.execute(
                    """UPDATE proactive_notification_attempts
                       SET state = 'permanent_failure', finished_at = ?, error_code = 'lease_expired_after_max_attempts'
                       WHERE job_id = ? AND state = 'started'""",
                    (now_text, job_id),
                )
                db.execute(
                    """UPDATE proactive_notification_outbox
                       SET state = 'dead', lease_until = NULL, last_error_code = 'lease_expired_after_max_attempts', updated_at = ?
                       WHERE job_id = ?""",
                    (now_text, job_id),
                )

            rows = db.execute(
                f"""SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox
                    WHERE channel IN ({placeholders})
                      AND attempts < max_attempts
                      AND available_at <= ?
                      AND (state = 'pending' OR (state = 'in_flight' AND lease_until <= ?))
                    ORDER BY available_at ASC, created_at ASC, job_id ASC
                    LIMIT ?""",
                (*channel_list, now_text, now_text, limit),
            ).fetchall()
            for row in rows:
                # Close the expired attempt before creating its successor.
                # The previous worker may still return later, so transition methods
                # also verify that the attempt is still the current attempt number.
                if row["state"] == "in_flight":
                    db.execute(
                        """UPDATE proactive_notification_attempts
                           SET state = 'retryable_failure', finished_at = ?, error_code = 'lease_expired'
                           WHERE job_id = ? AND attempt_number = ? AND state = 'started'""",
                        (now_text, row["job_id"], int(row["attempts"])),
                    )
                # Reclaiming expired leases is at-least-once. Provider idempotency should use job_id.
                attempt_number = int(row["attempts"]) + 1
                attempt_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
                db.execute(
                    """UPDATE proactive_notification_outbox
                       SET state = 'in_flight', attempts = ?, lease_until = ?, updated_at = ?
                       WHERE job_id = ?""",
                    (attempt_number, lease_until, now_text, row["job_id"]),
                )
                db.execute(
                    """INSERT INTO proactive_notification_attempts
                       (attempt_id, job_id, attempt_number, state, started_at)
                       VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'started', ?)""",
                    (attempt_id, row["job_id"], attempt_number, now_text),
                )
                updated = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (row["job_id"],)).fetchone()
                claimed.append(ClaimedNotification(_decode(updated), attempt_id, attempt_number))
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return claimed
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def mark_provider_accepted(self, *, job_id: str, attempt_id: str, provider_message_id: str | None) -> NotificationJob:
        now = _iso(utc_now())
        if provider_message_id is not None and (not provider_message_id.strip() or len(provider_message_id) > 512):
            raise OutboxValidationError("provider_message_id must be non-empty and at most 512 characters when provided")
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            job = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            attempt = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_attempts WHERE attempt_id = ? AND job_id = ?", (attempt_id, job_id)).fetchone()
            if job is None or attempt is None:
                raise OutboxNotFound("Notification job or attempt not found")
            if job["state"] in {"provider_accepted", "app_received"}:
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return _decode(job)
            if (job["state"] != "in_flight" or attempt["state"] != "started"
                    or int(attempt["attempt_number"]) != int(job["attempts"])):
                raise OutboxConflict("Only the current in-flight attempt can be marked provider-accepted")
            db.execute(
                """UPDATE proactive_notification_outbox
                   SET state = 'provider_accepted', lease_until = NULL, provider_message_id = ?,
                       provider_accepted_at = ?, last_error_code = NULL, updated_at = ? WHERE job_id = ?""",
                (provider_message_id, now, now, job_id),
            )
            db.execute(
                """UPDATE proactive_notification_attempts
                   SET state = 'provider_accepted', finished_at = ?, provider_message_id = ? WHERE attempt_id = ?""",
                (now, provider_message_id, attempt_id),
            )
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return _decode(row)
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def mark_delivered(
        self, *, job_id: str, attempt_id: str, provider_message_id: str | None
    ) -> NotificationJob:
        """Compatibility alias. The resulting state means provider_accepted, not device-delivered."""
        return self.mark_provider_accepted(
            job_id=job_id,
            attempt_id=attempt_id,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        )

    def mark_app_received(
        self,
        *,
        job_id: str,
        authenticated_device_id: str,
        received_at: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> NotificationJob:
        """Record an authenticated app-received receipt after API authorization.

        This proves only that the authenticated app reported receipt. It does
        not prove a notification was displayed or read. The caller must verify
        user session, device ownership and incident access before calling this.
        """
        if not authenticated_device_id.strip():
            raise OutboxValidationError("authenticated_device_id is required")
        current = received_at or utc_now()
        now = _iso(current)
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            job = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?",
                (job_id,),
            ).fetchone()
            if job is None:
                raise OutboxNotFound("Notification job not found")
            if job["recipient_id"] != authenticated_device_id:
                raise OutboxConflict("Device is not the recipient of this job")
            if job["state"] == "app_received":
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return _decode(job)
            if job["state"] not in {"provider_accepted", "app_received"}:
                raise OutboxConflict("Only provider-accepted jobs can be marked app-received")
            db.execute(
                """UPDATE proactive_notification_outbox
                   SET state='app_received', app_received_at=?, updated_at=?
                   WHERE job_id=?""",
                (now, now, job_id),
            )
            row = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id=?",
                (job_id,),
            ).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return _decode(row)
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def mark_failed(
        self,
        *,
        job_id: str,
        attempt_id: str,
        error_code: str,
        retryable: bool,
        retry_delay_seconds: int,
    ) -> NotificationJob:
        """Record failure without persisting raw exception text or credential-bearing details."""
        if not error_code.strip() or len(error_code) > 120:
            raise OutboxValidationError("error_code must be a non-empty provider-safe code of at most 120 characters")
        if not 0 <= retry_delay_seconds <= 31_536_000:
            raise OutboxValidationError("retry_delay_seconds must be between 0 and one year")
        now_dt = utc_now()
        now_text = _iso(now_dt)
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            job = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            attempt = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_attempts WHERE attempt_id = ? AND job_id = ?", (attempt_id, job_id)).fetchone()
            if job is None or attempt is None:
                raise OutboxNotFound("Notification job or attempt not found")
            if (job["state"] != "in_flight" or attempt["state"] != "started"
                    or int(attempt["attempt_number"]) != int(job["attempts"])):
                raise OutboxConflict("Only the current in-flight attempt can be marked failed")

            will_retry = retryable and int(job["attempts"]) < int(job["max_attempts"])
            next_state = "pending" if will_retry else "dead"
            attempt_state = "retryable_failure" if will_retry else "permanent_failure"
            next_available = _iso(now_dt + timedelta(seconds=retry_delay_seconds)) if will_retry else job["available_at"]
            db.execute(
                """UPDATE proactive_notification_outbox
                   SET state = ?, available_at = ?, lease_until = NULL, last_error_code = ?, updated_at = ?
                   WHERE job_id = ?""",
                (next_state, next_available, error_code, now_text, job_id),
            )
            db.execute(
                """UPDATE proactive_notification_attempts
                   SET state = ?, finished_at = ?, error_code = ? WHERE attempt_id = ?""",
                (attempt_state, now_text, error_code, attempt_id),
            )
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return _decode(row)
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def cancel_claimed(self, *, job_id: str, attempt_id: str, reason_code: str) -> NotificationJob:
        """Cancel a claimed job when a caller confirms its incident is no longer actionable."""
        if not reason_code.strip() or len(reason_code) > 120:
            raise OutboxValidationError("reason_code must be a short non-empty code")
        now = _iso(utc_now())
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            job = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            attempt = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_attempts WHERE attempt_id = ? AND job_id = ?", (attempt_id, job_id)).fetchone()
            if job is None or attempt is None:
                raise OutboxNotFound("Notification job or attempt not found")
            if (job["state"] != "in_flight" or attempt["state"] != "started"
                    or int(attempt["attempt_number"]) != int(job["attempts"])):
                raise OutboxConflict("Only the current in-flight attempt can be cancelled")
            db.execute(
                "UPDATE proactive_notification_outbox SET state = 'cancelled', lease_until = NULL, last_error_code = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE job_id = ?",
                (reason_code, now, job_id),
            )
            db.execute(
                "UPDATE proactive_notification_attempts SET state = 'cancelled', finished_at = ?, error_code = ? WHERE attempt_id = ?",
                (now, reason_code, attempt_id),
            )
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return _decode(row)
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def get(self, job_id: str) -> NotificationJob:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
        if row is None:
            raise OutboxNotFound(job_id)
        return _decode(row)

    def list_jobs(self, *, states: Iterable[str] | None = None, limit: int = 100) -> list[NotificationJob]:
        if not 1 <= limit <= 500:
            raise OutboxValidationError("limit must be between 1 and 500")
        state_list = tuple(dict.fromkeys(states or ()))
        valid_states = {
            "pending", "in_flight", "provider_accepted",
            "app_received", "dead", "cancelled"
        }
        if any(state not in valid_states for state in state_list):
            raise OutboxValidationError("states contains an unsupported state")
        query = "SELECT * FROM proactive_notification_outbox"
        args: tuple[Any, ...] = ()
        if state_list:
            placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in state_list)
            query += f" WHERE state IN ({placeholders})"
            args = state_list
        query += " ORDER BY created_at DESC, job_id DESC LIMIT ?"
        args += (limit,)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            rows = db.execute(query, args).fetchall()
        return [_decode(row) for row in rows]


"""Provider-agnostic dispatcher for durable proactive notifications.

No provider credentials or provider implementation is included here. Integrate a
real push/VoIP/in-app provider that implements NotificationProvider, register only
providers whose credentials and destination configuration have been verified, and
supply an authoritative incident-actionability check before delivery.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Callable, Mapping, Protocol

from .outbox import ClaimedNotification, NotificationJob, NotificationOutbox


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DeliveryReceipt:
    """Receipt returned by a real provider after it accepts a delivery request."""

    provider_message_id: str | None


class NotificationProvider(Protocol):
    """Adapter contract for a real provider such as push, in-app, or VoIP."""

    def deliver(self, job: NotificationJob, *, idempotency_key: str) -> DeliveryReceipt:
        """Send through the configured provider; never fabricate a receipt."""
        ...


class DispatchConfigurationError(RuntimeError):
    """Required dispatcher policy configuration is missing or invalid."""


class ProviderDeliveryError(RuntimeError):
    """A provider failure expressed as a safe code, never raw credential text."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        error_code: str,
        *,
        retryable: bool,
        retry_after_seconds: int | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if not error_code.strip() or len(error_code) > 120:
            raise ValueError("error_code must be a non-empty safe code of at most 120 characters")
        if retry_after_seconds is not None and not 0 <= retry_after_seconds <= 31_536_000:
            raise ValueError("retry_after_seconds must be between 0 and one year")
        super().__init__(error_code)
        self.error_code = error_code
        self.retryable = retryable
        self.retry_after_seconds = retry_after_seconds


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DispatchPolicy:
    batch_size: int
    lease_seconds: int
    base_retry_delay_seconds: int
    max_retry_delay_seconds: int

    @classmethod
    def from_environment(cls) -> "DispatchPolicy":
        """Load explicit retry/lease policy. Missing values never get defaults."""
        names = {
            "batch_size": "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE",
            "lease_seconds": "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_OUTBOX_LEASE_SECONDS",
            "base_retry_delay_seconds": "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_OUTBOX_RETRY_BASE_SECONDS",
            "max_retry_delay_seconds": "AARIYA_PROACTIVE_OUTBOX_RETRY_MAX_SECONDS",
        }
        parsed: dict[str, int] = {}
        for field, env_name in names.items():
            raw = os.environ.get(env_name, "").strip()
            if not raw:
                raise DispatchConfigurationError(f"{env_name} is required")
            try:
                parsed[field] = int(raw)
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise DispatchConfigurationError(f"{env_name} must be an integer") from exc
        try:
            return cls(**parsed)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise DispatchConfigurationError("Notification dispatcher policy is outside its allowed bounds") from exc

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 1 <= self.batch_size <= 500:
            raise ValueError("batch_size must be between 1 and 500")
        if not 1 <= self.lease_seconds <= 86_400:
            raise ValueError("lease_seconds must be between 1 and 86400")
        if not 0 <= self.base_retry_delay_seconds <= 31_536_000:
            raise ValueError("base_retry_delay_seconds must be between 0 and one year")
        if not 0 <= self.max_retry_delay_seconds <= 31_536_000:
            raise ValueError("max_retry_delay_seconds must be between 0 and one year")
        if self.max_retry_delay_seconds < self.base_retry_delay_seconds:
            raise ValueError("max_retry_delay_seconds must be >= base_retry_delay_seconds")

    def retry_delay(self, attempt_number: int, provider_delay: int | None = None) -> int:
        if provider_delay is not None:
            return min(provider_delay, self.max_retry_delay_seconds)
        exponent = min(max(attempt_number - 1, 0), 30)
        return min(self.base_retry_delay_seconds * (2**exponent), self.max_retry_delay_seconds)


class NotificationDispatcher:
    """Dispatch due jobs via configured provider adapters.

    The actionability callback is mandatory so incident-linked alerts can be
    cancelled when their real incident is resolved or no longer requires contact.
    The callback must query authoritative application state, not infer it from the
    notification payload.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        outbox: NotificationOutbox,
        providers: Mapping[str, NotificationProvider],
        policy: DispatchPolicy,
    ) -> None:
        if any(not channel.strip() for channel in providers):
            raise ValueError("provider channel keys must be non-empty")
        self.outbox = outbox
        self.providers = dict(providers)
        self.policy = policy

    def dispatch_due(
        self,
        *,
        can_deliver: Callable[[NotificationJob], bool],
    ) -> dict[str, object]:
        """Attempt the due batch and return counts plus job IDs and safe states."""
        if not callable(can_deliver):
            raise ValueError("can_deliver must be an authoritative incident-state callback")

        claims = self.outbox.claim_due(
            channels=self.providers.keys(),
            limit=self.policy.batch_size,
            lease_seconds=self.policy.lease_seconds,
        )
        results: list[dict[str, str]] = []
        counts = {"provider_accepted": 0, "retry_scheduled": 0, "dead": 0, "cancelled": 0}

        for claim in claims:
            job = claim.job
            try:
                actionable = can_deliver(job)
            except Exception:
                failed = self._record_failure(
                    claim, "eligibility_check_unavailable", retryable=True
                )
                state = "retry_scheduled" if failed.state == "pending" else "dead"
                counts[state] += 1
                results.append({"job_id": job.job_id, "state": state})
                continue

            if not actionable:
                cancelled = self.outbox.cancel_claimed(
                    job_id=job.job_id,
                    attempt_id=claim.attempt_id,
                    reason_code="incident_no_longer_actionable",
                )
                counts["cancelled"] += 1
                results.append({"job_id": cancelled.job_id, "state": cancelled.state})
                continue

            provider = self.providers[job.channel]
            try:
                receipt = provider.deliver(job, idempotency_key=job.job_id)
                if not isinstance(receipt, DeliveryReceipt):
                    raise TypeError("provider adapter did not return DeliveryReceipt")
            except ProviderDeliveryError as exc:
                failed = self._record_failure(
                    claim,
                    exc.error_code,
                    retryable=exc.retryable,
                    provider_delay=exc.retry_after_seconds,
                )
                state = "retry_scheduled" if failed.state == "pending" else "dead"
                counts[state] += 1
                results.append({"job_id": failed.job_id, "state": state})
                continue
            except Exception:
                # Never persist raw exception text; it may contain credentials or payload data.
                failed = self._record_failure(
                    claim, "provider_unexpected_error", retryable=True
                )
                state = "retry_scheduled" if failed.state == "pending" else "dead"
                counts[state] += 1
                results.append({"job_id": failed.job_id, "state": state})
                continue

            # Keep this outside the provider try/except. If persistence fails after a
            # provider accepted the request, leave the lease to expire and retry with
            # the same idempotency key instead of misclassifying a database error as
            # a provider failure. A provider should deduplicate that key when possible.
            accepted = self.outbox.mark_provider_accepted(
                job_id=job.job_id,
                attempt_id=claim.attempt_id,
                provider_message_id=receipt.provider_message_id,
            )
            counts["provider_accepted"] += 1
            results.append({"job_id": accepted.job_id, "state": accepted.state})

        return {"claimed": len(claims), "counts": counts, "results": results}

    def _record_failure(
        self,
        claim: ClaimedNotification,
        error_code: str,
        *,
        retryable: bool,
        provider_delay: int | None = None,
    ) -> NotificationJob:
        return self.outbox.mark_failed(
            job_id=claim.job.job_id,
            attempt_id=claim.attempt_id,
            error_code=error_code,
            retryable=retryable,
            retry_delay_seconds=self.policy.retry_delay(claim.attempt_number, provider_delay),
        )


1. New backend code

server/proactive/fid_enrollment.py

Open the full source code

Uses a short-lived, one-time challenge sent to the submitted FID. The FID must be confirmed by the same authenticated Aariya device before it becomes eligible for notification routing.




server/proactive/push_installation_api.py

Open the full source code

Defines the authenticated registration, challenge-confirmation and current-device revocation endpoints. It requires a real authenticated-device dependency and an app-attestation verification dependency.




server/proactive/push_installation_api_types.py

Open the source code

Shared platform and notification-permission types for the backend API and enrollment service.




server/proactive/fcm_admin_fid.py

Open the full source code

Sends privacy-minimized notifications using the Firebase Admin SDK's FID targeting. It rechecks the current device, incident ownership, consent and access authorization at dispatch time.




server/proactive/notification_worker.py

Open the updated worker

Now selects the FID-based provider and expects a real installation repository and incident-actionability adapter.



2. New Flutter code
These are individual Dart files, not a compressed package.

proactive_push_service.dart

Open the full Flutter source

Handles FID registration updates, OS notification permissions, foreground messages, notification taps, enrollment challenges, app-received receipts and sign-out/consent revocation.




proactive_push_backend_adapter.dart

Open the full adapter source

Connects the push service to Aariya's existing authenticated HTTP client through a small adapter contract. It does not introduce a separate token store or account system.
