to FIDChallenge messageConfirm challenge with current device sessionVerify expiry, ownership and one-time secretActivate installationConfirmed





The challenge secret is short-lived and must never appear in logs. The repository must enforce FID uniqueness, rate limits, one-time confirmation, expiry, revocation and current device ownership. A client-reported OS permission state is not the same thing as the user's explicit consent to receive Aariya's proactive notifications.
4. Integrate this into the existing server/main.py
After creating your existing FastAPI app, register the new routers using the actual Aariya dependencies.
from server.proactive.push_installation_api import (    create_push_installation_router,)from server.proactive.delivery_receipt_api import (    create_delivery_receipt_router,)app.include_router(    create_push_installation_router(        authenticated_device_dependency=(            existing_authenticated_device_dependency        ),        verified_app_attestation_dependency=(            existing_verified_app_check_dependency        ),        enrollment_service=existing_fid_enrollment_service,    ))app.include_router(    create_delivery_receipt_router(        outbox=existing_notification_outbox,        authenticated_device_dependency=(            existing_authenticated_device_dependency        ),        authorization=existing_delivery_receipt_authorization,    ))



The existing_* names are integration points, not existing symbols verified in your unavailable repository. They must be mapped to the real authenticated session, device registry, verified app attestation, enrollment repository and outbox. Do not use an unconditional attestation callback or create a parallel device database.

The enrollment service also requires an explicitly configured expiry, plus generic enrollment-notification text:
AARIYA_FID_ENROLLMENT_TTL_SECONDS=
AARIYA_FID_ENROLLMENT_NOTIFICATION_TITLE=
AARIYA_FID_ENROLLMENT_NOTIFICATION_BODY=


The values must come from your protected deployment configuration. The service rejects an invalid expiry rather than choosing an operational default.
5. Integrate into the existing Flutter app
Initialize Firebase in the existing app bootstrap and construct ProactivePushService only when the current Aariya session is authenticated.
final proactivePush = ProactivePushService(
  backend: AariyaProactivePushBackendAdapter(
    existingAariyaAuthenticatedApi,
  ),
  hasProactiveNotificationConsent:
      existingAariyaPreferences.hasProactiveNotificationConsent,
  onOpenIncident: existingAariyaNavigation.openAuthorizedIncident,
  onForegroundMessage:
      existingAariyaInbox.handleForegroundPush,
  onSafeError: existingAa
riyaStatusReporter.recordSafeError,
  platform: platformForCurrentRuntime,
);

await proactivePush.start();


Those existingAariya... dependencies must be connected to the real application's services. Derive the platform from the actual runtime; for web, provide the real VAPID public key and service-worker configuration.

Also call refreshRegistration() from the existing app lifecycle-resume handler when APNs registration is pending, and call stopForSignOutOrConsentRevocation() before destroying the authenticated session. The notification handler must fetch the incident via Aariya's authenticated API instead of trusting the incoming push payload as authoritative incident data.
6. Test code
The new tests are also available individually:
- FID enrollment tests
- Push installation API tests
- FID provider tests
Run the available backend suite with:
python -m py_compile server/proactive/*.py
pytest -q


The result is 75 passed. I could not run Flutter analysis or native-device tests because the Flutter SDK and actual Aariya Flutter project are not present in the source overlay.
7. What is needed next
The FID provider and app integration code are now prepared, but the complete notification system is not live until these pieces use the real application services:
1. Implement the FID installation repository against Aariya's current identity/device database, including one-time challenge verification and App Check.
2. Register the new routers through the actual FastAPI app lifecycle and replace the bootstrap authentication guard with current session and ownership checks.
3. Implement the adapter to your existing Flutter API client, compile the Dart files, and test the FID challenge/confirmation on an actual development device.
4. Configure the real Firebase project and Application Default Credentials, then verify a live push and its authenticated app-received receipt.
Firebase's current FID guidance and Admin SDK support are documented in the Flutter FCM setup and Python Admin SDK release notes. 

firebase.google.com
+1




The next development milestone is a live end-to-end push test against your actual Aariya backend and Flutter app. That needs the real repository and session/device services. Until those are available and the integration tests pass, these are tested additive source modules—not a claim that Aariya is already sending live alerts.


from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import hmac
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from server.proactive.fid_enrollment import (
    FidEnrollmentError,
    FidEnrollmentService,
    PendingEnrollmentWriteResult,
)
from server.proactive.push_installation_api_types import (
    NotificationPermissionStatus,
    PushPlatform,
)


class Repository:
    def __init__(self):
        self.pending = {}
        self.active = {}
        self.permission_updates = []
        self.cancelled = []
        self.confirmed = []
        self.allow = True

    def create_pending_enrollment(self, **kwargs):
        if not self.allow:
            return None
        current_fid = self.active.get(kwargs['firebase_installation_id'])
        if current_fid == (kwargs['user_id'], kwargs['device_id']):
            return PendingEnrollmentWriteResult(
                status='already_registered',
                updated_at=datetime(2026, 10, 24, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
                expires_at=None,
                should_send_challenge=False,
            )
        for challenge in self.pending.values():
            if (
                challenge['firebase_installation_id'] == kwargs['firebase_installation_id']
                and challenge['user_id'] == kwargs['user_id']
                and challenge['device_id'] == kwargs['device_id']
                and challenge['expires_at'] > datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            ):
                return PendingEnrollmentWriteResult(
                    status='pending_confirmation',
                    updated_at=challenge['updated_at'],
                    expires_at=challenge['expires_at'],
                    should_send_challenge=False,
                )
        self.pending[kwargs['challenge_id']] = dict(kwargs)
        return PendingEnrollmentWriteResult(
            status='created',
            updated_at=datetime(2026, 10, 24, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
            expires_at=kwargs['expires_at'],
            should_send_challenge=True,
        )

    def record_permission_status(self, **kwargs):
        self.permission_updates.append(kwargs)
        return datetime(2026, 10, 24, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc) if self.allow else None

    def confirm_pending_enrollment(self, **kwargs):
        row = self.pending.get(kwargs['challenge_id'])
        if row is None:
            return None
        if row['expires_at'] <= kwargs['now']:
            return None
        if (row['user_id'], row['device_id']) != (kwargs['user_id'], kwargs['device_id']):
            return None
        if not hmac.compare_digest(
            row['challenge_secret_sha256'],
            kwargs['challenge_secret_sha256'],
        ):
            return None
        fid = row['firebase_installation_id']
        self.active[fid] = (kwargs['user_id'], kwargs['device_id'])
        del self.pending[kwargs['challenge_id']]
        self.confirmed.append(kwargs['challenge_id'])
        return datetime(2026, 10, 24, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    def cancel_pending_enrollment(self, *, challenge_id):
        self.cancelled.append(challenge_id)
        self.pending.pop(challenge_id, None)

    def revoke_current_device_installation(self, *, user_id, device_id):
        removed = []
        for fid, owner in list(self.active.items()):
            if owner == (user_id, device_id):
                removed.append(fid)
                del self.active[fid]
        return bool(removed)


class Sender:
    def __init__(self, fail=False):
        self.fail = fail
        self.sent = []

    def send_challenge(self, **kwargs):
        if self.fail:
            raise RuntimeError('provider failure; do not leak text')
        self.sent.append(kwargs)
        return 'provider-receipt-test'


def _service(repo=None, sender=None):
    repo = repo or Repository()
    sender = sender or Sender()
    service = FidEnrollmentService(
        repository=repo,
        sender=sender,
        challenge_ttl_seconds=300,
    )
    return service, repo, sender


def test_fid_is_pending_until_same_device_confirms_challenge():
    service, repo, sender = _service()
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 24, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    result = service.register_or_update_permission(
        user_id='user-a',
        device_id='device-a',
        firebase_installation_id='cABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU',
        platform=PushPlatform.ANDROID,
        permission_status=NotificationPermissionStatus.AUTHORIZED,
        now=now,
    )
    assert result.status == 'pending_confirmation'
    assert len(sender.sent) == 1
    assert 'challenge_secret_sha256' in repo.pending[sender.sent[0]['challenge_id']]
    assert 'challenge_secret' not in repo.pending[sender.sent[0]['challenge_id']]

    message = sender.sent[0]
    confirmed = service.confirm(
        user_id='user-a',
        device_id='device-a',
        challenge_id=message['challenge_id'],
        challenge_secret=message['challenge_secret'],
        now=now + timedelta(seconds=1),
    )
    assert confirmed is not None
    assert 'cABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU' in repo.active


def test_challenge_cannot_be_confirmed_from_different_device():
    service, _repo, sender = _service()
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 24, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    service.register_or_update_permission(
        user_id='user-a', device_id='device-a',
        firebase_installation_id='cABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU',
        platform=PushPlatform.ANDROID,
        permission_status=NotificationPermissionStatus.AUTHORIZED,
        now=now,
    )
    message = sender.sent[0]
    assert service.confirm(
        user_id='user-a', device_id='device-b',
        challenge_id=message['challenge_id'],
        challenge_secret=message['challenge_secret'],
        now=now + timedelta(seconds=1),
    ) is None


def test_active_fid_registration_is_idempotent_and_does_not_resend_challenge():
    service, repo, sender = _service()
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 24, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    fid = 'cABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU'
    first = service.register_or_update_permission(
        user_id='user-a', device_id='device-a',
        firebase_installation_id=fid,
        platform=PushPlatform.ANDROID,
        permission_status=NotificationPermissionStatus.AUTHORIZED,
        now=now,
    )
    msg = sender.sent[0]
    service.confirm(
        user_id='user-a', device_id='device-a',
        challenge_id=msg['challenge_id'],
        challenge_secret=msg['challenge_secret'],
        now=now + timedelta(seconds=1),
    )
    next_result = service.register_or_update_permission(
        user_id='user-a', device_id='device-a',
        firebase_installation_id=fid,
        platform=PushPlatform.ANDROID,
        permission_status=NotificationPermissionStatus.AUTHORIZED,
        now=now + timedelta(seconds=2),
    )
    assert first.status == 'pending_confirmation'
    assert next_result.status == 'already_registered'
    assert len(sender.sent) == 1


def test_denied_permission_updates_status_without_enrolling_fid():
    service, repo, sender = _service()
    result = service.register_or_update_permission(
        user_id='user-a', device_id='device-a',
        firebase_installation_id=None,
        platform=PushPlatform.IOS,
        permission_status=NotificationPermissionStatus.DENIED,
    )
    assert result.status == 'permission_updated'
    assert len(repo.permission_updates) == 1
    assert sender.sent == []


def test_provider_send_failure_cancels_pending_enrollment():
    service, repo, sender = _service(sender=Sender(fail=True))
    with pytest.raises(FidEnrollmentError):
        service.register_or_update_permission(
            user_id='user-a', device_id='device-a',
            firebase_installation_id='cABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU',
            platform=PushPlatform.ANDROID,
            permission_status=NotificationPermissionStatus.AUTHORIZED,
        )
    assert len(repo.cancelled) == 1
    assert not repo.pending


def test_registry_denial_fails_closed():
    repo = Repository()
    repo.allow = False
    service, _, sender = _service(repo=repo)
    result = service.register_or_update_permission(
        user_id='user-a', device_id='device-a',
        firebase_installation_id='cABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU',
        platform=PushPlatform.ANDROID,
        permission_status=NotificationPermissionStatus.AUTHORIZED,
    )
    assert result is None
    assert sender.sent == []


from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from server.proactive.delivery_receipt_api import AuthenticatedDeviceContext
from server.proactive.fid_enrollment import (
    FIDEnrollmentResult,
    FidEnrollmentService,
)
from server.proactive.push_installation_api import create_push_installation_router
from server.proactive.push_installation_api_types import (
    NotificationPermissionStatus,
    PushPlatform,
)


class Registry:
    def __init__(self):
        self.pending = {}
        self.calls = []

    def create_pending_enrollment(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls.append(("begin", kwargs))
        self.pending[kwargs["challenge_id"]] = kwargs
        from server.proactive.fid_enrollment import PendingEnrollmentWriteResult
        return PendingEnrollmentWriteResult(
            status="created",
            updated_at=datetime(2026, 10, 24, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
            expires_at=kwargs["expires_at"],
            should_send_challenge=True,
        )

    def record_permission_status(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls.append(("permission", kwargs))
        return datetime(2026, 10, 24, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    def confirm_pending_enrollment(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls.append(("confirm", kwargs))
        row = self.pending.get(kwargs["challenge_id"])
        if row is None or row["user_id"] != kwargs["user_id"] or row["device_id"] != kwargs["device_id"]:
            return None
        if row["challenge_secret_sha256"] != kwargs["challenge_secret_sha256"]:
            return None
        return datetime(2026, 10, 24, 10, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    def cancel_pending_enrollment(self, *, challenge_id):
        self.pending.pop(challenge_id, None)

    def revoke_current_device_installation(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls.append(("revoke", kwargs))
        return True


class Sender:
    def __init__(self):
        self.sent = []

    def send_challenge(self, **kwargs):
        self.sent.append(kwargs)
        return "provider-test-receipt"


def _client():
    repo = Registry()
    sender = Sender()
    service = FidEnrollmentService(
        repository=repo,
        sender=sender,
        challenge_ttl_seconds=300,
    )
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(create_push_installation_router(
        authenticated_device_dependency=lambda: AuthenticatedDeviceContext(
            user_id="user-authenticated", device_id="device-authenticated"
        ),
        verified_app_attestation_dependency=lambda: {"verified": True},
        enrollment_service=service,
    ))
    return TestClient(app), repo, sender


def test_registration_starts_challenge_and_never_returns_fid_or_secret():
    client, repo, sender = _client()
    response = client.put(
        "/api/proactive/v1/devices/push-installation",
        json={
            "firebase_installation_id": "cABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU",
            "platform": "android",
            "permission_status": "authorized",
        },
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    assert response.json()["status"] == "pending_confirmation"
    assert "firebase_installation_id" not in response.json()
    assert "challenge_secret" not in response.json()
    assert len(sender.sent) == 1
    assert repo.calls[0][1]["user_id"] == "user-authenticated"
    assert repo.calls[0][1]["device_id"] == "device-authenticated"


def test_confirmation_is_bound_to_authenticated_device():
    client, _repo, sender = _client()
    started = client.put(
        "/api/proactive/v1/devices/push-installation",
        json={
            "firebase_installation_id": "cABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU",
            "platform": "android",
            "permission_status": "authorized",
        },
    )
    assert started.status_code == 200
    challenge = sender.sent[0]
    response = client.post(
        "/api/proactive/v1/devices/push-installation/confirm",
        json={
            "challenge_id": challenge["challenge_id"],
            "challenge_secret": challenge["challenge_secret"],
        },
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    assert response.json()["status"] == "confirmed"


def test_bad_fid_is_rejected():
    client, _, _ = _client()
    response = client.put(
        "/api/proactive/v1/devices/push-installation",
        json={
            "firebase_installation_id": "has spaces and /",
            "platform": "android",
            "permission_status": "authorized",
        },
    )
    assert response.status_code == 422


def test_permission_status_can_be_reported_without_fid():
    client, repo, sender = _client()
    response = client.put(
        "/api/proactive/v1/devices/push-installation",
        json={
            "firebase_installation_id": None,
            "platform": "ios",
            "permission_status": "denied",
        },
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["status"] == "permission_updated"
    assert repo.calls[0][0] == "permission"
    assert sender.sent == []


def test_current_device_can_revoke_push_installation():
    client, repo, _ = _client()
    response = client.delete("/api/proactive/v1/devices/push-installation")
    assert response.status_code == 204
    assert repo.calls[0] == (
        "revoke",
        {"user_id": "user-authenticated", "device_id": "device-authenticated"},
    )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import pytest

from server.proactive.dispatcher import ProviderDeliveryError
from server.proactive.fcm_admin_fid import FcmAdminFidProvider, FidPushTarget
from server.proactive.outbox import NotificationJob


@dataclass
class Obj:
    kwargs: dict


class FakeMessaging:
    class Notification:
        def __init__(self, **kwargs): self.kwargs = kwargs

    class AndroidNotification:
        def __init__(self, **kwargs): self.kwargs = kwargs

    class AndroidConfig:
        def __init__(self, **kwargs): self.kwargs = kwargs

    class Aps:
        def __init__(self, **kwargs): self.kwargs = kwargs

    class APNSPayload:
        def __init__(self, **kwargs): self.kwargs = kwargs

    class APNSConfig:
        def __init__(self, **kwargs): self.kwargs = kwargs

    class Message:
        def __init__(self, **kwargs): self.kwargs = kwargs

    def __init__(self, exception=None):
        self.exception = exception
        self.sent = []

    def send(self, message, *, app):
        self.sent.append((message, app))
        if self.exception:
            raise self.exception
        return "projects/real-project/messages/provider-receipt"


class Repo:
    def __init__(self, target):
        self.target = target
        self.invalidated = []

    def resolve_current_authorized_fid_target(self, *, device_id, incident_id):
        return self.target if self.target and self.target.device_id == device_id else None

    def invalidate_fid_if_version_matches(self, *, device_id, installation_version):
        self.invalidated.append((device_id, installation_version))
        return True


def target(**overrides):
    values = dict(
        device_id="device-record",
        user_id="owner",
        incident_owner_user_id="owner",
        firebase_installation_id="cABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU",
        installation_version="version-1",
        active=True,
        trusted=True,
        push_enabled=True,
        push_consent=True,
        incident_access_allowed=True,
    )
    values.update(overrides)
    return FidPushTarget(**values)


def job(**overrides):
    values = dict(
        job_id="job-record",
        dedupe_key="dedupe",
        incident_id="incident-record",
        channel="push",
        recipient_id="device-record",
        payload={"incident_id": "incident-record", "kind": "monitoring_source_health"},
        state="in_flight",
        attempts=1,
        max_attempts=3,
        available_at="2026-10-24T10:00:00+00:00",
        lease_until="2026-10-24T10:01:00+00:00",
        provider_message_id=None,
        provider_accepted_at=None,
        app_received_at=None,
        last_error_code=None,
        created_at="2026-10-24T10:00:00+00:00",
        updated_at="2026-10-24T10:00:00+00:00",
    )
    values.update(overrides)
    return NotificationJob(**values)


class App:
    project_id = "real-project"
    credential = object()


def make_provider(repo, sdk):
    return FcmAdminFidProvider(
        project_id="real-project",
        notification_title="Aariya needs your attention",
        notification_body="Open Aariya to review an update.",
        installation_repository=repo,
        firebase_app=App(),
        messaging_api=sdk,
    )


def test_provider_targets_fid_and_sends_minimal_incident_reference():
    sdk = FakeMessaging()
    provider = make_provider(Repo(target()), sdk)
    receipt = provider.deliver(job(), idempotency_key="job-record")

    assert receipt.provider_message_id.endswith("provider-receipt")
    message, app = sdk.sent[0]
    assert message.kwargs["fid"] == "cABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU"
    assert "token" not in message.kwargs
    assert message.kwargs["data"] == {
        "incident_id": "incident-record",
        "kind": "monitoring_source_health",
        "delivery_id": "job-record",
    }
    assert message.kwargs["notification"].kwargs["title"] == "Aariya needs your attention"
    assert app is provider.firebase_app


def test_provider_fails_closed_if_user_or_consent_check_fails():
    sdk = FakeMessaging()
    provider = make_provider(Repo(target(push_consent=False)), sdk)
    with pytest.raises(ProviderDeliveryError) as error:
        provider.deliver(job(), idempotency_key="job-record")
    assert error.value.error_code == "push_target_not_authorized"
    assert sdk.sent == []


def test_unregistered_fid_is_conditionally_invalidated():
    class UnregisteredError(Exception):
        pass

    sdk = FakeMessaging(exception=UnregisteredError())
    repository = Repo(target())
    provider = make_provider(repository, sdk)
    with pytest.raises(ProviderDeliveryError) as error:
        provider.deliver(job(), idempotency_key="job-record")
    assert error.value.error_code == "fcm_fid_unregistered"
    assert error.value.retryable is False
    assert repository.invalidated == [("device-record", "version-1")]


def test_temporary_provider_failure_is_retryable():
    class UnavailableError(Exception):
        pass

    provider = make_provider(Repo(target()), FakeMessaging(exception=UnavailableError()))
    with pytest.raises(ProviderDeliveryError) as error:
        provider.deliver(job(), idempotency_key="job-record")
    assert error.value.retryable is True


def test_fcm_incident_mismatch_never_sends():
    sdk = FakeMessaging()
    provider = make_provider(Repo(target()), sdk)
    with pytest.raises(ProviderDeliveryError):
        provider.deliver(
            job(payload={"incident_id": "different"}),
            idempotency_key="job-record",
        )
    assert sdk.sent == []

"""Proof-of-reachability enrollment for a Firebase Installation ID (FID).

A client-supplied FID is not automatically trusted. The service stores a
pending claim, sends a one-time challenge to that FID, and activates routing
only after the same authenticated Aariya device confirms the challenge.
The injected repository must use Aariya's real identity/device database.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import secrets
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any, Protocol

from .push_installation_api_types import (
    NotificationPermissionStatus,
    PushPlatform,
)


class FidEnrollmentError(RuntimeError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PendingEnrollmentWriteResult:
    status: str  # created, pending_confirmation, already_registered
    updated_at: datetime
    expires_at: datetime | None
    should_send_challenge: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class FIDEnrollmentResult:
    status: str
    updated_at: datetime
    expires_at: datetime | None = None


class FidEnrollmentRepository(Protocol):
    def create_pending_enrollment(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
        firebase_installation_id: str,
        platform: PushPlatform,
        permission_status: NotificationPermissionStatus,
        challenge_id: str,
        challenge_secret_sha256: str,
        expires_at: datetime,
    ) -> PendingEnrollmentWriteResult | None:
        """Persist/inspect current claim atomically. Return None when unauthorized.

        Must enforce current device ownership/trust and explicit server-side
        push consent; unique FID-to-device binding; per-user/device/FID rate
        limits; and no takeover of an FID bound to another active device. If
        the same FID is already verified for this device, return
        already_registered without creating another challenge. If a valid
        challenge is pending, return pending_confirmation with
        should_send_challenge=False. Otherwise create a pending challenge and
        return created with should_send_challenge=True. Store only the challenge
        hash, never the raw one-time secret.
        """
        ...

    def record_permission_status(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
        platform: PushPlatform,
        permission_status: NotificationPermissionStatus,
    ) -> datetime | None:
        """Persist client-reported OS permission state for an owned device.

        This state must be independent of Aariya's explicit notification
        consent, and a missing FID must not erase an existing FID.
        """
        ...

    def confirm_pending_enrollment(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
        challenge_id: str,
        challenge_secret_sha256: str,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> datetime | None:
        """Atomically verify owner/device/expiry/attempt limits/hash and activate.

        The implementation must use constant-time comparison for secret hashes,
        enforce one-time consumption, reject a revoked device, and write an audit
        record. Return persisted activation time; None means invalid/expired.
        """
        ...

    def cancel_pending_enrollment(self, *, challenge_id: str) -> None:
        """Cancel a challenge after the provider send fails; keep audit history."""
        ...

    def revoke_current_device_installation(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
    ) -> bool:
        ...


class FidChallengeSender(Protocol):
    def send_challenge(
        self,
        *,
        firebase_installation_id: str,
        challenge_id: str,
        challenge_secret: str,
    ) -> str:
        """Send a data-only one-time challenge through the real FCM Admin SDK."""
        ...


class FirebaseAdminFidChallengeSender:
    """Minimal FID challenge sender; no notification text or private data."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        firebase_app: Any,
        messaging_api: Any,
        notification_title: str,
        notification_body: str,
    ):
        if firebase_app is None:
            raise ValueError("Configured Firebase Admin app is required")
        if not notification_title.strip() or len(notification_title) > 100:
            raise ValueError("A generic FID-enrollment title is required")
        if not notification_body.strip() or len(notification_body) > 160:
            raise ValueError("A generic FID-enrollment body is required")
        self._app = firebase_app
        self._messaging = messaging_api
        self._notification_title = notification_title
        self._notification_body = notification_body

    def send_challenge(
        self,
        *,
        firebase_installation_id: str,
        challenge_id: str,
        challenge_secret: str,
    ) -> str:
        message = self._messaging.Message(
            fid=firebase_installation_id,
            notification=self._messaging.Notification(
                title=self._notification_title,
                body=self._notification_body,
            ),
            data={
                "kind": "fid_enrollment_challenge",
                "challenge_id": challenge_id,
                "challenge_secret": challenge_secret,
            },
        )
        response = self._messaging.send(message, app=self._app)
        if not isinstance(response, str) or not response.strip():
            raise FidEnrollmentError("FCM did not return a challenge receipt")
        return response


class FidEnrollmentService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        repository: FidEnrollmentRepository,
        sender: FidChallengeSender,
        challenge_ttl_seconds: int,
    ):
        if isinstance(challenge_ttl_seconds, bool) or not 60 <= challenge_ttl_seconds <= 3600:
            raise ValueError("FID enrollment TTL must be configured between 60 and 3600 seconds")
        self._repository = repository
        self._sender = sender
        self._ttl = challenge_ttl_seconds

    def register_or_update_permission(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
        firebase_installation_id: str | None,
        platform: PushPlatform,
        permission_status: NotificationPermissionStatus,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> FIDEnrollmentResult | None:
        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if current.tzinfo is None or current.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("now must include a timezone")
        current = current.astimezone(timezone.utc)

        if not user_id.strip() or not device_id.strip():
            return None

        if permission_status not in {
            NotificationPermissionStatus.AUTHORIZED,
            NotificationPermissionStatus.PROVISIONAL,
        } or not firebase_installation_id:
            updated = self._repository.record_permission_status(
                user_id=user_id,
                device_id=device_id,
                platform=platform,
                permission_status=permission_status,
            )
            if updated is None:
                return None
            if updated.tzinfo is None or updated.utcoffset() is None:
                raise FidEnrollmentError("Registry returned a naive timestamp")
            return FIDEnrollmentResult(
                status="permission_updated",
                updated_at=updated.astimezone(timezone.utc),
            )

        challenge_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        challenge_secret = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        secret_hash = hashlib.sha256(challenge_secret.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
        expires_at = current + timedelta(seconds=self._ttl)

        persisted_at = self._repository.create_pending_enrollment(
            user_id=user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            firebase_installation_id=firebase_installation_id,
            platform=platform,
            permission_status=permission_status,
            challenge_id=challenge_id,
            challenge_secret_sha256=secret_hash,
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )
        if persisted_at is None:
            return None

        if persisted_at.updated_at.tzinfo is None or persisted_at.updated_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise FidEnrollmentError("Registry returned a naive timestamp")

        if persisted_at.status == "already_registered":
            return FIDEnrollmentResult(
                status="already_registered",
                updated_at=persisted_at.updated_at.astimezone(timezone.utc),
            )

        if persisted_at.status == "pending_confirmation" and not persisted_at.should_send_challenge:
            if persisted_at.expires_at is None or persisted_at.expires_at.tzinfo is None:
                raise FidEnrollmentError("Pending enrollment expiry is missing or invalid")
            return FIDEnrollmentResult(
                status="pending_confirmation",
                updated_at=persisted_at.updated_at.astimezone(timezone.utc),
                expires_at=persisted_at.expires_at.astimezone(timezone.utc),
            )

        if persisted_at.status != "created" or not persisted_at.should_send_challenge:
            raise FidEnrollmentError("Registry returned an invalid enrollment state")

        try:
            self._sender.send_challenge(
                firebase_installation_id=firebase_installation_id,
                challenge_id=challenge_id,
                challenge_secret=challenge_secret,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            try:
                self._repository.cancel_pending_enrollment(
                    challenge_id=challenge_id
                )
            except Exception:
                pass
            raise FidEnrollmentError(
                "FID enrollment challenge could not be sent"
            ) from exc

        return FIDEnrollmentResult(
            status="pending_confirmation",
            updated_at=persisted_at.updated_at.astimezone(timezone.utc),
            expires_at=(persisted_at.expires_at or expires_at).astimezone(timezone.utc),
        )

    def confirm(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
        challenge_id: str,
        challenge_secret: str,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> datetime | None:
        if not challenge_id.strip() or len(challenge_id) > 80:
            return None
        if not challenge_secret.strip() or len(challenge_secret) > 256:
            return None
        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if current.tzinfo is None or current.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("now must include a timezone")

        secret_hash = hashlib.sha256(
            challenge_secret.encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        persisted_at = self._repository.confirm_pending_enrollment(
            user_id=user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            challenge_id=challenge_id,
            challenge_secret_sha256=secret_hash,
            now=current.astimezone(timezone.utc),
        )
        if persisted_at is not None and (
            persisted_at.tzinfo is None or persisted_at.utcoffset() is None
        ):
            raise FidEnrollmentError("Registry returned a naive timestamp")
        return persisted_at.astimezone(timezone.utc) if persisted_at else None

    def revoke_current_device(self, *, user_id: str, device_id: str) -> bool:
        return self._repository.revoke_current_device_installation(
            user_id=user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
        )

from enum import StrEnum


class PushPlatform(StrEnum):
    ANDROID = "android"
    IOS = "ios"
    MACOS = "macos"
    WEB = "web"


class NotificationPermissionStatus(StrEnum):
    AUTHORIZED = "authorized"
    PROVISIONAL = "provisional"
    DENIED = "denied"
    NOT_DETERMINED = "not_determined"
    UNKNOWN = "unknown"

"""Firebase Admin Python provider targeting Firebase Installation IDs (FIDs).

Requires firebase-admin >= 7.5.0. Uses Application Default Credentials and an
injected repository that resolves a current authorized installation at send
 time. Notification text is generic; private evidence remains behind Aariya's
 authenticated incident API.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import os
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Protocol

from .dispatcher import DeliveryReceipt, NotificationJob, ProviderDeliveryError

_PROJECT_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9-]{4,128}$")
_KIND_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{1,64}$")


class FcmFidConfigurationError(RuntimeError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class FidPushTarget:
    device_id: str
    user_id: str
    incident_owner_user_id: str
    firebase_installation_id: str
    installation_version: str
    active: bool
    trusted: bool
    push_enabled: bool
    push_consent: bool
    incident_access_allowed: bool


class FidPushInstallationRepository(Protocol):
    def resolve_current_authorized_fid_target(
        self,
        *,
        device_id: str,
        incident_id: str,
    ) -> FidPushTarget | None:
        """Resolve FID and recheck owner, trust, consent, device and incident ACL."""
        ...

    def invalidate_fid_if_version_matches(
        self,
        *,
        device_id: str,
        installation_version: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """Conditionally disable only the same FID version that failed."""
        ...


class FcmAdminFidProvider:
    """NotificationProvider implementation using FCM's recommended FID target."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        project_id: str,
        notification_title: str,
        notification_body: str,
        installation_repository: FidPushInstallationRepository,
        firebase_app: Any,
        messaging_api: Any | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if not isinstance(project_id, str) or not _PROJECT_ID_RE.fullmatch(project_id):
            raise FcmFidConfigurationError("A valid Firebase project ID is required")
        if not isinstance(notification_title, str) or not notification_title.strip() or len(notification_title) > 100:
            raise FcmFidConfigurationError("Generic notification title is required (max 100 chars)")
        if not isinstance(notification_body, str) or not notification_body.strip() or len(notification_body) > 240:
            raise FcmFidConfigurationError("Generic notification body is required (max 240 chars)")
        if firebase_app is None:
            raise FcmFidConfigurationError("A configured Firebase Admin app is required")

        self.project_id = project_id
        self.notification_title = notification_title
        self.notification_body = notification_body
        self.installation_repository = installation_repository
        self.firebase_app = firebase_app
        if messaging_api is None:
            try:
                from firebase_admin import messaging as messaging_module
            except ImportError as exc:
                raise FcmFidConfigurationError(
                    "Install firebase-admin>=7.5.0,<8 for FID delivery"
                ) from exc
            messaging_api = messaging_module
        self.messaging = messaging_api

    @classmethod
    def from_environment(
        cls,
        *,
        installation_repository: FidPushInstallationRepository,
    ) -> "FcmAdminFidProvider":
        project_id = os.environ.get("AARIYA_FCM_PROJECT_ID", "").strip()
        title = os.environ.get("AARIYA_FCM_NOTIFICATION_TITLE", "").strip()
        body = os.environ.get("AARIYA_FCM_NOTIFICATION_BODY", "").strip()

        if not project_id or not title or not body:
            raise FcmFidConfigurationError(
                "AARIYA_FCM_PROJECT_ID, AARIYA_FCM_NOTIFICATION_TITLE, "
                "and AARIYA_FCM_NOTIFICATION_BODY are required"
            )

        try:
            import firebase_admin
            from firebase_admin import credentials
        except ImportError as exc:
            raise FcmFidConfigurationError(
                "Install firebase-admin>=7.5.0,<8 for FID delivery"
            ) from exc

        app_name = "aariya-proactive-push"
        try:
            app = firebase_admin.get_app(app_name)
        except ValueError:
            try:
                app = firebase_admin.initialize_app(
                    credential=credentials.ApplicationDefault(),
                    options={"projectId": project_id},
                    name=app_name,
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                raise FcmFidConfigurationError(
                    "Firebase Admin could not initialize using Application Default Credentials"
                ) from exc

        configured_project = getattr(app, "project_id", None)
        if configured_project and configured_project != project_id:
            raise FcmFidConfigurationError(
                "Existing Firebase Admin app uses a different project ID"
            )

        return cls(
            project_id=project_id,
            notification_title=title,
            notification_body=body,
            installation_repository=installation_repository,
            firebase_app=app,
        )

    def check_credentials(self) -> None:
        """Obtain an ADC access token before accepting work into the worker loop."""
        try:
            credential = self.firebase_app.credential
            access = credential.get_access_token()
            token = getattr(access, "access_token", None)
            if not isinstance(token, str) or not token:
                raise ValueError("No access token")
        except Exception as exc:
            raise FcmFidConfigurationError(
                "Firebase Admin credentials are unavailable"
            ) from exc

    def _resolve_target(self, job: NotificationJob) -> FidPushTarget:
        if job.channel != "push":
            raise ProviderDeliveryError("fcm_channel_not_supported", retryable=False)
        if not job.incident_id:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError("fcm_incident_reference_required", retryable=False)
        if job.payload.get("incident_id") != job.incident_id:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError("fcm_incident_reference_mismatch", retryable=False)

        try:
            target = self.installation_repository.resolve_current_authorized_fid_target(
                device_id=job.recipient_id,
                incident_id=job.incident_id,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ProviderDeliveryError("push_target_lookup_failed", retryable=True) from exc

        if target is None or (
            target.device_id != job.recipient_id
            or not target.user_id.strip()
            or target.user_id != target.incident_owner_user_id
            or not target.firebase_installation_id.strip()
            or not target.installation_version.strip()
            or not target.active
            or not target.trusted
            or not target.push_enabled
            or not target.push_consent
            or not target.incident_access_allowed
        ):
            raise ProviderDeliveryError("push_target_not_authorized", retryable=False)
        return target

    @staticmethod
    def _collapse_id(job: NotificationJob) -> str:
        raw = f"{job.incident_id}:{job.job_id}".encode("utf-8")
        return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()[:32]

    def deliver(
        self,
        job: NotificationJob,
        *,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> DeliveryReceipt:
        target = self._resolve_target(job)
        kind_value = job.payload.get("kind", "incident")
        kind = kind_value if isinstance(kind_value, str) and _KIND_RE.fullmatch(kind_value) else "incident"
        delivery_id = idempotency_key
        collapse_id = self._collapse_id(job)

        try:
            message = self.messaging.Message(
                data={
                    "incident_id": job.incident_id,
                    "kind": kind,
                    "delivery_id": delivery_id,
                },
                fid=target.firebase_installation_id,
                notification=self.messaging.Notification(
                    title=self.notification_title,
                    body=self.notification_body,
                ),
                android=self.messaging.AndroidConfig(
                    priority="high",
                    collapse_key=collapse_id,
                    notification=self.messaging.AndroidNotification(
                        tag=collapse_id,
                    ),
                ),
                apns=self.messaging.APNSConfig(
                    headers={
                        "apns-priority": "10",
                        "apns-collapse-id": collapse_id,
                    },
                    payload=self.messaging.APNSPayload(
                        aps=self.messaging.Aps(sound="default")
                    ),
                ),
            )
            provider_message_id = self.messaging.send(
                message,
                app=self.firebase_app,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            name = type(exc).__name__

            if name == "UnregisteredError":
                try:
                    self.installation_repository.invalidate_fid_if_version_matches(
                        device_id=target.device_id,
                        installation_version=target.installation_version,
                    )
                except Exception:
                    # Do not mask the provider failure; stale FID remains blocked
                    # by the send error and the next attempt re-resolves access.
                    pass
                raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                    "fcm_fid_unregistered", retryable=False
                ) from exc

            if name in {"QuotaExceededError", "UnavailableError", "InternalError", "DeadlineExceededError"}:
                raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                    "fcm_temporary_failure", retryable=True
                ) from exc

            if name in {"InvalidArgumentError", "SenderIdMismatchError", "ThirdPartyAuthError", "AuthenticationError"}:
                raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                    "fcm_rejected_request", retryable=False
                ) from exc

            # Unknown exceptions are retried within the outbox's bounded attempt
            # policy. Raw exception text is never logged or persisted.
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_unclassified_failure", retryable=True
            ) from exc

        if not isinstance(provider_message_id, str) or not provider_message_id.strip():
            raise ProviderDeliveryError(
                "fcm_missing_provider_receipt", retryable=True
            )

        return DeliveryReceipt(provider_message_id=provider_message_id)

1. New source files
These are individual source files, not a compressed package.

call_lifecycle.py — persistent call state machine

Open the complete Python source

Stores call requests and an append-only call-event history. Supports idempotent creation, ringing, answering, declining, ending, expiry, and controlled room access. Call state changes are validated rather than accepted as arbitrary updates.




livekit_tokens.py — restricted audio-room access

Open the complete Python source

Uses the LiveKit server SDK to create a short-lived JWT scoped to one opaque Aariya call room. The participant cannot administer rooms, list rooms, create rooms, or publish arbitrary data. LiveKit's tokens encode participant identity and room permissions, and their secrets must remain server-side. 

LiveKit Documentation







call_api.py — authenticated call controls

Open the complete Python source

Adds routes for the assigned device to report its call UI, answer, decline, end, and inspect a call. Access-token issuance is bound to the authenticated user and assigned device.



The corresponding tests are available individually:
- Call lifecycle tests
- LiveKit token tests
- Call API tests
2. LiveKit token code
This is the complete server/proactive/livekit_tokens.py implementation.
"""Restricted LiveKit room token issuer for Aariya voice sessions."""from __future__ import annotationsimport osfrom urllib.parse import urlsplitclass LiveKitConfigurationError(RuntimeError):    passclass LiveKitTokenIssuer:    def __init__(        self,        *,        server_url: str,        api_key: str,        api_secret: str,    ):        parsed = urlsplit(server_url)        if parsed.scheme not in {"wss", "https"} or not parsed.hostname:            raise LiveKitConfigurationError(                "LIVEKIT_URL must be an absolute wss:// or https:// URL"            )        if (            parsed.username            or parsed.password            or parsed.query            or parsed.fragment        ):            raise LiveKitConfigurationError(                "LIVEKIT_URL must not contain credentials, query or fragment"            )



3. Add the dependency and configuration
The current LiveKit Python server API is installed through livekit-api. The overlay specifies version 1.2.2 or a compatible release in the same major version. 

PyPI




The entry in requirements-proactive.txt is:
# Required only for LiveKit room tokens for in-app voice calls.
livekit-api>=1.2.2,<2


Add the following to the protected backend environment, not the Flutter application:
LIVEKIT_URL=
LIVEKIT_API_KEY=
LIVEKIT_API_SECRET=


Do not place LIVEKIT_API_SECRET in Dart, frontend environment variables, or mobile application assets.
4. Register the call API in Aariya's existing FastAPI app
Call routing should attach to your existing app. It should not create a second Aariya application.
from server.proactive.call_api import create_call_routerfrom server.proactive.call_lifecycle import (    CallCoordinator,    CallStore,)from server.proactive.livekit_tokens import LiveKitTokenIssuercall_store = CallStore(existing_database_path)call_coordinator = CallCoordinator(    store=call_store,    authorizer=existing_call_authorization_service,    token_issuer=LiveKitTokenIssuer.from_environment(),)app.include_router(    create_call_router(        coordinator=call_coordinator,        authenticated_device_dependency=(            existing_authenticated_device_dependency        ),    ))



The existing_* names are integration points, not symbols verified in your live repository. They must be mapped to the real Aariya database path, authenticated device dependency, and authorization service. The authorization service must recheck incident ownership, the current escalation decision, call consent, device trust, and revocation status.

historyDeclinesNo answerAnswers





The call state machine and restricted room-token issuance are implemented. There are still three major pieces before this sequence can operate end to end.

First, incoming-call delivery. A normal FCM notification is not sufficient for reliable native incoming-call handling on iOS when the app is terminated. iOS VoIP calls need a PushKit/CallKit implementation; Android needs its native incoming-call/Telecom integration. Apple's requirements are documented in its PushKit guidance. 

Apple Developer Documentation




Second, the real Aariya voice bridge. The LiveKit room needs an audio participant connected to the existing Aariya brain. A new room is not a substitute for your cognitive system: the bridge must route speech into your current recognition/understanding pipeline and return voice through the existing voice director and TTS components. 

GitHub




Third, verified provider events. LiveKit or the native call provider must report the actual room join, disconnect, and call lifecycle. These callbacks must be authenticated and idempotent. A call must not become active merely because someone requested a token.
6. Verification and next development stage
Run the current overlay's tests:
python -m py_compile server/proactive/*.py
pytest -q


The observed result is 90 tests passed. They validate call-state transitions, expiry, duplicate requests, room-token restrictions and authenticated API behavior through isolated test adapters. No real LiveKit server or native device call has been tested.

The next implementation is the incoming-call invitation transport: a durable call-invitation job, a real iOS PushKit/APNs path, an Android native incoming-call path, and verified callback handling. After that, the existing Aariya conversation runtime can be attached to LiveKit.

The live Aariya server/main.py, actual device/authentication services and Flutter app are still not available in this session, so these files remain an additive source overlay rather than a verified merge into your production codebase.


"""Authenticated application API for answering and joining Aariya calls.

Call creation and VoIP invitation dispatch are internal operations. This router
only exposes actions for an already authenticated user/device.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Callable

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict

from .call_lifecycle import (
    CallAuthorizationDenied,
    CallConflict,
    CallCoordinator,
    CallError,
    CallNotFound,
    CallRecord,
    InvalidCall,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AuthenticatedCallDevice:
    user_id: str
    device_id: str


class CallStateResponse(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    call_id: str
    state: str
    incident_id: str
    conversation_id: str
    expires_at: str


class CallJoinResponse(CallStateResponse):
    server_url: str
    room_name: str
    participant_identity: str
    access_token: str
    expires_in_seconds: int


def _state_response(record: CallRecord) -> CallStateResponse:
    return CallStateResponse(
        call_id=record.call_id,
        state=record.state,
        incident_id=record.incident_id,
        conversation_id=record.conversation_id,
        expires_at=record.expires_at,
    )


def create_call_router(
    *,
    coordinator: CallCoordinator,
    authenticated_device_dependency: Callable,
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/proactive/v1/calls",
        tags=["Aariya internet voice calls"],
    )

    @router.post("/{call_id}/ringing", response_model=CallStateResponse)
    def call_ui_presented(
        call_id: str,
        principal: AuthenticatedCallDevice = Depends(
            authenticated_device_dependency
        ),
    ) -> CallStateResponse:
        try:
            record = coordinator.user_call_ui_presented(
                call_id=call_id,
                authenticated_user_id=principal.user_id,
                device_id=principal.device_id,
            )
            return _state_response(record)
        except CallNotFound as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Call not found") from exc
        except PermissionError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Call access denied") from exc
        except (CallConflict, InvalidCall) as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    @router.post("/{call_id}/answer", response_model=CallJoinResponse)
    def answer_call(
        call_id: str,
        principal: AuthenticatedCallDevice = Depends(
            authenticated_device_dependency
        ),
    ) -> CallJoinResponse:
        try:
            call = coordinator.user_respond(
                call_id=call_id,
                authenticated_user_id=principal.user_id,
                device_id=principal.device_id,
                response="answer",
            )
            details = coordinator.issue_user_join_details(
                call_id=call.call_id,
                authenticated_user_id=principal.user_id,
                device_id=principal.device_id,
            )
            return CallJoinResponse(
                **_state_response(call).model_dump(),
                server_url=details.server_url,
                room_name=details.room_name,
                participant_identity=details.participant_identity,
                access_token=details.access_token,
                expires_in_seconds=details.expires_in_seconds,
            )
        except CallNotFound as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Call not found") from exc
        except PermissionError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Call access denied") from exc
        except (CallConflict, InvalidCall) as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except CallError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Call transition rejected") from exc

    @router.post("/{call_id}/decline", response_model=CallStateResponse)
    def decline_call(
        call_id: str,
        principal: AuthenticatedCallDevice = Depends(
            authenticated_device_dependency
        ),
    ) -> CallStateResponse:
        try:
            record = coordinator.user_respond(
                call_id=call_id,
                authenticated_user_id=principal.user_id,
                device_id=principal.device_id,
                response="decline",
            )
            return _state_response(record)
        except CallNotFound as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Call not found") from exc
        except PermissionError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Call access denied") from exc
        except (CallConflict, InvalidCall) as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    @router.post("/{call_id}/end", response_model=CallStateResponse)
    def end_call(
        call_id: str,
        principal: AuthenticatedCallDevice = Depends(
            authenticated_device_dependency
        ),
    ) -> CallStateResponse:
        try:
            record = coordinator.user_end_call(
                call_id=call_id,
                authenticated_user_id=principal.user_id,
                device_id=principal.device_id,
            )
            return _state_response(record)
        except CallNotFound as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Call not found") from exc
        except PermissionError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Call access denied") from exc
        except (CallConflict, InvalidCall) as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    @router.get("/{call_id}", response_model=CallStateResponse)
    def get_call(
        call_id: str,
        principal: AuthenticatedCallDevice = Depends(
            authenticated_device_dependency
        ),
    ) -> CallStateResponse:
        try:
            call = coordinator.store.get(call_id)
            coordinator.authorizer.authorize_user_action(
                authenticated_user_id=principal.user_id,
                device_id=principal.device_id,
                call=call,
                action="read",
            )
            if call.user_id != principal.user_id or call.device_id != principal.device_id:
                raise CallAuthorizationDenied("Call is not assigned to this user/device")
            return _state_response(call)
        except CallNotFound as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Call not found") from exc
        except PermissionError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Call access denied") from exc

    return router


"""Restricted LiveKit room token issuer for Aariya voice sessions."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from urllib.parse import urlsplit


class LiveKitConfigurationError(RuntimeError):
    pass


class LiveKitTokenIssuer:
    def __init__(self, *, server_url: str, api_key: str, api_secret: str):
        parsed = urlsplit(server_url)
        if parsed.scheme not in {"wss", "https"} or not parsed.hostname:
            raise LiveKitConfigurationError(
                "LIVEKIT_URL must be an absolute wss:// or https:// URL"
            )
        if parsed.username or parsed.password or parsed.query or parsed.fragment:
            raise LiveKitConfigurationError(
                "LIVEKIT_URL must not contain credentials, query or fragment"
            )
        if not api_key.strip() or not api_secret.strip():
            raise LiveKitConfigurationError(
                "LiveKit API key and secret are required"
            )
        self._server_url = server_url.rstrip("/")
        self._api_key = api_key
        self._api_secret = api_secret

    @classmethod
    def from_environment(cls) -> "LiveKitTokenIssuer":
        values = {
            "LIVEKIT_URL": os.environ.get("LIVEKIT_URL", "").strip(),
            "LIVEKIT_API_KEY": os.environ.get("LIVEKIT_API_KEY", "").strip(),
            "LIVEKIT_API_SECRET": os.environ.get("LIVEKIT_API_SECRET", ""),
        }
        missing = [name for name, value in values.items() if not value]
        if missing:
            raise LiveKitConfigurationError(
                "Missing required LiveKit configuration: " + ", ".join(missing)
            )
        return cls(
            server_url=values["LIVEKIT_URL"],
            api_key=values["LIVEKIT_API_KEY"],
            api_secret=values["LIVEKIT_API_SECRET"],
        )

    @property
    def server_url(self) -> str:
        return self._server_url

    def issue(self, *, room_name: str, participant_identity: str) -> str:
        if not room_name.startswith("aariya-call-") or len(room_name) > 100:
            raise ValueError("Invalid Aariya call room identifier")
        if not participant_identity or len(participant_identity) > 160:
            raise ValueError("Invalid participant identity")

        try:
            from datetime import timedelta
            from livekit import api
        except ImportError as exc:
            raise LiveKitConfigurationError(
                "Install livekit-api to issue call tokens"
            ) from exc

        # Least privilege: participant can join this room and exchange audio,
        # but cannot administer or enumerate rooms, nor publish arbitrary data.
        grants = api.VideoGrants(
            room_join=True,
            room=room_name,
            can_publish=True,
            can_subscribe=True,
            can_publish_data=False,
            room_admin=False,
            room_create=False,
            room_list=False,
        )
        return (
            api.AccessToken(self._api_key, self._api_secret)
            .with_identity(participant_identity)
            .with_grants(grants)
            .with_ttl(timedelta(minutes=5))
            .to_jwt()
        )


"""Durable lifecycle for Aariya-initiated in-app voice calls.

This module stores call state and issues narrowly scoped room-join requests
through injected, trusted adapters. It does not send VoIP pushes or implement
Aariya's speech/voice runtime by itself.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sqlite3
import uuid
from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Protocol


class CallError(RuntimeError):
    pass


class CallNotFound(CallError):
    pass


class CallConflict(CallError):
    pass


class CallAuthorizationDenied(PermissionError):
    pass


class InvalidCall(ValueError):
    pass


TERMINAL_STATES = frozenset({
    "declined", "no_answer", "ended", "failed", "cancelled", "expired",
})

ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {
    "requested": frozenset({"ringing", "cancelled", "failed", "expired"}),
    "ringing": frozenset({"answered", "declined", "no_answer", "cancelled", "failed", "expired"}),
    "answered": frozenset({"active", "ended", "failed"}),
    "active": frozenset({"ended", "failed"}),
    "declined": frozenset(),
    "no_answer": frozenset(),
    "ended": frozenset(),
    "failed": frozenset(),
    "cancelled": frozenset(),
    "expired": frozenset(),
}


def utc_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def iso_utc(value: datetime) -> str:
    if value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None:
        raise InvalidCall("Timestamps must be timezone-aware")
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="microseconds")


def _json(value: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))


def _fingerprint(values: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(_json(values).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CallRecord:
    call_id: str
    idempotency_key: str
    request_fingerprint: str
    incident_id: str
    escalation_decision_id: str
    user_id: str
    device_id: str
    conversation_id: str
    room_name: str
    reason_code: str
    state: str
    provider_call_id: str | None
    expires_at: str
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str
    answered_at: str | None
    ended_at: str | None
    last_error_code: str | None

    @classmethod
    def from_row(cls, row: sqlite3.Row) -> "CallRecord":
        return cls(**{field: row[field] for field in cls.__dataclass_fields__})


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CallEvent:
    event_id: str
    call_id: str
    provider_event_id: str | None
    actor_type: str
    actor_id: str
    from_state: str
    to_state: str
    event_type: str
    metadata: dict[str, Any]
    created_at: str


class CallAuthorizer(Protocol):
    def authorize_proactive_call(
        self,
        *, user_id: str, device_id: str, incident_id: str,
        escalation_decision_id: str,
    ) -> None:
        """Verify current ownership, device trust, consent, call policy and incident access."""
        ...

    def authorize_user_join(
        self, *, authenticated_user_id: str, call: CallRecord,
    ) -> None:
        ...

    def authorize_user_action(
        self, *, authenticated_user_id: str, device_id: str,
        call: CallRecord, action: str,
    ) -> None:
        """Verify current user ownership, active device and call permissions."""
        ...

    def authorize_agent_join(self, *, call: CallRecord) -> None:
        """Must only be callable by the authenticated internal voice worker."""
        ...


class JoinTokenIssuer(Protocol):
    @property
    def server_url(self) -> str: ...

    def issue(self, *, room_name: str, participant_identity: str) -> str:
        ...


class CallStore:
    """SQLite persistence for call requests and state transition history."""

    def __init__(self, database_path: Path):
        if not database_path.is_absolute() or str(database_path) == ":memory:":
            raise ValueError("A file-backed absolute database path is required")
        self.database_path = database_path
        self.database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            db.executescript("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_calls (
                    call_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                    request_fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL,
                    incident_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    escalation_decision_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    device_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    conversation_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    room_name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                    reason_code TEXT NOT NULL,
                    state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN (
                        'requested','ringing','answered','active','declined',
                        'no_answer','ended','failed','cancelled','expired'
                    )),
                    provider_call_id TEXT,
                    expires_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    answered_at TEXT,
                    ended_at TEXT,
                    last_error_code TEXT
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_proactive_calls_user_state
                    ON proactive_calls(user_id, state, created_at DESC);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_call_events (
                    event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    call_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES proactive_calls(call_id),
                    provider_event_id TEXT UNIQUE,
                    actor_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(actor_type IN (
                        'system','user','provider','voice_runtime'
                    )),
                    actor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    from_state TEXT NOT NULL,
                    to_state TEXT NOT NULL,
                    event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                    metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_proactive_call_events_call_time
                    ON proactive_call_events(call_id, created_at);
            """)

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        db = sqlite3.connect(self.database_path, timeout=5, isolation_level=None)
        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
        db.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000")
        db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
        return db

    def create_request(
        self, *, idempotency_key: str, incident_id: str,
        escalation_decision_id: str, user_id: str, device_id: str,
        conversation_id: str, reason_code: str, expires_at: datetime,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[CallRecord, bool]:
        fields = {
            "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
            "incident_id": incident_id,
            "escalation_decision_id": escalation_decision_id,
            "user_id": user_id,
            "device_id": device_id,
            "conversation_id": conversation_id,
            "reason_code": reason_code,
        }
        for key, value in fields.items():
            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip() or len(value) > 512:
                raise InvalidCall(f"{key} must be a non-empty string of at most 512 characters")
        current = now or utc_now()
        expires_text = iso_utc(expires_at)
        if expires_at.astimezone(timezone.utc) <= current.astimezone(timezone.utc):
            raise InvalidCall("Call invitation expiry must be in the future")
        fingerprint = _fingerprint({**fields, "expires_at": expires_text})
        timestamp = iso_utc(current)
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            existing = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE idempotency_key = ?",
                (idempotency_key,),
            ).fetchone()
            if existing:
                if existing["request_fingerprint"] != fingerprint:
                    raise CallConflict("Call idempotency key reused with different content")
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return CallRecord.from_row(existing), True

            call_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            room_name = f"aariya-call-{uuid.uuid4().hex}"
            db.execute("""
                INSERT INTO proactive_calls (
                    call_id, idempotency_key, request_fingerprint, incident_id,
                    escalation_decision_id, user_id, device_id, conversation_id,
                    room_name, reason_code, state, provider_call_id, expires_at,
                    created_at, updated_at, answered_at, ended_at, last_error_code
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'requested', NULL, ?, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, NULL)
            """, (
                call_id, idempotency_key, fingerprint, incident_id,
                escalation_decision_id, user_id, device_id, conversation_id,
                room_name, reason_code, expires_text, timestamp, timestamp,
            ))
            self._append_event(
                db, call_id=call_id, actor_type="system", actor_id="call-coordinator",
                from_state="none", to_state="requested", event_type="call_requested",
                metadata={"reason_code": reason_code}, created_at=timestamp,
            )
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return CallRecord.from_row(row), False
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    @staticmethod
    def _append_event(
        db: sqlite3.Connection, *, call_id: str, actor_type: str,
        actor_id: str, from_state: str, to_state: str, event_type: str,
        metadata: dict[str, Any], created_at: str,
        provider_event_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        db.execute("""
            INSERT INTO proactive_call_events (
                event_id, call_id, provider_event_id, actor_type, actor_id,
                from_state, to_state, event_type, metadata_json, created_at
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            str(uuid.uuid4()), call_id, provider_event_id, actor_type,
            actor_id, from_state, to_state, event_type, _json(metadata), created_at,
        ))

    def get(self, call_id: str) -> CallRecord:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone()
        if row is None:
            raise CallNotFound(call_id)
        return CallRecord.from_row(row)

    def transition(
        self, *, call_id: str, target_state: str, actor_type: str,
        actor_id: str, event_type: str, metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
        provider_event_id: str | None = None, provider_call_id: str | None = None,
        error_code: str | None = None, now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[CallRecord, bool]:
        if actor_type not in {"system", "user", "provider", "voice_runtime"}:
            raise InvalidCall("Unsupported actor type")
        if target_state not in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS:
            raise InvalidCall("Unsupported target call state")
        if not actor_id.strip() or not event_type.strip():
            raise InvalidCall("Actor and event type are required")
        stamp = iso_utc(now or utc_now())
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            if provider_event_id:
                previous_event = db.execute(
                    "SELECT * FROM proactive_call_events WHERE provider_event_id = ?",
                    (provider_event_id,),
                ).fetchone()
                if previous_event:
                    if previous_event["call_id"] != call_id:
                        raise CallConflict("Provider event ID reused across calls")
                    row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone()
                    if row is None:
                        raise CallNotFound(call_id)
                    db.execute("COMMIT")
                    return CallRecord.from_row(row), True

            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise CallNotFound(call_id)
            current = row["state"]
            if current == target_state:
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return CallRecord.from_row(row), True
            if target_state not in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[current]:
                raise CallConflict(f"Invalid call transition: {current} -> {target_state}")

            answered_at = stamp if target_state == "answered" else row["answered_at"]
            ended_at = stamp if target_state in TERMINAL_STATES else row["ended_at"]
            db.execute("""
                UPDATE proactive_calls
                SET state = ?, updated_at = ?, answered_at = ?, ended_at = ?,
                    provider_call_id = COALESCE(?, provider_call_id), last_error_code = ?
                WHERE call_id = ? AND state = ?
            """, (
                target_state, stamp, answered_at, ended_at,
                provider_call_id, error_code, call_id, current,
            ))
            self._append_event(
                db, call_id=call_id, actor_type=actor_type, actor_id=actor_id,
                from_state=current, to_state=target_state, event_type=event_type,
                metadata=metadata or {}, created_at=stamp,
                provider_event_id=provider_event_id,
            )
            updated = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return CallRecord.from_row(updated), False
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def expire_due(self, *, now: datetime | None = None, limit: int = 100) -> int:
        """Expire unanswered calls whose configured invitation deadline passed."""
        if not 1 <= limit <= 1000:
            raise ValueError("limit must be between 1 and 1000")
        stamp = iso_utc(now or utc_now())
        db = self._connect()
        expired = 0
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            rows = db.execute(
                """
                SELECT * FROM proactive_calls
                WHERE state IN ('requested', 'ringing')
                  AND expires_at <= ?
                ORDER BY expires_at, call_id
                LIMIT ?
                """,
                (stamp, limit),
            ).fetchall()
            for row in rows:
                db.execute(
                    """
                    UPDATE proactive_calls
                    SET state = 'expired', updated_at = ?, ended_at = ?
                    WHERE call_id = ? AND state = ?
                    """,
                    (stamp, stamp, row["call_id"], row["state"]),
                )
                self._append_event(
                    db,
                    call_id=row["call_id"],
                    actor_type="system",
                    actor_id="call-expiry-worker",
                    from_state=row["state"],
                    to_state="expired",
                    event_type="call_invitation_expired",
                    metadata={"reason_code": "invitation_deadline_elapsed"},
                    created_at=stamp,
                )
                expired += 1
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return expired
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def list_events(self, call_id: str) -> tuple[CallEvent, ...]:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            if not db.execute("SELECT 1 FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone():
                raise CallNotFound(call_id)
            rows = db.execute(
