                "SELECT * FROM proactive_call_events WHERE call_id = ? ORDER BY created_at, event_id",
                (call_id,),
            ).fetchall()
        return tuple(CallEvent(
            event_id=row["event_id"], call_id=row["call_id"],
            provider_event_id=row["provider_event_id"], actor_type=row["actor_type"],
            actor_id=row["actor_id"], from_state=row["from_state"],
            to_state=row["to_state"], event_type=row["event_type"],
            metadata=json.loads(row["metadata_json"]), created_at=row["created_at"],
        ) for row in rows)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CallJoinDetails:
    call_id: str
    state: str
    server_url: str
    room_name: str
    participant_identity: str
    access_token: str
    expires_in_seconds: int


class CallCoordinator:
    def __init__(
        self, *, store: CallStore, authorizer: CallAuthorizer,
        token_issuer: JoinTokenIssuer,
    ):
        self.store = store
        self.authorizer = authorizer
        self.token_issuer = token_issuer

    def request_proactive_call(
        self, *, idempotency_key: str, incident_id: str,
        escalation_decision_id: str, user_id: str, device_id: str,
        conversation_id: str, reason_code: str, expires_at: datetime,
    ) -> tuple[CallRecord, bool]:
        # Authorization must query current incident/policy/device/consent state.
        self.authorizer.authorize_proactive_call(
            user_id=user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            incident_id=incident_id,
            escalation_decision_id=escalation_decision_id,
        )
        return self.store.create_request(
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
            incident_id=incident_id,
            escalation_decision_id=escalation_decision_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            conversation_id=conversation_id,
            reason_code=reason_code,
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )

    def user_call_ui_presented(
        self, *, call_id: str, authenticated_user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
    ) -> CallRecord:
        """Record that the assigned authenticated app reports call UI presented.

        This is not proof that the human saw or acknowledged the call.
        """
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        self.authorizer.authorize_user_action(
            authenticated_user_id=authenticated_user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            call=call,
            action="present_call_ui",
        )
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id or device_id != call.device_id:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("Call is not assigned to this user/device")
        updated, _duplicate = self.store.transition(
            call_id=call_id,
            target_state="ringing",
            actor_type="user",
            actor_id=device_id,
            event_type="device_reported_call_ui_presented",
            metadata={"device_id": device_id},
        )
        return updated

    def user_respond(
        self, *, call_id: str, authenticated_user_id: str,
        device_id: str, response: str,
    ) -> CallRecord:
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        if response not in {"answer", "decline"}:
            raise InvalidCall("response must be answer or decline")
        self.authorizer.authorize_user_action(
            authenticated_user_id=authenticated_user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            call=call,
            action=response,
        )
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id or device_id != call.device_id:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("Call is not assigned to this user/device")
        target = "answered" if response == "answer" else "declined"
        updated, _duplicate = self.store.transition(
            call_id=call_id,
            target_state=target,
            actor_type="user",
            actor_id=device_id,
            event_type=f"user_{response}",
            metadata={"device_id": device_id},
        )
        return updated

    def user_end_call(
        self, *, call_id: str, authenticated_user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
    ) -> CallRecord:
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        self.authorizer.authorize_user_action(
            authenticated_user_id=authenticated_user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            call=call,
            action="end",
        )
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id or device_id != call.device_id:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("Call is not assigned to this user/device")
        updated, _duplicate = self.store.transition(
            call_id=call_id,
            target_state="ended",
            actor_type="user",
            actor_id=device_id,
            event_type="user_ended_call",
            metadata={"device_id": device_id},
        )
        return updated

    def mark_active_after_verified_room_join(
        self, *, call_id: str, verified_room: str,
        verified_participant_identity: str,
    ) -> CallRecord:
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        if verified_room != call.room_name:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("Room does not match the call")
        expected_agent = f"aariya-{call.call_id.replace('-', '')}"
        if verified_participant_identity != expected_agent:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied(
                "Only the configured Aariya voice runtime can activate the call"
            )
        updated, _duplicate = self.store.transition(
            call_id=call_id,
            target_state="active",
            actor_type="voice_runtime",
            actor_id=verified_participant_identity,
            event_type="aariya_voice_runtime_joined",
            metadata={"room_name": call.room_name},
        )
        return updated

    def issue_user_join_details(
        self, *, call_id: str, authenticated_user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
    ) -> CallJoinDetails:
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        self.authorizer.authorize_user_join(
            authenticated_user_id=authenticated_user_id,
            call=call,
        )
        self.authorizer.authorize_user_action(
            authenticated_user_id=authenticated_user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            call=call,
            action="join",
        )
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id or device_id != call.device_id:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("Call is not assigned to this user/device")
        if call.state not in {"answered", "active"}:
            raise CallConflict("User must accept the call before joining")
        token = self.token_issuer.issue(
            room_name=call.room_name,
            participant_identity=f"user-{call.call_id.replace('-', '')}",
        )
        return CallJoinDetails(
            call_id=call.call_id,
            state=call.state,
            server_url=self.token_issuer.server_url,
            room_name=call.room_name,
            participant_identity=f"user-{call.call_id.replace('-', '')}",
            access_token=token,
            expires_in_seconds=300,
        )

    def issue_agent_join_details(
        self, *, call_id: str,
    ) -> CallJoinDetails:
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        self.authorizer.authorize_agent_join(call=call)
        if call.state not in {"answered", "active"}:
            raise CallConflict("The user has not accepted this call")
        identity = f"aariya-{call.call_id.replace('-', '')}"
        token = self.token_issuer.issue(
            room_name=call.room_name,
            participant_identity=identity,
        )
        return CallJoinDetails(
            call_id=call.call_id,
            state=call.state,
            server_url=self.token_issuer.server_url,
            room_name=call.room_name,
            participant_identity=identity,
            access_token=token,
            expires_in_seconds=300,
        )
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from server.proactive.call_api import (
    AuthenticatedCallDevice,
    create_call_router,
)
from server.proactive.call_lifecycle import CallCoordinator, CallStore


class Authorizer:
    def authorize_proactive_call(self, **kwargs):
        return None

    def authorize_user_join(self, *, authenticated_user_id, call):
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id:
            raise PermissionError("wrong user")

    def authorize_user_action(self, *, authenticated_user_id, device_id, call, action):
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id or device_id != call.device_id:
            raise PermissionError("wrong user or device")

    def authorize_agent_join(self, *, call):
        return None


class Issuer:
    server_url = "wss://calls.invalid"

    def issue(self, *, room_name, participant_identity):
        return "test-token"


def setup(tmp_path: Path, principal: AuthenticatedCallDevice | None = None):
    store = CallStore((tmp_path / "calls.sqlite3").resolve())
    coordinator = CallCoordinator(
        store=store, authorizer=Authorizer(), token_issuer=Issuer()
    )
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    call, _ = coordinator.request_proactive_call(
        idempotency_key="key-1",
        incident_id="incident-1",
        escalation_decision_id="decision-1",
        user_id="user-1",
        device_id="device-1",
        conversation_id="existing-conversation-1",
        reason_code="incident_requires_attention",
        expires_at=now + timedelta(minutes=2),
    )
    store.transition(
        call_id=call.call_id,
        target_state="ringing",
        actor_type="provider",
        actor_id="verified-provider-callback",
        event_type="call_ringing",
        provider_event_id="provider-event-1",
    )
    actual_principal = principal or AuthenticatedCallDevice(
        user_id="user-1", device_id="device-1"
    )
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(create_call_router(
        coordinator=coordinator,
        authenticated_device_dependency=lambda: actual_principal,
    ))
    return TestClient(app), call


def test_answer_returns_scoped_join_details(tmp_path):
    client, call = setup(tmp_path)
    response = client.post(f"/api/proactive/v1/calls/{call.call_id}/answer")
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    body = response.json()
    assert body["state"] == "answered"
    assert body["room_name"] == call.room_name
    assert body["access_token"] == "test-token"
    assert body["conversation_id"] == call.conversation_id


def test_unassigned_device_cannot_answer(tmp_path):
    client, call = setup(
        tmp_path,
        AuthenticatedCallDevice(user_id="user-1", device_id="other-device"),
    )
    response = client.post(f"/api/proactive/v1/calls/{call.call_id}/answer")
    assert response.status_code == 403


def test_decline_does_not_provide_join_token(tmp_path):
    client, call = setup(tmp_path)
    response = client.post(f"/api/proactive/v1/calls/{call.call_id}/decline")
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    assert response.json()["state"] == "declined"


def test_expired_call_invitation_transitions_to_expired(tmp_path):
    client, call = setup(tmp_path)
    store = CallStore((tmp_path / "calls.sqlite3").resolve())
    expired = store.expire_due(
        now=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=3)
    )
    assert expired == 1
    assert store.get(call.call_id).state == "expired"

"""Durable lifecycle for Aariya-initiated in-app voice calls.

This module stores call state and issues narrowly scoped room-join requests
through injected, trusted adapters. It does not send VoIP pushes or implement
Aariya's speech/voice runtime by itself.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sqlite3
import uuid
from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Protocol


class CallError(RuntimeError):
    pass


class CallNotFound(CallError):
    pass


class CallConflict(CallError):
    pass


class CallAuthorizationDenied(PermissionError):
    pass


class InvalidCall(ValueError):
    pass


TERMINAL_STATES = frozenset({
    "declined", "no_answer", "ended", "failed", "cancelled", "expired",
})

ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {
    "requested": frozenset({"ringing", "cancelled", "failed", "expired"}),
    "ringing": frozenset({"answered", "declined", "no_answer", "cancelled", "failed", "expired"}),
    "answered": frozenset({"active", "ended", "failed"}),
    "active": frozenset({"ended", "failed"}),
    "declined": frozenset(),
    "no_answer": frozenset(),
    "ended": frozenset(),
    "failed": frozenset(),
    "cancelled": frozenset(),
    "expired": frozenset(),
}


def utc_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def iso_utc(value: datetime) -> str:
    if value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None:
        raise InvalidCall("Timestamps must be timezone-aware")
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="microseconds")


def _json(value: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))


def _fingerprint(values: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(_json(values).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CallRecord:
    call_id: str
    idempotency_key: str
    request_fingerprint: str
    incident_id: str
    escalation_decision_id: str
    user_id: str
    device_id: str
    conversation_id: str
    room_name: str
    reason_code: str
    state: str
    provider_call_id: str | None
    expires_at: str
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str
    answered_at: str | None
    ended_at: str | None
    last_error_code: str | None

    @classmethod
    def from_row(cls, row: sqlite3.Row) -> "CallRecord":
        return cls(**{field: row[field] for field in cls.__dataclass_fields__})


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CallEvent:
    event_id: str
    call_id: str
    provider_event_id: str | None
    actor_type: str
    actor_id: str
    from_state: str
    to_state: str
    event_type: str
    metadata: dict[str, Any]
    created_at: str


class CallAuthorizer(Protocol):
    def authorize_proactive_call(
        self,
        *, user_id: str, device_id: str, incident_id: str,
        escalation_decision_id: str,
    ) -> None:
        """Verify current ownership, device trust, consent, call policy and incident access."""
        ...

    def authorize_user_join(
        self, *, authenticated_user_id: str, call: CallRecord,
    ) -> None:
        ...

    def authorize_user_action(
        self, *, authenticated_user_id: str, device_id: str,
        call: CallRecord, action: str,
    ) -> None:
        """Verify current user ownership, active device and call permissions."""
        ...

    def authorize_agent_join(self, *, call: CallRecord) -> None:
        """Must only be callable by the authenticated internal voice worker."""
        ...


class JoinTokenIssuer(Protocol):
    @property
    def server_url(self) -> str: ...

    def issue(self, *, room_name: str, participant_identity: str) -> str:
        ...


class CallStore:
    """SQLite persistence for call requests and state transition history."""

    def __init__(self, database_path: Path):
        if not database_path.is_absolute() or str(database_path) == ":memory:":
            raise ValueError("A file-backed absolute database path is required")
        self.database_path = database_path
        self.database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            db.executescript("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_calls (
                    call_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                    request_fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL,
                    incident_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    escalation_decision_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    device_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    conversation_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    room_name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                    reason_code TEXT NOT NULL,
                    state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN (
                        'requested','ringing','answered','active','declined',
                        'no_answer','ended','failed','cancelled','expired'
                    )),
                    provider_call_id TEXT,
                    expires_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    answered_at TEXT,
                    ended_at TEXT,
                    last_error_code TEXT
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_proactive_calls_user_state
                    ON proactive_calls(user_id, state, created_at DESC);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proactive_call_events (
                    event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    call_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES proactive_calls(call_id),
                    provider_event_id TEXT UNIQUE,
                    actor_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(actor_type IN (
                        'system','user','provider','voice_runtime'
                    )),
                    actor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    from_state TEXT NOT NULL,
                    to_state TEXT NOT NULL,
                    event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                    metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_proactive_call_events_call_time
                    ON proactive_call_events(call_id, created_at);
            """)

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        db = sqlite3.connect(self.database_path, timeout=5, isolation_level=None)
        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
        db.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000")
        db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
        return db

    def create_request(
        self, *, idempotency_key: str, incident_id: str,
        escalation_decision_id: str, user_id: str, device_id: str,
        conversation_id: str, reason_code: str, expires_at: datetime,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[CallRecord, bool]:
        fields = {
            "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
            "incident_id": incident_id,
            "escalation_decision_id": escalation_decision_id,
            "user_id": user_id,
            "device_id": device_id,
            "conversation_id": conversation_id,
            "reason_code": reason_code,
        }
        for key, value in fields.items():
            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip() or len(value) > 512:
                raise InvalidCall(f"{key} must be a non-empty string of at most 512 characters")
        current = now or utc_now()
        expires_text = iso_utc(expires_at)
        if expires_at.astimezone(timezone.utc) <= current.astimezone(timezone.utc):
            raise InvalidCall("Call invitation expiry must be in the future")
        fingerprint = _fingerprint({**fields, "expires_at": expires_text})
        timestamp = iso_utc(current)
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            existing = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE idempotency_key = ?",
                (idempotency_key,),
            ).fetchone()
            if existing:
                if existing["request_fingerprint"] != fingerprint:
                    raise CallConflict("Call idempotency key reused with different content")
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return CallRecord.from_row(existing), True

            call_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            room_name = f"aariya-call-{uuid.uuid4().hex}"
            db.execute("""
                INSERT INTO proactive_calls (
                    call_id, idempotency_key, request_fingerprint, incident_id,
                    escalation_decision_id, user_id, device_id, conversation_id,
                    room_name, reason_code, state, provider_call_id, expires_at,
                    created_at, updated_at, answered_at, ended_at, last_error_code
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'requested', NULL, ?, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, NULL)
            """, (
                call_id, idempotency_key, fingerprint, incident_id,
                escalation_decision_id, user_id, device_id, conversation_id,
                room_name, reason_code, expires_text, timestamp, timestamp,
            ))
            self._append_event(
                db, call_id=call_id, actor_type="system", actor_id="call-coordinator",
                from_state="none", to_state="requested", event_type="call_requested",
                metadata={"reason_code": reason_code}, created_at=timestamp,
            )
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return CallRecord.from_row(row), False
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    @staticmethod
    def _append_event(
        db: sqlite3.Connection, *, call_id: str, actor_type: str,
        actor_id: str, from_state: str, to_state: str, event_type: str,
        metadata: dict[str, Any], created_at: str,
        provider_event_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        db.execute("""
            INSERT INTO proactive_call_events (
                event_id, call_id, provider_event_id, actor_type, actor_id,
                from_state, to_state, event_type, metadata_json, created_at
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            str(uuid.uuid4()), call_id, provider_event_id, actor_type,
            actor_id, from_state, to_state, event_type, _json(metadata), created_at,
        ))

    def get(self, call_id: str) -> CallRecord:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone()
        if row is None:
            raise CallNotFound(call_id)
        return CallRecord.from_row(row)

    def transition(
        self, *, call_id: str, target_state: str, actor_type: str,
        actor_id: str, event_type: str, metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
        provider_event_id: str | None = None, provider_call_id: str | None = None,
        error_code: str | None = None, now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[CallRecord, bool]:
        if actor_type not in {"system", "user", "provider", "voice_runtime"}:
            raise InvalidCall("Unsupported actor type")
        if target_state not in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS:
            raise InvalidCall("Unsupported target call state")
        if not actor_id.strip() or not event_type.strip():
            raise InvalidCall("Actor and event type are required")
        stamp = iso_utc(now or utc_now())
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            if provider_event_id:
                previous_event = db.execute(
                    "SELECT * FROM proactive_call_events WHERE provider_event_id = ?",
                    (provider_event_id,),
                ).fetchone()
                if previous_event:
                    if previous_event["call_id"] != call_id:
                        raise CallConflict("Provider event ID reused across calls")
                    row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone()
                    if row is None:
                        raise CallNotFound(call_id)
                    db.execute("COMMIT")
                    return CallRecord.from_row(row), True

            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise CallNotFound(call_id)
            current = row["state"]
            if current == target_state:
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return CallRecord.from_row(row), True
            if target_state not in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[current]:
                raise CallConflict(f"Invalid call transition: {current} -> {target_state}")

            answered_at = stamp if target_state == "answered" else row["answered_at"]
            ended_at = stamp if target_state in TERMINAL_STATES else row["ended_at"]
            db.execute("""
                UPDATE proactive_calls
                SET state = ?, updated_at = ?, answered_at = ?, ended_at = ?,
                    provider_call_id = COALESCE(?, provider_call_id), last_error_code = ?
                WHERE call_id = ? AND state = ?
            """, (
                target_state, stamp, answered_at, ended_at,
                provider_call_id, error_code, call_id, current,
            ))
            self._append_event(
                db, call_id=call_id, actor_type=actor_type, actor_id=actor_id,
                from_state=current, to_state=target_state, event_type=event_type,
                metadata=metadata or {}, created_at=stamp,
                provider_event_id=provider_event_id,
            )
            updated = db.execute("SELECT * FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return CallRecord.from_row(updated), False
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def expire_due(self, *, now: datetime | None = None, limit: int = 100) -> int:
        """Expire unanswered calls whose configured invitation deadline passed."""
        if not 1 <= limit <= 1000:
            raise ValueError("limit must be between 1 and 1000")
        stamp = iso_utc(now or utc_now())
        db = self._connect()
        expired = 0
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            rows = db.execute(
                """
                SELECT * FROM proactive_calls
                WHERE state IN ('requested', 'ringing')
                  AND expires_at <= ?
                ORDER BY expires_at, call_id
                LIMIT ?
                """,
                (stamp, limit),
            ).fetchall()
            for row in rows:
                db.execute(
                    """
                    UPDATE proactive_calls
                    SET state = 'expired', updated_at = ?, ended_at = ?
                    WHERE call_id = ? AND state = ?
                    """,
                    (stamp, stamp, row["call_id"], row["state"]),
                )
                self._append_event(
                    db,
                    call_id=row["call_id"],
                    actor_type="system",
                    actor_id="call-expiry-worker",
                    from_state=row["state"],
                    to_state="expired",
                    event_type="call_invitation_expired",
                    metadata={"reason_code": "invitation_deadline_elapsed"},
                    created_at=stamp,
                )
                expired += 1
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return expired
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def list_events(self, call_id: str) -> tuple[CallEvent, ...]:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            if not db.execute("SELECT 1 FROM proactive_calls WHERE call_id = ?", (call_id,)).fetchone():
                raise CallNotFound(call_id)
            rows = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM proactive_call_events WHERE call_id = ? ORDER BY created_at, event_id",
                (call_id,),
            ).fetchall()
        return tuple(CallEvent(
            event_id=row["event_id"], call_id=row["call_id"],
            provider_event_id=row["provider_event_id"], actor_type=row["actor_type"],
            actor_id=row["actor_id"], from_state=row["from_state"],
            to_state=row["to_state"], event_type=row["event_type"],
            metadata=json.loads(row["metadata_json"]), created_at=row["created_at"],
        ) for row in rows)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CallJoinDetails:
    call_id: str
    state: str
    server_url: str
    room_name: str
    participant_identity: str
    access_token: str
    expires_in_seconds: int


class CallCoordinator:
    def __init__(
        self, *, store: CallStore, authorizer: CallAuthorizer,
        token_issuer: JoinTokenIssuer,
    ):
        self.store = store
        self.authorizer = authorizer
        self.token_issuer = token_issuer

    def request_proactive_call(
        self, *, idempotency_key: str, incident_id: str,
        escalation_decision_id: str, user_id: str, device_id: str,
        conversation_id: str, reason_code: str, expires_at: datetime,
    ) -> tuple[CallRecord, bool]:
        # Authorization must query current incident/policy/device/consent state.
        self.authorizer.authorize_proactive_call(
            user_id=user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            incident_id=incident_id,
            escalation_decision_id=escalation_decision_id,
        )
        return self.store.create_request(
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
            incident_id=incident_id,
            escalation_decision_id=escalation_decision_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            conversation_id=conversation_id,
            reason_code=reason_code,
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )

    def user_call_ui_presented(
        self, *, call_id: str, authenticated_user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
    ) -> CallRecord:
        """Record that the assigned authenticated app reports call UI presented.

        This is not proof that the human saw or acknowledged the call.
        """
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        self.authorizer.authorize_user_action(
            authenticated_user_id=authenticated_user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            call=call,
            action="present_call_ui",
        )
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id or device_id != call.device_id:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("Call is not assigned to this user/device")
        updated, _duplicate = self.store.transition(
            call_id=call_id,
            target_state="ringing",
            actor_type="user",
            actor_id=device_id,
            event_type="device_reported_call_ui_presented",
            metadata={"device_id": device_id},
        )
        return updated

    def user_respond(
        self, *, call_id: str, authenticated_user_id: str,
        device_id: str, response: str,
    ) -> CallRecord:
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        if response not in {"answer", "decline"}:
            raise InvalidCall("response must be answer or decline")
        self.authorizer.authorize_user_action(
            authenticated_user_id=authenticated_user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            call=call,
            action=response,
        )
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id or device_id != call.device_id:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("Call is not assigned to this user/device")
        target = "answered" if response == "answer" else "declined"
        updated, _duplicate = self.store.transition(
            call_id=call_id,
            target_state=target,
            actor_type="user",
            actor_id=device_id,
            event_type=f"user_{response}",
            metadata={"device_id": device_id},
        )
        return updated

    def user_end_call(
        self, *, call_id: str, authenticated_user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
    ) -> CallRecord:
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        self.authorizer.authorize_user_action(
            authenticated_user_id=authenticated_user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            call=call,
            action="end",
        )
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id or device_id != call.device_id:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("Call is not assigned to this user/device")
        updated, _duplicate = self.store.transition(
            call_id=call_id,
            target_state="ended",
            actor_type="user",
            actor_id=device_id,
            event_type="user_ended_call",
            metadata={"device_id": device_id},
        )
        return updated

    def mark_active_after_verified_room_join(
        self, *, call_id: str, verified_room: str,
        verified_participant_identity: str,
    ) -> CallRecord:
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        if verified_room != call.room_name:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("Room does not match the call")
        expected_agent = f"aariya-{call.call_id.replace('-', '')}"
        if verified_participant_identity != expected_agent:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied(
                "Only the configured Aariya voice runtime can activate the call"
            )
        updated, _duplicate = self.store.transition(
            call_id=call_id,
            target_state="active",
            actor_type="voice_runtime",
            actor_id=verified_participant_identity,
            event_type="aariya_voice_runtime_joined",
            metadata={"room_name": call.room_name},
        )
        return updated

    def issue_user_join_details(
        self, *, call_id: str, authenticated_user_id: str,
        device_id: str,
    ) -> CallJoinDetails:
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        self.authorizer.authorize_user_join(
            authenticated_user_id=authenticated_user_id,
            call=call,
        )
        self.authorizer.authorize_user_action(
            authenticated_user_id=authenticated_user_id,
            device_id=device_id,
            call=call,
            action="join",
        )
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id or device_id != call.device_id:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("Call is not assigned to this user/device")
        if call.state not in {"answered", "active"}:
            raise CallConflict("User must accept the call before joining")
        token = self.token_issuer.issue(
            room_name=call.room_name,
            participant_identity=f"user-{call.call_id.replace('-', '')}",
        )
        return CallJoinDetails(
            call_id=call.call_id,
            state=call.state,
            server_url=self.token_issuer.server_url,
            room_name=call.room_name,
            participant_identity=f"user-{call.call_id.replace('-', '')}",
            access_token=token,
            expires_in_seconds=300,
        )

    def issue_agent_join_details(
        self, *, call_id: str,
    ) -> CallJoinDetails:
        call = self.store.get(call_id)
        self.authorizer.authorize_agent_join(call=call)
        if call.state not in {"answered", "active"}:
            raise CallConflict("The user has not accepted this call")
        identity = f"aariya-{call.call_id.replace('-', '')}"
        token = self.token_issuer.issue(
            room_name=call.room_name,
            participant_identity=identity,
        )
        return CallJoinDetails(
            call_id=call.call_id,
            state=call.state,
            server_url=self.token_issuer.server_url,
            room_name=call.room_name,
            participant_identity=identity,
            access_token=token,
            expires_in_seconds=300,
        )

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from server.proactive.call_api import (
    AuthenticatedCallDevice,
    create_call_router,
)
from server.proactive.call_lifecycle import CallCoordinator, CallStore


class Authorizer:
    def authorize_proactive_call(self, **kwargs):
        return None

    def authorize_user_join(self, *, authenticated_user_id, call):
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id:
            raise PermissionError("wrong user")

    def authorize_user_action(self, *, authenticated_user_id, device_id, call, action):
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id or device_id != call.device_id:
            raise PermissionError("wrong user or device")

    def authorize_agent_join(self, *, call):
        return None


class Issuer:
    server_url = "wss://calls.invalid"

    def issue(self, *, room_name, participant_identity):
        return "test-token"


def setup(tmp_path: Path, principal: AuthenticatedCallDevice | None = None):
    store = CallStore((tmp_path / "calls.sqlite3").resolve())
    coordinator = CallCoordinator(
        store=store, authorizer=Authorizer(), token_issuer=Issuer()
    )
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    call, _ = coordinator.request_proactive_call(
        idempotency_key="key-1",
        incident_id="incident-1",
        escalation_decision_id="decision-1",
        user_id="user-1",
        device_id="device-1",
        conversation_id="existing-conversation-1",
        reason_code="incident_requires_attention",
        expires_at=now + timedelta(minutes=2),
    )
    store.transition(
        call_id=call.call_id,
        target_state="ringing",
        actor_type="provider",
        actor_id="verified-provider-callback",
        event_type="call_ringing",
        provider_event_id="provider-event-1",
    )
    actual_principal = principal or AuthenticatedCallDevice(
        user_id="user-1", device_id="device-1"
    )
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(create_call_router(
        coordinator=coordinator,
        authenticated_device_dependency=lambda: actual_principal,
    ))
    return TestClient(app), call


def test_answer_returns_scoped_join_details(tmp_path):
    client, call = setup(tmp_path)
    response = client.post(f"/api/proactive/v1/calls/{call.call_id}/answer")
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    body = response.json()
    assert body["state"] == "answered"
    assert body["room_name"] == call.room_name
    assert body["access_token"] == "test-token"
    assert body["conversation_id"] == call.conversation_id


def test_unassigned_device_cannot_answer(tmp_path):
    client, call = setup(
        tmp_path,
        AuthenticatedCallDevice(user_id="user-1", device_id="other-device"),
    )
    response = client.post(f"/api/proactive/v1/calls/{call.call_id}/answer")
    assert response.status_code == 403


def test_decline_does_not_provide_join_token(tmp_path):
    client, call = setup(tmp_path)
    response = client.post(f"/api/proactive/v1/calls/{call.call_id}/decline")
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    assert response.json()["state"] == "declined"


def test_expired_call_invitation_transitions_to_expired(tmp_path):
    client, call = setup(tmp_path)
    store = CallStore((tmp_path / "calls.sqlite3").resolve())
    expired = store.expire_due(
        now=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=3)
    )
    assert expired == 1
    assert store.get(call.call_id).state == "expired"

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
import types

import pytest

from server.proactive.livekit_tokens import (
    LiveKitConfigurationError,
    LiveKitTokenIssuer,
)


class FakeGrants:
    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        self.values = kwargs


class FakeAccessToken:
    last = None

    def __init__(self, api_key, api_secret):
        self.api_key = api_key
        self.api_secret = api_secret
        self.identity = None
        self.grants = None
        self.ttl = None
        FakeAccessToken.last = self

    def with_identity(self, identity):
        self.identity = identity
        return self

    def with_grants(self, grants):
        self.grants = grants
        return self

    def with_ttl(self, ttl):
        self.ttl = ttl
        return self

    def to_jwt(self):
        return "isolated-test-token"


def install_livekit_stub(monkeypatch):
    api_module = types.SimpleNamespace(
        AccessToken=FakeAccessToken,
        VideoGrants=FakeGrants,
    )
    module = types.ModuleType("livekit")
    module.api = api_module
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "livekit", module)


def test_issuer_scopes_token_to_single_room_and_no_admin(monkeypatch):
    install_livekit_stub(monkeypatch)
    issuer = LiveKitTokenIssuer(
        server_url="wss://example.invalid",
        api_key="test-key",
        api_secret="test-secret",
    )
    token = issuer.issue(
        room_name="aariya-call-opaque-room-id",
        participant_identity="user-opaque-participant-id",
    )
    assert token == "isolated-test-token"
    minted = FakeAccessToken.last
    assert minted.grants.values["room_join"] is True
    assert minted.grants.values["room"] == "aariya-call-opaque-room-id"
    assert minted.grants.values["room_admin"] is False
    assert minted.grants.values["room_create"] is False
    assert minted.grants.values["room_list"] is False
    assert minted.grants.values["can_publish_data"] is False
    assert minted.ttl.total_seconds() == 300


def test_issuer_rejects_room_outside_aariya_namespace(monkeypatch):
    install_livekit_stub(monkeypatch)
    issuer = LiveKitTokenIssuer(
        server_url="wss://example.invalid",
        api_key="test-key",
        api_secret="test-secret",
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        issuer.issue(room_name="other-room", participant_identity="participant")


def test_issuer_rejects_url_credentials():
    with pytest.raises(LiveKitConfigurationError):
        LiveKitTokenIssuer(
            server_url="wss://user:password@example.invalid",
            api_key="test-key",
            api_secret="test-secret",
        )

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from server.proactive.call_lifecycle import (
    CallAuthorizationDenied,
    CallConflict,
    CallCoordinator,
    CallStore,
    InvalidCall,
)


class Authorizer:
    def authorize_proactive_call(self, **kwargs):
        return None

    def authorize_user_join(self, *, authenticated_user_id, call):
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("wrong user")

    def authorize_user_action(self, *, authenticated_user_id, device_id, call, action):
        if authenticated_user_id != call.user_id or device_id != call.device_id:
            raise CallAuthorizationDenied("wrong user/device")

    def authorize_agent_join(self, *, call):
        return None


class TokenIssuer:
    server_url = "wss://calls.example.invalid"

    def issue(self, *, room_name, participant_identity):
        return f"test-token:{room_name}:{participant_identity}"


def make_call(tmp_path: Path):
    store = CallStore((tmp_path / "calls.sqlite3").resolve())
    coordinator = CallCoordinator(
        store=store,
        authorizer=Authorizer(),
        token_issuer=TokenIssuer(),
    )
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    call, duplicate = coordinator.request_proactive_call(
        idempotency_key="decision:device:call",
        incident_id="incident-reference",
        escalation_decision_id="decision-reference",
        user_id="opaque-user-id",
        device_id="registered-device-id",
        conversation_id="existing-conversation-id",
        reason_code="critical_incident",
        expires_at=now + timedelta(minutes=2),
    )
    assert duplicate is False
    return store, coordinator, call


def test_call_request_is_durable_and_idempotent(tmp_path):
    store, _coordinator, call = make_call(tmp_path)
    reopened = CallStore(store.database_path)
    record = reopened.get(call.call_id)
    assert record.state == "requested"
    assert record.room_name.startswith("aariya-call-")
    assert record.incident_id == "incident-reference"


def test_reused_idempotency_key_with_changed_request_is_rejected(tmp_path):
    store, _coordinator, call = make_call(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(CallConflict):
        store.create_request(
            idempotency_key=call.idempotency_key,
            incident_id=call.incident_id,
            escalation_decision_id="different-decision",
            user_id=call.user_id,
            device_id=call.device_id,
            conversation_id=call.conversation_id,
            reason_code=call.reason_code,
            expires_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=2),
        )


def test_user_must_answer_before_join_token_is_issued(tmp_path):
    _store, coordinator, call = make_call(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(CallConflict):
        coordinator.issue_user_join_details(
            call_id=call.call_id,
            authenticated_user_id=call.user_id,
            device_id=call.device_id,
        )


def test_wrong_user_cannot_answer_or_get_token(tmp_path):
    store, coordinator, call = make_call(tmp_path)
    store.transition(
        call_id=call.call_id,
        target_state="ringing",
        actor_type="provider",
        actor_id="verified-call-provider",
        event_type="provider_call_ringing",
    )
    with pytest.raises(CallAuthorizationDenied):
        coordinator.user_respond(
            call_id=call.call_id,
            authenticated_user_id="another-user",
            device_id=call.device_id,
            response="answer",
        )


def test_answer_then_issue_room_scoped_user_token(tmp_path):
    store, coordinator, call = make_call(tmp_path)
    store.transition(
        call_id=call.call_id,
        target_state="ringing",
        actor_type="provider",
        actor_id="verified-call-provider",
        event_type="provider_call_ringing",
    )
    answered = coordinator.user_respond(
        call_id=call.call_id,
        authenticated_user_id=call.user_id,
        device_id=call.device_id,
        response="answer",
    )
    assert answered.state == "answered"
    details = coordinator.issue_user_join_details(
        call_id=call.call_id,
        authenticated_user_id=call.user_id,
        device_id=call.device_id,
    )
    assert details.room_name == call.room_name
    assert details.participant_identity.startswith("user-")
    assert details.expires_in_seconds == 300
    assert "opaque-user-id" not in details.participant_identity


def test_state_machine_rejects_answer_from_requested(tmp_path):
    store, _coordinator, call = make_call(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(CallConflict):
        store.transition(
            call_id=call.call_id,
            target_state="answered",
            actor_type="provider",
            actor_id="provider",
            event_type="unverified_answer_event",
        )


def test_duplicate_provider_webhook_is_idempotent(tmp_path):
    store, _coordinator, call = make_call(tmp_path)
    first, duplicate = store.transition(
        call_id=call.call_id,
        target_state="ringing",
        actor_type="provider",
        actor_id="provider",
        event_type="provider_ringing",
        provider_event_id="provider-event-1",
    )
    second, was_duplicate = store.transition(
        call_id=call.call_id,
        target_state="ringing",
        actor_type="provider",
        actor_id="provider",
        event_type="provider_ringing",
        provider_event_id="provider-event-1",
    )
    assert first.state == second.state == "ringing"
    assert duplicate is False
    assert was_duplicate is True
    assert len(store.list_events(call.call_id)) == 2


def test_only_verified_agent_join_can_mark_call_active(tmp_path):
    _store, coordinator, call = make_call(tmp_path)
    _store.transition(
        call_id=call.call_id,
        target_state="ringing",
        actor_type="provider",
        actor_id="provider",
        event_type="ringing",
    )
    coordinator.user_respond(
        call_id=call.call_id,
        authenticated_user_id=call.user_id,
        device_id=call.device_id,
        response="answer",
    )
    with pytest.raises(CallAuthorizationDenied):
        coordinator.mark_active_after_verified_room_join(
            call_id=call.call_id,
            verified_room="wrong-room",
            verified_participant_identity="aariya-voice-runtime",
        )


I’ve added the next layer connecting Aariya’s existing call lifecycle to real iOS and Android delivery providers. The code includes a durable queue, retries, device authorization checks, provider adapters, and authenticated app-receipt endpoints.

1. Durable invitation outbox

Stores invitation status, attempt history, retry schedules, worker leases, expiry, and delivery receipts in SQLite. Duplicate queueing is handled idempotently.




2. Platform-specific transports

iOS uses an APNs VoIP transport for PushKit. Android uses a high-priority FCM wake-up transport so the app can present the call through its native call interface.




3. Authenticated receipt API

An invitation is not marked received merely because the provider accepted the request. The authenticated app must confirm receipt from the correct registered device.



Code files
Delivery queue and dispatcher

Open call_invitation_delivery.py

Persistence, leases, retry handling, expiration, cancellation, status transitions, and receipt auditing.


iOS and Android provider adapters

Open call_invitation_transports.py

APNs PushKit sender, FCM Android wake-up sender, credential configuration, target authorization and stale-token handling.


Authenticated invitation API

Open call_invitation_api.py

Endpoints for acknowledging receipt and reading invitation status from the assigned, authenticated device.


Dependencies

Open requirements-proactive.txt

Optional dependencies for APNs HTTP/2, JWT signing, and Firebase Admin.


How the backend connects the components
Once the call is authorized and created, the backend can enqueue an invitation for the platform recorded in the trusted device registry:
from datetime import datetimefrom pathlib import Pathfrom server.proactive.call_invitation_delivery import (    CallInvitationDispatcher,    InvitationStore,)from server.proactive.call_invitation_transports import (    ApnsVoipTransport,    FcmCallWakeTransport,)invitation_store = InvitationStore(    Path("/var/lib/aariya/call-invitations.sqlite3").resolve())# These repositories must be connected to Aariya's real,# authenticated device registry and current consent records.apns = ApnsVoipTransport.from_environment(    target_repository=real_voip_device_repository)fcm = FcmCallWakeTransport.from_environment(    target_repository=real_fcm_device_repository)dispatcher = CallInvitationDispatcher(    store=invitation_store,    transports={        "ios_pushkit": apns,        "android_fcm_wake": fcm,    },    call_state_reader=lambda call_id: call_store.get(call_id).state,)# `call` must already exist and be authorized.



This is the connection pattern, not a claim that the shown repository names or worker supervisor already exist in your live Aariya source. They must be mapped to the real implementations, and the worker must run under a supervised process rather than being launched ad hoc for each call.
Verification
108 tests passed

Verified with Python compilation and pytest -q in the additive Aariya source overlay.

The tests cover queue persistence, idempotency, retry scheduling, expired calls, wrong-device access, APNs provider responses, FCM provider responses, and delivery receipts. Provider tests use test adapters; no real APNs/FCM delivery or phone call has been verified.

Two platform requirements remain important. On iOS 13 and later, incoming PushKit VoIP notifications must be reported to CallKit. 

Apple Developer Documentation


 On Android, high-priority FCM must lead to a legitimate user-visible call flow; it does not itself implement the native call interface. 

firebase.google.com


"""Durable delivery queue for Aariya incoming-call invitations.

This module deliberately separates *provider acceptance* from *device receipt*.
It contains no fake provider and never claims a device displayed a call merely
because APNs/FCM accepted a request. Platform adapters must be supplied by the
application at runtime.

Supported adapter keys:
  * ``ios_pushkit``: APNs VoIP push to a registered PushKit token; iOS must
    immediately report the call to CallKit.
  * ``android_fcm_wake``: high-priority FCM wake-up/data message; the Android
    app must use Android Telecom/Core-Telecom to present the incoming call.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import sqlite3
import uuid
from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable, Mapping, Protocol

PLATFORMS = frozenset({"ios_pushkit", "android_fcm_wake"})
ACTIVE_CALL_STATES = frozenset({"requested", "ringing"})
TERMINAL_DELIVERY_STATES = frozenset({
    "app_received", "failed", "cancelled", "expired", "dead_letter",
})
ERROR_CODE = re.compile(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{1,96}$")


class InvitationError(RuntimeError):
    pass


class InvitationNotFound(InvitationError):
    pass


class InvitationConflict(InvitationError):
    pass


class InvitationDeliveryError(InvitationError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, *, retryable: bool):
        if not ERROR_CODE.fullmatch(code):
            raise ValueError("Delivery error code must be a short non-sensitive identifier")
        super().__init__(code)
        self.code = code
        self.retryable = retryable


def utc_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def iso_utc(value: datetime) -> str:
    if value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None:
        raise ValueError("Timestamp must be timezone-aware")
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="microseconds")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class InvitationRecord:
    invitation_id: str
    call_id: str
    user_id: str
    device_id: str
    platform: str
    idempotency_key: str
    expires_at: str
    status: str
    attempts: int
    next_attempt_at: str
    lease_owner: str | None
    lease_until: str | None
    provider_message_id: str | None
    last_error_code: str | None
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str

    @classmethod
    def from_row(cls, row: sqlite3.Row) -> "InvitationRecord":
        return cls(**{key: row[key] for key in cls.__dataclass_fields__})


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class InvitationEnvelope:
    """Minimum data required to wake the selected device.

    Do not add incident text, diagnostic details, user names, credentials,
    conversation content, or access tokens to this provider payload.
    """
    invitation_id: str
    call_id: str
    user_id: str
    device_id: str
    platform: str
    expires_at: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ProviderAcceptance:
    provider_message_id: str | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DispatchSummary:
    claimed: int
    accepted: int
    retry_scheduled: int
    terminal: int
    skipped: int


class CallStateReader(Protocol):
    def __call__(self, call_id: str) -> str: ...


class InvitationTransport(Protocol):
    def send(self, envelope: InvitationEnvelope, *, idempotency_key: str) -> ProviderAcceptance:
        """Send using a real provider and a provider-supported idempotency key.

        Raise InvitationDeliveryError with a sanitized code and retryable flag
        for known failures. Never return acceptance before the provider does.
        """
        ...


class InvitationStore:
    """SQLite-backed outbox with leases, retry schedule and receipt audit."""

    def __init__(self, database_path: Path, *, max_attempts: int = 8):
        if not database_path.is_absolute() or str(database_path) == ":memory:":
            raise ValueError("A file-backed absolute database path is required")
        if not 1 <= max_attempts <= 100:
            raise ValueError("max_attempts must be between 1 and 100")
        self.database_path = database_path
        self.max_attempts = max_attempts
        self.database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            db.executescript("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS call_invitation_outbox (
                    invitation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    call_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    device_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    platform TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(platform IN ('ios_pushkit','android_fcm_wake')),
                    idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                    expires_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(status IN (
                        'pending','leased','retry_wait','provider_accepted','app_received',
                        'failed','cancelled','expired','dead_letter'
                    )),
                    attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(attempts >= 0),
                    next_attempt_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    lease_owner TEXT,
                    lease_until TEXT,
                    provider_message_id TEXT,
                    last_error_code TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    UNIQUE(call_id, device_id, platform)
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_invites_due
                    ON call_invitation_outbox(status, next_attempt_at, expires_at);
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS call_invitation_delivery_events (
                    event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    invitation_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES call_invitation_outbox(invitation_id),
                    receipt_id TEXT UNIQUE,
                    event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                    from_status TEXT NOT NULL,
                    to_status TEXT NOT NULL,
                    actor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    details_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_invite_events_time
                    ON call_invitation_delivery_events(invitation_id, created_at);
            """)

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        db = sqlite3.connect(self.database_path, timeout=5, isolation_level=None)
        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
        db.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000")
        db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
        return db

    @staticmethod
    def _append_event(
        db: sqlite3.Connection, *, invitation_id: str, event_type: str,
        from_status: str, to_status: str, actor_id: str,
        details: dict[str, object], created_at: str, receipt_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        db.execute("""
            INSERT INTO call_invitation_delivery_events (
                event_id, invitation_id, receipt_id, event_type, from_status,
                to_status, actor_id, details_json, created_at
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            str(uuid.uuid4()), invitation_id, receipt_id, event_type, from_status,
            to_status, actor_id,
            json.dumps(details, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False),
            created_at,
        ))

    def enqueue(self, *, call_id: str, user_id: str, device_id: str,
                platform: str, expires_at: datetime,
                now: datetime | None = None) -> tuple[InvitationRecord, bool]:
        for name, value in (("call_id", call_id), ("user_id", user_id), ("device_id", device_id)):
            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip() or len(value) > 512:
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a non-empty string of at most 512 characters")
        if platform not in PLATFORMS:
            raise ValueError(f"platform must be one of: {', '.join(sorted(PLATFORMS))}")
        stamp_dt = now or utc_now()
        expires_text = iso_utc(expires_at)
        stamp = iso_utc(stamp_dt)
        if expires_at.astimezone(timezone.utc) <= stamp_dt.astimezone(timezone.utc):
            raise ValueError("Invitation expiry must be in the future")
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            old = db.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE call_id=? AND device_id=? AND platform=?",
                (call_id, device_id, platform),
            ).fetchone()
            if old:
                # Same call/device/platform may be enqueued exactly once; expiry is immutable.
                if old["user_id"] != user_id or old["expires_at"] != expires_text:
                    raise InvitationConflict("Call invitation already exists with different parameters")
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return InvitationRecord.from_row(old), True
            invitation_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            idem = f"call-invite:{invitation_id}"
            db.execute("""
                INSERT INTO call_invitation_outbox (
                    invitation_id, call_id, user_id, device_id, platform,
                    idempotency_key, expires_at, status, attempts, next_attempt_at,
                    lease_owner, lease_until, provider_message_id, last_error_code,
                    created_at, updated_at
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending', 0, ?, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?, ?)
            """, (invitation_id, call_id, user_id, device_id, platform, idem,
                  expires_text, stamp, stamp, stamp))
            self._append_event(
                db, invitation_id=invitation_id, event_type="invitation_enqueued",
                from_status="none", to_status="pending", actor_id="call-invitation-service",
                details={"platform": platform}, created_at=stamp,
            )
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE invitation_id=?",
                             (invitation_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return InvitationRecord.from_row(row), False
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def get(self, invitation_id: str) -> InvitationRecord:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE invitation_id=?",
                             (invitation_id,)).fetchone()
        if row is None:
            raise InvitationNotFound(invitation_id)
        return InvitationRecord.from_row(row)

    def claim_due(self, *, worker_id: str, now: datetime | None = None,
                  limit: int = 20, lease_seconds: int = 30) -> tuple[InvitationRecord, ...]:
        if not worker_id.strip() or len(worker_id) > 128:
            raise ValueError("worker_id is invalid")
        if not 1 <= limit <= 500 or not 5 <= lease_seconds <= 300:
            raise ValueError("limit or lease_seconds is out of range")
        stamp_dt = now or utc_now()
        stamp = iso_utc(stamp_dt)
        lease_until = iso_utc(stamp_dt + timedelta(seconds=lease_seconds))
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            expired = db.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox
                WHERE status IN ('pending','retry_wait','leased') AND expires_at <= ?
            """, (stamp,)).fetchall()
            for row in expired:
                db.execute("""UPDATE call_invitation_outbox
                    SET status='expired', lease_owner=NULL, lease_until=NULL, updated_at=?
                    WHERE invitation_id=?""", (stamp, row["invitation_id"]))
                self._append_event(db, invitation_id=row["invitation_id"],
                    event_type="invitation_expired_before_delivery", from_status=row["status"],
                    to_status="expired", actor_id=worker_id,
                    details={"reason_code": "invitation_deadline_elapsed"}, created_at=stamp)
            exhausted = db.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox
                WHERE status IN ('pending','retry_wait','leased') AND expires_at > ?
                  AND attempts >= ? AND (status != 'leased' OR lease_until <= ?)
            """, (stamp, self.max_attempts, stamp)).fetchall()
            for row in exhausted:
                db.execute("""UPDATE call_invitation_outbox
                    SET status='dead_letter', lease_owner=NULL, lease_until=NULL, updated_at=?
                    WHERE invitation_id=?""", (stamp, row["invitation_id"]))
                self._append_event(db, invitation_id=row["invitation_id"],
                    event_type="invitation_dead_lettered", from_status=row["status"],
                    to_status="dead_letter", actor_id=worker_id,
                    details={"reason_code": "attempt_limit_reached"}, created_at=stamp)
            rows = db.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox
                WHERE expires_at > ? AND (
                    (status IN ('pending','retry_wait') AND next_attempt_at <= ?) OR
                    (status='leased' AND lease_until <= ?)
                ) AND attempts < ?
                ORDER BY next_attempt_at, created_at, invitation_id LIMIT ?
            """, (stamp, stamp, stamp, self.max_attempts, limit)).fetchall()
            records: list[InvitationRecord] = []
            for row in rows:
                old_status = row["status"]
                db.execute("""UPDATE call_invitation_outbox
                    SET status='leased', attempts=attempts+1, lease_owner=?, lease_until=?, updated_at=?
                    WHERE invitation_id=?""", (worker_id, lease_until, stamp, row["invitation_id"]))
                self._append_event(db, invitation_id=row["invitation_id"],
                    event_type="invitation_claimed", from_status=old_status,
                    to_status="leased", actor_id=worker_id,
                    details={"attempt": row["attempts"] + 1}, created_at=stamp)
                updated = db.execute("SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE invitation_id=?",
                                     (row["invitation_id"],)).fetchone()
                records.append(InvitationRecord.from_row(updated))
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return tuple(records)
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def mark_provider_accepted(self, *, invitation_id: str, worker_id: str,
                               provider_message_id: str | None,
                               now: datetime | None = None) -> InvitationRecord:
        stamp = iso_utc(now or utc_now())
        if provider_message_id is not None and (not provider_message_id.strip() or len(provider_message_id) > 512):
            raise ValueError("provider_message_id is invalid")
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE invitation_id=?",
                             (invitation_id,)).fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise InvitationNotFound(invitation_id)
            if row["status"] == "provider_accepted" or row["status"] == "app_received":
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return InvitationRecord.from_row(row)
            if row["status"] != "leased" or row["lease_owner"] != worker_id:
                raise InvitationConflict("Invitation lease is no longer owned by this worker")
            if row["expires_at"] <= stamp:
                db.execute("""UPDATE call_invitation_outbox SET status='expired', lease_owner=NULL,
                            lease_until=NULL, updated_at=? WHERE invitation_id=?""", (stamp, invitation_id))
                status = "expired"
                event = "provider_accepted_after_deadline"
            else:
                db.execute("""UPDATE call_invitation_outbox SET status='provider_accepted',
                    provider_message_id=?, lease_owner=NULL, lease_until=NULL,
                    last_error_code=NULL, updated_at=? WHERE invitation_id=?""",
                    (provider_message_id, stamp, invitation_id))
                status = "provider_accepted"
                event = "provider_accepted_invitation"
            self._append_event(db, invitation_id=invitation_id, event_type=event,
                from_status=row["status"], to_status=status, actor_id=worker_id,
                details={"provider_message_id_present": provider_message_id is not None},
                created_at=stamp)
            updated = db.execute("SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE invitation_id=?",
                                 (invitation_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return InvitationRecord.from_row(updated)
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def mark_failure(self, *, invitation_id: str, worker_id: str,
                     error_code: str, retryable: bool,
                     now: datetime | None = None) -> InvitationRecord:
        if not ERROR_CODE.fullmatch(error_code):
            raise ValueError("error_code must be a short non-sensitive identifier")
        stamp_dt = now or utc_now()
        stamp = iso_utc(stamp_dt)
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE invitation_id=?",
                             (invitation_id,)).fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise InvitationNotFound(invitation_id)
            if row["status"] != "leased" or row["lease_owner"] != worker_id:
                raise InvitationConflict("Invitation lease is no longer owned by this worker")
            if row["expires_at"] <= stamp:
                target, retry_at = "expired", None
            elif not retryable:
                target, retry_at = "failed", None
            elif row["attempts"] >= self.max_attempts:
                target, retry_at = "dead_letter", None
            else:
                target = "retry_wait"
                # Capped exponential retry: 2, 4, 8... up to 5 minutes.
                delay = min(300, 2 ** min(max(row["attempts"], 1), 8))
                retry_at = iso_utc(stamp_dt + timedelta(seconds=delay))
            db.execute("""UPDATE call_invitation_outbox SET status=?, next_attempt_at=COALESCE(?, next_attempt_at),
                lease_owner=NULL, lease_until=NULL, last_error_code=?, updated_at=? WHERE invitation_id=?""",
                (target, retry_at, error_code, stamp, invitation_id))
            self._append_event(db, invitation_id=invitation_id,
                event_type="invitation_delivery_failed", from_status="leased", to_status=target,
                actor_id=worker_id, details={"error_code": error_code, "retryable": retryable},
                created_at=stamp)
            updated = db.execute("SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE invitation_id=?",
                                 (invitation_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return InvitationRecord.from_row(updated)
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def mark_app_received(self, *, invitation_id: str, user_id: str, device_id: str,
                          receipt_id: str, now: datetime | None = None) -> tuple[InvitationRecord, bool]:
        """Record an app receipt only after an API auth layer verified this device."""
        if not isinstance(receipt_id, str) or not receipt_id.strip() or len(receipt_id) > 512:
            raise ValueError("receipt_id is invalid")
        stamp = iso_utc(now or utc_now())
        db = self._connect()
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            # Authorize ownership before consulting receipt IDs; otherwise a known
            # receipt identifier could be used to probe another user's invitation.
            row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE invitation_id=?",
                             (invitation_id,)).fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise InvitationNotFound(invitation_id)
            if row["user_id"] != user_id or row["device_id"] != device_id:
                raise PermissionError("Invitation does not belong to this authenticated device")
            prior = db.execute("SELECT * FROM call_invitation_delivery_events WHERE receipt_id=?",
                               (receipt_id,)).fetchone()
            if prior:
                if prior["invitation_id"] != invitation_id:
                    raise InvitationConflict("Receipt ID reused for another invitation")
                db.execute("COMMIT")
                return InvitationRecord.from_row(row), True
            if row["status"] not in {"provider_accepted", "app_received"}:
                raise InvitationConflict("Provider acceptance must be recorded before app receipt")
            updated_status = row["status"]
            if row["status"] == "provider_accepted":
                updated_status = "app_received"
                db.execute("UPDATE call_invitation_outbox SET status='app_received', updated_at=? WHERE invitation_id=?",
                           (stamp, invitation_id))
            self._append_event(db, invitation_id=invitation_id,
                event_type="app_received_invitation", from_status=row["status"],
                to_status=updated_status, actor_id=device_id,
                details={"receipt_verified_by_api": True}, created_at=stamp,
                receipt_id=receipt_id)
            updated = db.execute("SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE invitation_id=?",
                                 (invitation_id,)).fetchone()
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return InvitationRecord.from_row(updated), False
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def cancel_call(self, *, call_id: str, reason_code: str,
                    now: datetime | None = None) -> int:
        if not ERROR_CODE.fullmatch(reason_code):
            raise ValueError("reason_code must be a short non-sensitive identifier")
        stamp = iso_utc(now or utc_now())
        db = self._connect()
        changed = 0
        try:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            rows = db.execute("""SELECT * FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE call_id=? AND status IN
                ('pending','leased','retry_wait')""", (call_id,)).fetchall()
            for row in rows:
                db.execute("""UPDATE call_invitation_outbox SET status='cancelled', lease_owner=NULL,
                    lease_until=NULL, updated_at=? WHERE invitation_id=?""", (stamp, row["invitation_id"]))
                self._append_event(db, invitation_id=row["invitation_id"],
                    event_type="invitation_cancelled", from_status=row["status"], to_status="cancelled",
                    actor_id="call-lifecycle-coordinator", details={"reason_code": reason_code},
                    created_at=stamp)
                changed += 1
            db.execute("COMMIT")
            return changed
        except Exception:
            if db.in_transaction:
                db.execute("ROLLBACK")
            raise
        finally:
            db.close()

    def events(self, invitation_id: str) -> tuple[dict[str, object], ...]:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            if db.execute("SELECT 1 FROM call_invitation_outbox WHERE invitation_id=?", (invitation_id,)).fetchone() is None:
                raise InvitationNotFound(invitation_id)
            rows = db.execute("SELECT * FROM call_invitation_delivery_events WHERE invitation_id=? ORDER BY created_at,event_id",
                              (invitation_id,)).fetchall()
        return tuple({
            "event_id": row["event_id"], "event_type": row["event_type"],
            "from_status": row["from_status"], "to_status": row["to_status"],
            "actor_id": row["actor_id"], "details": json.loads(row["details_json"]),
            "created_at": row["created_at"],
        } for row in rows)


class CallInvitationDispatcher:
    """Lease and deliver invitations through injected production transports."""

    def __init__(self, *, store: InvitationStore,
                 transports: Mapping[str, InvitationTransport],
                 call_state_reader: CallStateReader):
        invalid = set(transports) - PLATFORMS
        if invalid:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown invitation transport(s): {', '.join(sorted(invalid))}")
        self.store = store
        self.transports = dict(transports)
        self.call_state_reader = call_state_reader

    def dispatch_once(self, *, worker_id: str, limit: int = 20,
                      now: datetime | None = None) -> DispatchSummary:
        claimed = self.store.claim_due(worker_id=worker_id, now=now, limit=limit)
        accepted = retry_scheduled = terminal = skipped = 0
        for invite in claimed:
            # Refuse to ring for a call the authoritative lifecycle says is over.
            try:
                call_state = self.call_state_reader(invite.call_id)
            except Exception:
                self.store.mark_failure(invitation_id=invite.invitation_id, worker_id=worker_id,
                                        error_code="call_state_unavailable", retryable=True, now=now)
                retry_scheduled += 1
                continue
            if call_state not in ACTIVE_CALL_STATES:
                self.store.cancel_call(call_id=invite.call_id, reason_code="call_not_active", now=now)
                # cancel_call handles all unaccepted rows; this record may already be leased.
                if self.store.get(invite.invitation_id).status == "leased":
                    self.store.mark_failure(invitation_id=invite.invitation_id, worker_id=worker_id,
                                            error_code="call_not_active", retryable=False, now=now)
                skipped += 1
                continue
            transport = self.transports.get(invite.platform)
            if transport is None:
                self.store.mark_failure(invitation_id=invite.invitation_id, worker_id=worker_id,
                                        error_code="transport_not_configured", retryable=False, now=now)
                terminal += 1
                continue
            envelope = InvitationEnvelope(
                invitation_id=invite.invitation_id, call_id=invite.call_id,
                user_id=invite.user_id, device_id=invite.device_id,
                platform=invite.platform, expires_at=invite.expires_at,
            )
            try:
                receipt = transport.send(envelope, idempotency_key=invite.idempotency_key)
            except InvitationDeliveryError as exc:
                result = self.store.mark_failure(invitation_id=invite.invitation_id,
                    worker_id=worker_id, error_code=exc.code, retryable=exc.retryable, now=now)
                if result.status == "retry_wait":
                    retry_scheduled += 1
                else:
                    terminal += 1
